Here's where the Ranikuthi V5 rebuild stands right now.
Where V5 came from
V4.1 was a 46,000-line, single-workbook spec with 100+ Apps Script modules already drafted, but with real problems: two Member Credit account codes in use (14100 and 24100), a numbering collision where a whole planned module tree reused the same file numbers as actually-built code, and no schema-level way to prevent the drift that caused all of this. Rather than patch it, we agreed to rebuild schema, architecture, and code from a clean slate for V5 ? while carrying forward every correct business decision, discarding nothing that was actually right.
The five-part architecture (locked, Section 4)
Instead of one workbook, V5 splits into five independent Google Sheets + Apps Script projects:
* Part 0 ? Gateway/Identity: auth, sessions, RBAC, settings, audit ? the one thing everything else depends on
* Part 1 ? Members/Flat Directory: flats, occupancy
* Part 2 ? Billing & Financial Log (committee-only): bills, receipts, vendors, expenses
* Part 3 ? Accounting Book (committee + CA): the ledger, Chart of Accounts, reports
* Part 4 ? Service Directory & Complaints: helpdesk, notices
Cross-part rules: single writer per shared field, Part 2 never writes straight into Part 3's ledger (it queues through Pending_Transactions), every part verifies session tokens through one Gateway call rather than keeping its own, and each part's Script/Deployment ID ? the one truly unavoidable constant ? lives once in that part's own settings, never copy-pasted into code.
The business rules ? 35 locked clauses (Section 3)
Covering, among others: mobile-only login with lockout and session timeouts; RBAC as data, not code; rate and penalty calculation with admin-selectable modes (flat-specific vs per-sqft; flat/percentage/per-day penalty); the receipt waterfall with running part-payment history; Member Credit treated as a liability with a real refund procedure; maker-checker approval on high-value expenses; immutable receipts/vouchers with SHA-256-hashed, standardized-letterhead PDFs; continuous, gap-free numbering; a three-tier admin lockout recovery policy (peer reset ? logged break-glass override); paise always rounding up; and a canonicalization rule so whitespace/case differences never cause silent duplicate records.
What's fixed for good, regardless of workbook split
The corrected Chart of Accounts (23100 for Member Credit, not 14100/24100), the mutuality-principle income split for tax defensibility, Misc Bills/Receipts for donations and puja collections, GST and bank reconciliation built in now even though neither is active yet, and the CQRS-style account-summary dashboard (live ledger as truth, cached summary for speed, reconciled on a schedule).
In progress
Section 5 ? Schema/Blueprint, workbook by workbook. Part 0 (Gateway/Identity) is drafted ? 7 sheets, every field traceable to a locked clause ? awaiting your confirmation before I move to Part 1 (Members/Flat Directory).
Nothing here has touched code yet, by design ? schema and logic are meant to be airtight before a single line of Apps Script gets written.


Here is the complete Backbone & Logic Specification ? L-01 through L-40, fully locked.
L-01 ? Identity. Login identifier is Mobile Number only. Email is never a login credential.
L-02 ? Account lockout. 5 consecutive failed logins ? account locked 15 minutes.
L-03 ? Session. 30-minute inactivity timeout; 12-hour absolute timeout; a new login invalidates any prior session token for that user.
L-04 ? Role-permission mapping is data, not code. RBAC is resolved at runtime from a Roles_Permission_Matrix sheet. No role branching hardcoded in Apps Script.
L-05 ? Rate resolution (amended). Rate-per-sqft is the standing default billing mode, switchable in Global_Settings by admin. Resolution order: flat-specific fixed rate ? global flat rate ? per-sqft.
L-06 ? Billing idempotency. Exactly one authoritative bill per (Flat_ID, Bill_Month_Year). Re-running bill generation for an already-billed period is a no-op or an explicit, audited recalculation.
L-07 ? Penalty timing and mode (amended). Late fine applies in the next billing cycle. Calculation mode is admin-selectable: flat rate, percentage, or per-day charge ? days late.
L-08 ? Receipt waterfall (amended). Payment applies in order: (1) outstanding late fines, (2) oldest unpaid maintenance period, (3) current period dues, (4) surplus ? Member Credit. Part-payment receipts show the running payment history for that cycle, statement-style, carried forward into the next cycle's receipt.
L-09 ? Member Credit is a liability. Overpayment is never income. Booked to 23100 ? Member Advance/Credit Payable, reduced by FIFO application against dues or an explicit refund (L-32).
L-10 ? Concession requires justification. Any non-zero concession on a receipt requires a Committee Advice Note reference.
L-11 ? Receipts and vouchers are immutable. Never hard-deleted or edited in place. Corrections are void/reversal entries.
L-12 ? Document integrity. Every controlled PDF gets a SHA-256 hash at generation time; a VOID document keeps its hash plus a linked reversal record.
L-13 ? Continuous numbering. Bill_ID, Receipt_No, Voucher_No generated from an atomic Sequence_Counters mechanism ? gap-free within a financial year, LockService-guarded.
L-14 ? Governed transaction contract. Authenticated ? authorized ? validated ? concurrency-locked ? posted ? ledger-balanced (debit = credit) ? audited ? document-traceable.
L-15 ? Cross-workbook write boundary. Part 2 never writes directly into Part 3's Ledger_Entries. It writes to Pending_Transactions; only Part 3 posts from that queue.
L-16 ? Compensating action required. Every queue step that can fail after a prior step succeeded has a defined, automatic compensating (reversing) action.
L-17 ? Dashboard is CQRS. Member_Balance_Summary is a cached read-model, always rebuildable from Ledger_Entries; mismatches raise to Data_Quality_Exceptions.
L-18 ? Income segregation by mutuality principle. Member income (401xx) recorded separately from non-member income (402xx), regardless of GST registration status.
L-19 ? Miscellaneous transactions are first-class. Puja receipts, donations, etc. use a distinct Misc_Bills/Misc_Receipts path.
L-20 ? Schema change discipline. Any change to a locked sheet requires impact analysis and a Change Log entry first.
L-21 ? Logic Gate before freeze. Spec ? code (settings-driven) ? unit tests ? integration tests ? backtest ? doc review ? Change Log entry, before a module is marked FROZEN.
L-22 ? UI confirmation gate. "Are you sure?" confirmation required before any receipt-committing UI action fires.
L-23 ? Document letterhead standard. Society name/address in the PDF header; SHA-256 hash in the footer, 9pt italic.
L-24 ? PDF link column. Maintenance_Bills, Payment_Receipts, and Expenses_Vouchers each carry a PDF_Drive_URL column.
L-25 ? Void filename convention. Voided receipt PDFs retained with _VOID appended to the filename.
L-26 ? Minimum Admin redundancy. At least two active Admin-tier users at all times; RBAC blocks any action that would leave zero or one.
L-27 ? Peer reset. A second Admin can reset a locked-out Admin's password via Admin_Reset_Utility, OTP-verified, fully audited.
L-28 ? Break-glass recovery. If every Admin is locked out, recovery runs only via a protected function executed directly from the Apps Script editor by the Google Workspace account owner ? logged as BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY with a mandatory justification note.
L-29 ? Lockout is not recovery. The 15-minute lock (L-02) self-clears; L-27/L-28 exist only for forgotten passwords or lost OTP channels.
L-30 ? Paise rounding. Always rounds up (ceiling), never fractional, across billing, penalty, and GST.
L-31 ? Maker-checker for high-value expenses. A second, different committee member must approve any Expenses_Voucher above a Global_Settings-configurable threshold; maker ? checker; both recorded on the voucher.
L-32 ? Member Credit refund procedure. Requires a Committee Advice Note; posts as debit 23100 / credit Cash-Bank; produces a SHA-256-hashed refund voucher; sets Remaining_Paise to zero with Status "Refunded."
L-33 ? Token verification contract. Every non-Gateway part verifies session tokens via one exposed Gateway function, briefly cached ? no part keeps its own session store.
L-34 ? Constant vs configuration (amended). Each part's Script/Deployment ID is the one unavoidable, Google-assigned constant, stored once in Script Properties ? never copy-pasted as a literal across function bodies.
L-35 ? Canonicalization rule. Lookup/matching keys (Flat_ID, Account_Code, Setting_Key, Role_Name, etc.) are trimmed, whitespace-collapsed, and case-fixed before storage and comparison.
L-36 ? Admin hard-reset & email OTP provisioning. Admin may directly hard-reset any user's password; OTP delivered by email (MailApp), not SMS/WhatsApp API, since this is a non-paid platform. Admin-issued reset sets Force_Password_Reset TRUE.
L-37 ? Universal billing; discount at receipt only. All flats bill at the same universal rate. Any discount ? including for developer-owned flats ? is a receipt-level Concession_Paise with a Committee_Advice_Note (L-10), never a bill-level reduction. Developer_Discount_Paise as a separate bill field is retired.
L-38 ? Ownership transfer. Previous owner's dues must be fully paid or explicitly written off by the committee before transfer finalizes. New owner is assigned the same Flat_ID; previous owner's Occupancy_Log record is closed, not deleted. Committee write-offs post to 60300 ? Written-Off Member Dues (Committee Approved).
L-39 ? Financial Year. 1 July ? 30 June. Year-end closing into 30300 then 30200 requires formal CA-reviewed sign-off.
L-40 ? Notification channel. Default is email, targeted or global. Admin may additionally push the same notice via a non-API WhatsApp click-to-chat button ? necessarily a manual, per-recipient/group action, not an automated bulk send.
Still open: Backup_Log frequency and retention period ? not yet decided.
For a system carrying financial records with an 8-year Indian statutory retention expectation, I'd go with daily automated backups, but with a tiered retention schedule ? daily backups alone would blow through the free 15GB Drive quota eventually, and a single "keep everything forever" policy is wasteful when most daily snapshots are never actually needed.
Proposed L-41 ? Backup policy:
* Frequency: Daily, automated via a time-driven Apps Script trigger, run once at a fixed off-peak hour (e.g. 2:00 AM IST) ? after any day's billing/receipt activity is done, before the next day's starts.
* Scope: All five workbooks copied in the same run (via DriveApp.makeCopy or an export), into the existing BackUps Drive folder, filed under that day's date.
* Retention (grandfather-father-son rotation):
* Last 7 daily copies kept, older dailies auto-deleted
* Last 8 weekly (Sunday) copies kept
* Monthly copies kept for 12 months
* Financial-Year-end snapshot (30 June, tied to L-39) kept permanently ? this is the one that actually matters for statutory 7?8 year record retention, so it never rotates out regardless of the rest of the schedule
* Pre-change backup: An additional, unscheduled backup fires automatically immediately before any locked-schema change under L-20 ? so a bad schema migration is always recoverable from the moment just before it happened, not just the previous night.
* Verification, not just creation: Each backup run logs its Drive file ID and a SHA-256 hash into Backup_Log (per L-12's integrity principle) ? and once a month, an automated check re-opens the most recent backup to confirm it's actually readable, not just that the copy operation reported success.
This keeps storage bounded on the free tier indefinitely (roughly 7+8+12+1-per-year files, not one-per-day forever) while making sure the one snapshot that legally matters most ? year-end ? is never the one that gets rotated away by accident.Locked as L-41. Section 3 is now genuinely complete ? L-01 through L-41, no open items left in the backbone.
Ready to move to Part 1 of Section 5 (Schema/Blueprint) ? Members/Flat Directory whenever you say go.

I've also folded Backup_Log in here ? it's a cross-cutting system concern, and Gateway is the natural root for it since it backs up all five workbooks.
Sheet: Users User_ID, Flat_ID, Full_Name, Mobile_Number, Email_Address, Role_Name, Account_Status, Password_Hash, Password_Salt, Force_Password_Reset, Failed_Login_Count, Locked_Until, Active_Session_Token, Session_Issued_At, Last_Activity_Timestamp, Last_Login_Timestamp, Created_Timestamp, Created_By, Updated_Timestamp Notable design calls: Email_Address exists purely for OTP delivery (L-36) ? never a login field (L-01). Session_Issued_At is a new addition I've made ? L-03's 12-hour absolute timeout can't actually be computed from Last_Activity_Timestamp alone (that only tracks the 30-minute inactivity clock); you need the original issue time as a separate anchor.
Sheet: Global_Settings Setting_Key, Setting_Value, Value_Type, Editable_By_Role, Description, Updated_Timestamp, Updated_By Value_Type (STRING/NUMBER/BOOLEAN/JSON) exists because L-05 and L-07 store structured mode choices, not just plain values ? the code needs to know how to parse Setting_Value before using it.
Sheet: Roles_Permission_Matrix Role_Name, Permission_Key, Allowed, Workbook_Scope, Notes Direct implementation of L-04. Workbook_Scope matters in a five-workbook system ? a permission can be scoped to Part 2 only, for instance.
Sheet: OTP_Log (redesigned from V4.1's Password_Reset_Log ? broadened scope) OTP_ID, User_ID, Purpose, Delivery_Channel, OTP_Hash, Requested_Timestamp, Expiry_Timestamp, Verified_Timestamp, Status, Requested_By Purpose (FIRST_LOGIN / ADMIN_HARD_RESET / SELF_SERVICE_RESET) is new ? V4.1 conflated these into one undifferentiated reset log. Delivery_Channel is locked to EMAIL per L-36.
Sheet: Admin_Reset_Utility Reset_Action_ID, Actor_User_ID, Actor_Role, Target_User_ID, Reset_Timestamp, Force_Password_Reset_Set, OTP_Reference, Status Actor_Role distinguishes an L-27 peer-Admin reset from an L-36 direct hard-reset ? same mechanism, different authority level, now visible in the record itself rather than inferred.
Sheet: System_Audit_Trail Audit_ID, Timestamp, Actor_User_ID, Actor_Role, Action_Type, Target_Workbook, Target_Sheet, Record_Key, Change_Details, Correlation_ID, Justification_Note Justification_Note is new ? nullable for ordinary actions, but mandatory whenever Action_Type = BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY (L-28).
Sheet: Read_Audit_Log Read_Log_ID, Item_Type, Item_ID, User_ID, Read_Timestamp
Sheet: Backup_Log Backup_ID, Backup_Timestamp, Backup_Type, Source_Workbook, Drive_File_ID, Drive_URL, SHA256_Hash, Verification_Status, Verification_Timestamp Backup_Type (DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY/YEAR_END/PRE_SCHEMA_CHANGE) directly encodes L-41's rotation tiers, so a cleanup routine can tell which copies are safe to age out and which (YEAR_END) never rotate.
That's 8 sheets, nothing copied blindly from V4.1 ? each column earns its place against a clause. Confirm this before I move to Part 1: Members/Flat Directory?

Here is the single, locked path from here to a finished system ? no branching, no parallel tracks, one part fully finished before the next begins.
Master Build Sequence (locked)
Stage A ? Gateway (Part 0), code. Schema is done and verified. Now: write the Gateway Apps Script code, layer by layer ? canonicalization utility (L-35) ? Global_Settings accessor ? RBAC resolver against Roles_Permission_Matrix (L-04) ? auth/session (L-01?L-03, L-33) ? OTP/reset flows (L-27, L-36) ? audit logging (L-28) ? backup routine (L-41). Test each layer as it's written, not all at the end. Only once every Gateway function is tested and passes the Logic Gate (L-21) does Gateway get marked FROZEN.
Why Gateway goes first, fully, before anything else: Parts 1?4 all call into Gateway for auth (L-33). If we design Part 1's schema before Gateway's actual function contracts are settled, Part 1 gets built against a guess ? and a guess is exactly the kind of drift this rebuild exists to eliminate. Freeze the thing everyone depends on before anyone depends on it.
Stage B ? Members/Flat Directory (Part 1). Only starts once Gateway is FROZEN. Schema ? build sheet ? verify (same discipline as Part 0) ? code against Gateway's real, frozen API ? test ? freeze.
Stage C ? Billing & Financial Log (Part 2). Depends on Gateway (frozen) and Part 1 (frozen, for Flat_ID). Same schema?build?verify?code?test?freeze cycle. Includes the Pending_Transactions queue writer (L-15).
Stage D ? Accounting Book (Part 3). Depends on Gateway and consumes Part 2's queue (L-16 compensating actions live here). Same cycle. Chart of Accounts gets seeded here.
Stage E ? Service Directory & Complaints (Part 4). Depends only on Gateway and Part 1 ? genuinely independent of billing/accounting. Deliberately built last, not in parallel with C/D, purely to keep this a single track with zero ambiguity about what's "in progress" at any moment.
Stage F ? Frontend SPA. Built only once all five backends are frozen ? against real, tested endpoints, never against a guess of what an endpoint will look like.
Stage G ? Full-system backtest, final Logic Gate, go-live.
The rule that prevents drift going forward: at any point in this conversation, there is exactly one active stage. We don't sketch Part 2's schema while Part 0's code is unfinished, and we don't revisit a FROZEN part except through a formal Change Log entry (L-20). If you ever ask "what should we do next," the answer is always "continue the current stage" unless it's genuinely complete.
Right now: Stage A has begun. Next concrete unit of work is the canonicalization utility and Global_Settings accessor ? the two lowest-level Gateway functions everything else calls. Shall I write those first?Locked and saved. Let's go. Following L-34 exactly: the Sheet ID gets stored once in Script Properties, read through a single getter ? never scattered as string literals across files.
File 1 ? Bootstrap.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * Bootstrap.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Owns the ONE place the Spreadsheet ID is ever written as a
 * literal (L-34). Every other file reads it through getSS_().
 * Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() exactly once after deployment.
 */

function setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() {
  // The one permitted literal in the entire codebase (L-34).
  var SPREADSHEET_ID = '1UwZLXKz3vvK_WoXD1vJ2JrVObO75nibdNK8PheR-eV0';
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID', SPREADSHEET_ID);
  Logger.log('Spreadsheet ID stored in Script Properties.');
}

/** Returns the bound Spreadsheet. Every other file calls this ? never SpreadsheetApp.openById() directly. */
function getSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) {
    throw new Error('SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once first.');
  }
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);
}

/** Sheet name constants ? single source, so a typo anywhere else becomes a thrown error, not a silent miss. */
var SHEETS_ = {
  USERS: 'Users',
  GLOBAL_SETTINGS: 'Global_Settings',
  ROLES_PERMISSION_MATRIX: 'Roles_Permission_Matrix',
  OTP_LOG: 'OTP_Log',
  ADMIN_RESET_UTILITY: 'Admin_Reset_Utility',
  SYSTEM_AUDIT_TRAIL: 'System_Audit_Trail',
  READ_AUDIT_LOG: 'Read_Audit_Log',
  BACKUP_LOG: 'Backup_Log'
};

/** Returns a named sheet, or throws ? never returns null/undefined for silent failure downstream. */
function getSheet_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getSS_().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) {
    throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName + '. Schema drift? Check L-20.');
  }
  return sheet;
}
File 2 ? Canonicalize.gs (implements L-35)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * Canonicalize.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-35: every lookup/matching key is canonicalized before storage
 * and before comparison. Two forms:
 *   canonicalizeKey()  -> UPPER_SNAKE_CASE, for machine keys/codes
 *   canonicalizeName() -> Title Case, for human-facing display names
 * Neither ever throws on bad input ? they degrade to an empty
 * string, and callers decide whether that's a validation error.
 */

/** Collapses whitespace and trims. Shared first step for both forms below. */
function _normalizeWhitespace(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}

/** Machine key form: TRIM -> COLLAPSE WHITESPACE -> UPPERCASE -> spaces to underscores. */
function canonicalizeKey(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
}

/** Human display-name form: TRIM -> COLLAPSE WHITESPACE -> Title Case each word. */
function canonicalizeName(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.replace(/\w\S*/g, function (word) {
    return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.substr(1).toLowerCase();
  });
}

/** Strict equality check for two raw values as lookup keys ? this is what L-35 actually exists to guarantee. */
function keysMatch(a, b) {
  return canonicalizeKey(a) === canonicalizeKey(b);
}
File 3 ? ConfigService.gs (Global_Settings accessor)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * ConfigService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * The ONLY code path allowed to read or write Global_Settings.
 * Implements Value_Type-aware parsing (Global_Settings schema)
 * and the Editable_By_Role write check.
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs
 */

var CONFIG_HEADERS_ = ['Setting_Key', 'Setting_Value', 'Value_Type', 'Editable_By_Role', 'Description', 'Updated_Timestamp', 'Updated_By'];

/** Reads the Global_Settings sheet into {key: {value, type, editableByRole, row}} once per call. No caching yet ? correctness first. */
function _loadSettings_() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.GLOBAL_SETTINGS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var map = {};
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    var row = data[r];
    if (!row[0]) continue; // skip blank rows
    var key = canonicalizeKey(row[0]);
    map[key] = {
      rawValue: row[1],
      valueType: String(row[2] || 'STRING').toUpperCase(),
      editableByRole: row[3],
      rowIndex: r + 1 // 1-based sheet row
    };
  }
  return map;
}

/** Parses Setting_Value according to its Value_Type column. Throws on a type it doesn't recognise ? silent misparse is worse than a loud error. */
function _parseByType_(rawValue, valueType) {
  switch (valueType) {
    case 'STRING':  return String(rawValue);
    case 'NUMBER':  return Number(rawValue);
    case 'BOOLEAN': return String(rawValue).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
    case 'JSON':     return JSON.parse(rawValue);
    default:
      throw new Error('Unknown Value_Type "' + valueType + '" in Global_Settings. Fix the sheet, not the code.');
  }
}

/** Public read. Returns the typed value, or null if the key doesn't exist (never throws for a missing optional setting). */
function getSetting(settingKey) {
  var settings = _loadSettings_();
  var key = canonicalizeKey(settingKey);
  if (!settings[key]) return null;
  return _parseByType_(settings[key].rawValue, settings[key].valueType);
}

/**
 * Public write. Enforces the Editable_By_Role column (L-04's spirit:
 * even config edits are role-gated, not just "if you can run the script").
 * actorRole must be passed in by the caller ? ConfigService does not
 * resolve who's logged in; that's AuthService's job (next file).
 */
function setSetting(settingKey, newValue, actorRole, actorUserId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.GLOBAL_SETTINGS);
  var settings = _loadSettings_();
  var key = canonicalizeKey(settingKey);
  var existing = settings[key];

  if (!existing) {
    throw new Error('Setting "' + settingKey + '" does not exist. Settings are not created ad hoc from write calls ? add the row deliberately (L-20).');
  }

  var requiredRole = canonicalizeKey(existing.editableByRole);
  var actual = canonicalizeKey(actorRole);
  if (requiredRole && requiredRole !== actual && requiredRole !== 'ANY') {
    throw new Error('Role "' + actorRole + '" is not permitted to edit "' + settingKey + '" (requires ' + existing.editableByRole + ').');
  }

  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);
  try {
    sheet.getRange(existing.rowIndex, 2).setValue(newValue);          // Setting_Value
    sheet.getRange(existing.rowIndex, 6).setValue(new Date());        // Updated_Timestamp
    sheet.getRange(existing.rowIndex, 7).setValue(actorUserId || ''); // Updated_By
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

File 5 ? SeedSettings.gs (one-time, deliberate ? not ad hoc, per L-20)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * SeedSettings.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Writes the initial Global_Settings rows this system's locked
 * clauses actually require values for. Run ONCE. Uses direct
 * sheet writes (not setSetting) because setSetting refuses to
 * create new rows on purpose ? seeding is the one deliberate
 * exception, done here and only here.
 */
function setup_SeedGlobalSettings() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.GLOBAL_SETTINGS);
  var existing = _loadSettings_(); // from ConfigService.gs ? avoid duplicate seeding
  var now = new Date();

  var seedRows = [
    // Setting_Key, Setting_Value, Value_Type, Editable_By_Role, Description
    ['RATE_MODE_DEFAULT', 'PER_SQFT', 'STRING', 'ADMIN', 'L-05: default billing rate mode (FLAT_SPECIFIC / GLOBAL_FLAT / PER_SQFT)'],
    ['PENALTY_MODE', 'PERCENTAGE', 'STRING', 'ADMIN', 'L-07: FLAT / PERCENTAGE / PER_DAY'],
    ['PENALTY_FLAT_AMOUNT_PAISE', '0', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-07: used only when PENALTY_MODE = FLAT'],
    ['PENALTY_PERCENTAGE', '2', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-07: used only when PENALTY_MODE = PERCENTAGE'],
    ['PENALTY_PER_DAY_PAISE', '0', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-07: used only when PENALTY_MODE = PER_DAY'],
    ['MAKER_CHECKER_THRESHOLD_PAISE', '500000', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-31: expense vouchers above this need a second approver (?5,000 default)'],
    ['SESSION_INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MIN', '30', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-03'],
    ['SESSION_ABSOLUTE_TIMEOUT_HRS', '12', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-03'],
    ['LOGIN_FAILURE_LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD', '5', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-02'],
    ['LOGIN_LOCKOUT_MINUTES', '15', 'NUMBER', 'ADMIN', 'L-02'],

  ];

  var written = 0, skipped = 0;
  seedRows.forEach(function (r) {
    var key = canonicalizeKey(r[0]);
    if (existing[key]) { skipped++; return; } // never overwrite ? re-running this is safe
    sheet.appendRow([r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3], r[4], now, 'SEED_SCRIPT']);
    written++;
  });

  Logger.log('Seed complete. Written: ' + written + ', Skipped (already present): ' + skipped);
}
File 6 ? RbacService.gs (implements L-04)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * RbacService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-04: role-permission mapping is DATA, not code. This file
 * contains zero role-name branching ? it only ever reads
 * Roles_Permission_Matrix and canonicalizes both sides of the
 * comparison (L-35) before checking a match.
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs
 */

/** Loads the full matrix once per call: [{role, permission, allowed, scope, notes}] */
function _loadPermissionMatrix_() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.ROLES_PERMISSION_MATRIX);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rows = [];
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    var row = data[r];
    if (!row[0]) continue;
    rows.push({
      role: canonicalizeKey(row[0]),
      permission: canonicalizeKey(row[1]),
      allowed: String(row[2]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE',
      scope: canonicalizeKey(row[3]),
      notes: row[4]
    });
  }
  return rows;
}

/**
 * Public check. workbookScope is optional ? pass it when a permission
 * is scoped to a specific part (e.g. 'PART2'); omit for Gateway-only checks.
 * Returns false for "no matching row" ? silently, on purpose: RBAC
 * should never leak *why* access was denied to the caller.
 */
function hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, workbookScope) {
  var role = canonicalizeKey(actorRole);
  var permission = canonicalizeKey(permissionKey);
  var scope = workbookScope ? canonicalizeKey(workbookScope) : null;

  var matrix = _loadPermissionMatrix_();
  for (var i = 0; i < matrix.length; i++) {
    var row = matrix[i];
    if (row.role !== role || row.permission !== permission) continue;
    if (scope && row.scope !== 'ALL' && row.scope !== scope) continue;
    return row.allowed;
  }
  return false; // no matching row = no access, always
}

/** Throws instead of returning false ? use this at the top of any state-changing function, not hasPermission() directly. */
function requirePermission_(actorRole, permissionKey, workbookScope) {
  if (!hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, workbookScope)) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: role "' + actorRole + '" lacks permission "' + permissionKey + '"' + (workbookScope ? ' in scope ' + workbookScope : '') + '.');
  }
}

File 8 ? PasswordService.gs (needed before AuthService can do anything real)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * PasswordService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Apps Script has no bcrypt/scrypt. SHA-256 + a per-user random
 * salt is the practical option on this platform ? documented here
 * as a known constraint, not a silent shortcut.
 */

function generateSalt_() {
  return Utilities.getUuid() + Utilities.getUuid(); // 72 chars of randomness
}

function hashPassword_(plainPassword, salt) {
  var digest = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, plainPassword + salt);
  return digest.map(function (b) { return (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join('');
}

function verifyPassword_(plainPassword, salt, expectedHash) {
  return hashPassword_(plainPassword, salt) === expectedHash;
}
File 9 ? SeedRoles.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * SeedRoles.gs ? one-time seed for Roles_Permission_Matrix.
 * Safe to re-run: skips any (role, permission, scope) already present.
 */
function setup_SeedRolesPermissionMatrix() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.ROLES_PERMISSION_MATRIX);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var existingKeys = {};
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (!data[r][0]) continue;
    existingKeys[canonicalizeKey(data[r][0]) + '|' + canonicalizeKey(data[r][1]) + '|' + canonicalizeKey(data[r][3])] = true;
  }

  var rows = [
    ['ADMIN', 'MANAGE_USERS', 'TRUE', 'ALL', 'Create/deactivate users, assign roles'],
    ['ADMIN', 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET_PASSWORD', 'TRUE', 'ALL', 'L-36'],
    ['ADMIN', 'EDIT_SETTINGS', 'TRUE', 'ALL', 'L-05/L-07/etc.'],
    ['ADMIN', 'SEND_NOTIFICATION', 'TRUE', 'ALL', 'L-40'],
    ['ADMIN', 'VIEW_AUDIT_TRAIL', 'TRUE', 'ALL', ''],
    ['COMMITTEE', 'POST_EXPENSE_VOUCHER', 'TRUE', 'PART2', 'L-31 maker'],
    ['COMMITTEE', 'APPROVE_EXPENSE_VOUCHER', 'TRUE', 'PART2', 'L-31 checker'],
    ['COMMITTEE', 'APPROVE_WRITE_OFF', 'TRUE', 'PART3', 'L-38'],
    ['COMMITTEE', 'APPROVE_CREDIT_REFUND', 'TRUE', 'PART3', 'L-32'],
    ['COMMITTEE', 'VIEW_LEDGER', 'TRUE', 'PART3', ''],
    ['CA', 'VIEW_LEDGER', 'TRUE', 'PART3', ''],
    ['CA', 'APPROVE_YEAR_END_CLOSE', 'TRUE', 'PART3', 'L-39'],
    ['RESIDENT', 'VIEW_OWN_BALANCE', 'TRUE', 'PART3', 'L-17'],
    ['RESIDENT', 'RAISE_COMPLAINT', 'TRUE', 'PART4', ''],
    ['RESIDENT', 'VIEW_OWN_BILLS', 'TRUE', 'PART2', '']
  ];

  var written = 0, skipped = 0;
  rows.forEach(function (r) {
    var k = canonicalizeKey(r[0]) + '|' + canonicalizeKey(r[1]) + '|' + canonicalizeKey(r[3]);
    if (existingKeys[k]) { skipped++; return; }
    sheet.appendRow(r);
    written++;
  });
  Logger.log('RBAC seed complete. Written: ' + written + ', Skipped: ' + skipped);
}
File 10 ? AuthService.gs (implements L-01, L-02, L-03, L-33)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * AuthService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, ConfigService.gs, PasswordService.gs
 */

var USERS_COLS_ = {
  USER_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, FULL_NAME: 3, MOBILE: 4, EMAIL: 5, ROLE: 6, STATUS: 7,
  PASS_HASH: 8, PASS_SALT: 9, FORCE_RESET: 10, FAIL_COUNT: 11, LOCKED_UNTIL: 12,
  SESSION_TOKEN: 13, SESSION_ISSUED: 14, LAST_ACTIVITY: 15, LAST_LOGIN: 16,
  CREATED_TS: 17, CREATED_BY: 18, UPDATED_TS: 19
};

/** Mobile numbers compare on digits only ? a space or a leading 0 must never create a false non-match. */
function canonicalizeMobile(value) {
  return _normalizeWhitespace(value).replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
}

function _findUserRowByMobile_(mobileNumber) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeMobile(mobileNumber);
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeMobile(data[r][USERS_COLS_.MOBILE - 1]) === target) {
      return { rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

function login(mobileNumber, plainPassword) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(10000);
  try {
    var found = _findUserRowByMobile_(mobileNumber);
    if (!found) throw new Error('Invalid mobile number or password.'); // never reveal which

    var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
    var v = found.values;
    var now = new Date();

    if (String(v[USERS_COLS_.STATUS - 1]).toUpperCase() !== 'ACTIVE') {
      throw new Error('Account is not active. Contact an Admin.');
    }

    var lockedUntil = v[USERS_COLS_.LOCKED_UNTIL - 1];
    if (lockedUntil && new Date(lockedUntil) > now) {
      throw new Error('Account locked until ' + new Date(lockedUntil).toLocaleTimeString() + '.'); // L-02
    }

    var passOk = verifyPassword_(plainPassword, v[USERS_COLS_.PASS_SALT - 1], v[USERS_COLS_.PASS_HASH - 1]);

    if (!passOk) {
      var failCount = (Number(v[USERS_COLS_.FAIL_COUNT - 1]) || 0) + 1;
      var threshold = getSetting('LOGIN_FAILURE_LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD') || 5;
      sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.FAIL_COUNT).setValue(failCount);
      if (failCount >= threshold) {
        var lockoutMin = getSetting('LOGIN_LOCKOUT_MINUTES') || 15;
        var until = new Date(now.getTime() + lockoutMin * 60000);
        sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.LOCKED_UNTIL).setValue(until); // L-02
      }
      throw new Error('Invalid mobile number or password.');
    }

    // Success: reset lockout state, issue a new session (invalidates any prior one ? L-03)
    var token = Utilities.getUuid();
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.FAIL_COUNT).setValue(0);
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.LOCKED_UNTIL).setValue('');
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.SESSION_TOKEN).setValue(token);
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.SESSION_ISSUED).setValue(now);
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.LAST_ACTIVITY).setValue(now);
    sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, USERS_COLS_.LAST_LOGIN).setValue(now);

    return {
      token: token,
      userId: v[USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1],
      role: v[USERS_COLS_.ROLE - 1],
      flatId: v[USERS_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
      forcePasswordReset: String(v[USERS_COLS_.FORCE_RESET - 1]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE'
    };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/** L-33: the ONE function every other Part calls to verify a token. Enforces both L-03 timeouts. */
function verifySessionToken(token) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][USERS_COLS_.SESSION_TOKEN - 1] === token && token) {
      var issued = new Date(data[r][USERS_COLS_.SESSION_ISSUED - 1]);
      var lastActivity = new Date(data[r][USERS_COLS_.LAST_ACTIVITY - 1]);
      var inactivityMin = getSetting('SESSION_INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MIN') || 30;
      var absoluteHrs = getSetting('SESSION_ABSOLUTE_TIMEOUT_HRS') || 12;

      var inactiveMinutes = (now - lastActivity) / 60000;
      var ageHours = (now - issued) / 3600000;

      if (inactiveMinutes > inactivityMin || ageHours > absoluteHrs) {
        sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.SESSION_TOKEN).setValue(''); // expire it
        return null;
      }
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.LAST_ACTIVITY).setValue(now); // sliding window
      return { userId: data[r][USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1], role: data[r][USERS_COLS_.ROLE - 1], flatId: data[r][USERS_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1] };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

File 12 ? AdminBootstrap.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * AdminBootstrap.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Run ONCE, directly from the Apps Script editor ? never exposed
 * as a web app endpoint. This is the only place a user is ever
 * created without going through RBAC, because there is no Admin
 * yet to authorize it (same reasoning as L-28 break-glass).
 * Enforces L-26 from the very first row: creates exactly two
 * Admins together, never one.
 */
function setup_CreateInitialAdmins(admin1, admin2) {
  // admin1 / admin2 shape: {fullName, mobileNumber, emailAddress, flatId}
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();

  var existingAdminCount = 0;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][USERS_COLS_.ROLE - 1]) === 'ADMIN') existingAdminCount++;
  }
  if (existingAdminCount > 0) {
    throw new Error('Admins already exist (' + existingAdminCount + '). This bootstrap only runs on a genuinely empty system. Use the normal Admin-creation flow instead.');
  }
  if (!admin1 || !admin2) {
    throw new Error('L-26 requires creating two Admins together, not one. Pass both admin1 and admin2.');
  }

  var now = new Date();
  [admin1, admin2].forEach(function (a) {
    var salt = generateSalt_();
    var tempPassword = Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8); // shown once, below, in the execution log only
    var hash = hashPassword_(tempPassword, salt);
    var userId = 'U-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();

    sheet.appendRow([
      userId, a.flatId || '', canonicalizeName(a.fullName), canonicalizeMobile(a.mobileNumber),
      a.emailAddress || '', 'ADMIN', 'ACTIVE', hash, salt,
      'TRUE',           // Force_Password_Reset ? must change on first login
      0, '',            // Failed_Login_Count, Locked_Until
      '', '',           // Active_Session_Token, Session_Issued_At
      '', '',           // Last_Activity_Timestamp, Last_Login_Timestamp
      now, 'BOOTSTRAP', now
    ]);

    Logger.log('Created Admin ' + userId + ' (' + a.mobileNumber + '). ONE-TIME temp password: ' + tempPassword + ' ? relay this securely, it will not be shown again.');
  });

  Logger.log('L-26 satisfied: 2 Admins created. Bootstrap complete ? this function will now refuse to run again.');
}

File 14 ? PasswordChangeService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * PasswordChangeService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, PasswordService.gs, AuthService.gs (USERS_COLS_)
 */

/**
 * Called by a logged-in user (valid session token) to set a new password.
 * This is the ONLY path that clears Force_Password_Reset ? it never
 * clears silently as a side effect of anything else.
 */
function changePassword(sessionToken, oldPlainPassword, newPlainPassword) {
  var session = verifySessionToken(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');

  if (!newPlainPassword || newPlainPassword.length < 8) {
    throw new Error('New password must be at least 8 characters.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1] === session.userId) {
      var currentHash = data[r][USERS_COLS_.PASS_HASH - 1];
      var currentSalt = data[r][USERS_COLS_.PASS_SALT - 1];
      var forceReset = String(data[r][USERS_COLS_.FORCE_RESET - 1]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';

      // Skip old-password verification only when a reset was admin/OTP-forced; otherwise always verify.
      if (!forceReset && !verifyPassword_(oldPlainPassword, currentSalt, currentHash)) {
        throw new Error('Current password is incorrect.');
      }

      var newSalt = generateSalt_();
      var newHash = hashPassword_(newPlainPassword, newSalt);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_HASH).setValue(newHash);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_SALT).setValue(newSalt);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.FORCE_RESET).setValue('FALSE');
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.UPDATED_TS).setValue(new Date());
      return { success: true };
    }
  }
  throw new Error('User not found for active session ? this should not happen.');
}
File 15 ? OtpService.gs (implements the email-OTP half of L-27, L-36)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * OtpService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs
 */

var OTP_COLS_ = { OTP_ID: 1, USER_ID: 2, PURPOSE: 3, CHANNEL: 4, HASH: 5, REQUESTED: 6, EXPIRY: 7, VERIFIED: 8, STATUS: 9, REQUESTED_BY: 10 };

function _generateOtpCode_() {
  return String(Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)); // 6-digit
}

/**
 * purpose: 'FIRST_LOGIN' | 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET' | 'SELF_SERVICE_RESET' (L-36)
 * Always EMAIL channel ? L-36 locks this to email on a non-paid platform.
 */
function requestOtp(targetUserId, purpose, requestedByUserId) {
  var usersSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var usersData = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var targetEmail = null;
  for (var r = 1; r < usersData.length; r++) {
    if (usersData[r][USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1] === targetUserId) {
      targetEmail = usersData[r][USERS_COLS_.EMAIL - 1];
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!targetEmail) throw new Error('No email on file for user ' + targetUserId + ' ? cannot send OTP.');

  var code = _generateOtpCode_();
  var salt = generateSalt_();
  var hash = hashPassword_(code, salt); // reusing PasswordService's hash fn ? OTP is just a short-lived password
  var now = new Date();
  var expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + 10 * 60000); // 10-minute validity
  var otpId = 'OTP-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();

  var otpSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OTP_LOG);
  otpSheet.appendRow([otpId, targetUserId, canonicalizeKey(purpose), 'EMAIL', hash + '::' + salt, now, expiry, '', 'PENDING', requestedByUserId || targetUserId]);

  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: targetEmail,
    subject: 'Ranikuthi FOA ? Your one-time verification code',
    body: 'Your verification code is: ' + code + '\n\nThis code expires in 10 minutes. If you did not request this, contact an Admin immediately.'
  });

  return { otpId: otpId, expiresAt: expiry };
}

/** Verifies a submitted code against the stored hash and expiry. Marks the OTP row VERIFIED or EXPIRED/FAILED ? never reusable either way. */
function verifyOtp(otpId, submittedCode) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OTP_LOG);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var now = new Date();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][OTP_COLS_.OTP_ID - 1] === otpId) {
      var status = data[r][OTP_COLS_.STATUS - 1];
      if (status !== 'PENDING') throw new Error('This OTP has already been used or has expired.');

      var expiry = new Date(data[r][OTP_COLS_.EXPIRY - 1]);
      if (now > expiry) {
        sheet.getRange(r + 1, OTP_COLS_.STATUS).setValue('EXPIRED');
        throw new Error('OTP expired. Please request a new one.');
      }

      var parts = String(data[r][OTP_COLS_.HASH - 1]).split('::');
      var storedHash = parts[0], storedSalt = parts[1];
      var codeOk = hashPassword_(submittedCode, storedSalt) === storedHash;

      sheet.getRange(r + 1, OTP_COLS_.STATUS).setValue(codeOk ? 'VERIFIED' : 'FAILED');
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, OTP_COLS_.VERIFIED).setValue(now);
      if (!codeOk) throw new Error('Incorrect code.');

      return { userId: data[r][OTP_COLS_.USER_ID - 1], purpose: data[r][OTP_COLS_.PURPOSE - 1] };
    }
  }
  throw new Error('OTP request not found.');
}

File 17 ? AdminResetService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * AdminResetService.gs ? implements L-27 (peer reset) and the
 * Admin-initiated half of L-36 (hard reset).
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, AuthService.gs, RbacService.gs,
 *             OtpService.gs, PasswordService.gs
 *
 * Two-step flow, deliberately: an Admin cannot silently hand
 * someone a new password. The OTP must land in the TARGET user's
 * own inbox and be verified before any password is touched ?
 * proving the target still controls that channel, not just that
 * an Admin decided to act.
 */

var RESET_COLS_ = { RESET_ID: 1, ACTOR: 2, ACTOR_ROLE: 3, TARGET: 4, TIMESTAMP: 5, FORCE_RESET_SET: 6, OTP_REF: 7, STATUS: 8 };

/** Step 1: Admin initiates. Sends the OTP to the TARGET's email, not the actor's. */
function adminInitiateReset(actorSessionToken, targetUserId) {
  var actor = verifySessionToken(actorSessionToken);
  if (!actor) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(actor.role, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET_PASSWORD'); // L-04 ? RBAC gates this, not a role-name if-check

  var otp = requestOtp(targetUserId, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET', actor.userId);

  var resetSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.ADMIN_RESET_UTILITY);
  var resetId = 'RST-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  resetSheet.appendRow([resetId, actor.userId, actor.role, targetUserId, new Date(), '', otp.otpId, 'OTP_SENT']);

  return { resetId: resetId, otpId: otp.otpId, message: 'OTP sent to target user\'s registered email.' };
}

/** Step 2: after the target (or actor, relaying) supplies the OTP code, this issues a new temp password. */
function adminCompleteReset(actorSessionToken, resetId, submittedCode) {
  var actor = verifySessionToken(actorSessionToken);
  if (!actor) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(actor.role, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET_PASSWORD');

  var resetSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.ADMIN_RESET_UTILITY);
  var data = resetSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rowIndex = -1, resetRow = null;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][RESET_COLS_.RESET_ID - 1] === resetId) { rowIndex = r + 1; resetRow = data[r]; break; }
  }
  if (!resetRow) throw new Error('Reset request not found.');
  if (resetRow[RESET_COLS_.STATUS - 1] !== 'OTP_SENT') throw new Error('This reset request is not awaiting verification (status: ' + resetRow[RESET_COLS_.STATUS - 1] + ').');

  var targetUserId = resetRow[RESET_COLS_.TARGET - 1];
  var otpId = resetRow[RESET_COLS_.OTP_REF - 1];
  var verifyResult = verifyOtp(otpId, submittedCode); // throws on wrong/expired/reused code
  if (verifyResult.userId !== targetUserId) throw new Error('OTP/target mismatch ? this should not happen.');

  // Generate and set new temp password
  var usersSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var usersData = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var tempPassword = null;
  for (var u = 1; u < usersData.length; u++) {
    if (usersData[u][USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1] === targetUserId) {
      var newSalt = generateSalt_();
      tempPassword = Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
      var newHash = hashPassword_(tempPassword, newSalt);
      usersSheet.getRange(u + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_HASH).setValue(newHash);
      usersSheet.getRange(u + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_SALT).setValue(newSalt);
      usersSheet.getRange(u + 1, USERS_COLS_.FORCE_RESET).setValue('TRUE');
      usersSheet.getRange(u + 1, USERS_COLS_.UPDATED_TS).setValue(new Date());
      // Any reset invalidates an existing session too ? old token must not survive a password reset.
      usersSheet.getRange(u + 1, USERS_COLS_.SESSION_TOKEN).setValue('');
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!tempPassword) throw new Error('Target user not found ? this should not happen.');

  resetSheet.getRange(rowIndex, RESET_COLS_.FORCE_RESET_SET).setValue('TRUE');
  resetSheet.getRange(rowIndex, RESET_COLS_.STATUS).setValue('COMPLETED');

  Logger.log('Reset completed for ' + targetUserId + '. ONE-TIME temp password: ' + tempPassword + ' ? relay securely.');
  return { targetUserId: targetUserId, tempPassword: tempPassword };
}

File 19 ? BreakGlassRecovery.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * BreakGlassRecovery.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-28: run ONLY directly from the Apps Script editor by the
 * underlying Google Workspace account owner ? deliberately NOT
 * exposed via any web app endpoint, and not gated by RBAC (there's
 * no logged-in Admin to check permissions against ? that's the
 * whole point of this function existing).
 * Mandatory justification note, logged distinctly, per L-28.
 */
function breakGlassResetAdminPassword(targetUserId, justificationNote) {
  if (!justificationNote || justificationNote.trim().length < 10) {
    throw new Error('L-28 requires a real, typed justification note (min 10 characters) ? this is a deliberate friction point, not a formality to skip.');
  }

  var usersSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var found = false;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][USERS_COLS_.USER_ID - 1] === targetUserId) {
      if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][USERS_COLS_.ROLE - 1]) !== 'ADMIN') {
        throw new Error('Break-glass recovery is for Admin lockout only (L-28). This user is not an Admin.');
      }
      var newSalt = generateSalt_();
      var tempPassword = Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
      var newHash = hashPassword_(tempPassword, newSalt);

      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_HASH).setValue(newHash);
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.PASS_SALT).setValue(newSalt);
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.FORCE_RESET).setValue('TRUE');
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.LOCKED_UNTIL).setValue('');
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.FAIL_COUNT).setValue(0);
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.SESSION_TOKEN).setValue('');
      usersSheet.getRange(r + 1, USERS_COLS_.UPDATED_TS).setValue(new Date());

      // Distinct, mandatory audit entry ? this is the part L-28 actually cares about most.
      var auditSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.SYSTEM_AUDIT_TRAIL);
      auditSheet.appendRow([
        'AUD-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
        new Date(), 'SYSTEM_OWNER', 'OWNER', 'BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY',
        'PART0', SHEETS_.USERS, targetUserId,
        'Password force-reset via break-glass recovery.', '', justificationNote
      ]);

      Logger.log('BREAK-GLASS: Admin ' + targetUserId + ' password reset. ONE-TIME temp password: ' + tempPassword);
      Logger.log('This action has been logged to System_Audit_Trail as BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY with your justification note.');
      found = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!found) throw new Error('User ' + targetUserId + ' not found.');
}
That completes L-28. File 20 ? AuditService.gs ? a single shared logger, retrofitted into every service that should have been writing to System_Audit_Trail all along:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * AuditService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * ONE function every other service calls to log an action.
 * Never write directly to System_Audit_Trail from anywhere else ?
 * that's exactly the kind of duplicated logic L-04's spirit warns against.
 */
function logAudit_(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetWorkbook, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, justificationNote) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.SYSTEM_AUDIT_TRAIL);
  sheet.appendRow([
    'AUD-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase(),
    new Date(), actorUserId || '', actorRole || '', actionType,
    targetWorkbook || 'PART0', targetSheet || '', recordKey || '',
    changeDetails || '', correlationId || '', justificationNote || ''
  ]);
}
Now retrofitting the three calls that were missing it ? add this one line to each existing function, right before its return:

File 21 ? BackupService.gs (implements L-41):
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * BackupService.gs ? L-41
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Run backupNow(type) on a schedule via a time-driven trigger
 * (set up separately in the Apps Script editor: Triggers > Add
 * Trigger > runDailyBackup > Time-driven > Day timer > 2am-3am).
 * type: 'DAILY' | 'WEEKLY' | 'MONTHLY' | 'YEAR_END' | 'PRE_SCHEMA_CHANGE'
 */
function backupNow(type) {
  var ss = getSS_();
  var folderName = 'BackUps';
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(folderName);
  var folder = folders.hasNext() ? folders.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(folderName);

  var dateStr = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), 'Asia/Kolkata', 'yyyy-MM-dd_HHmm');
  var copyName = 'RANIKUTHI_V5_P0_GATEWAY_IDENTITY_BACKUP_' + type + '_' + dateStr;

  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(ss.getId());
  var copy = file.makeCopy(copyName, folder);

  var blob = copy.getBlob();
  var digest = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, blob.getBytes());
  var hash = digest.map(function (b) { return (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join('');

  var backupId = 'BAK-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var logSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.BACKUP_LOG);
  logSheet.appendRow([backupId, new Date(), type, 'PART0', copy.getId(), copy.getUrl(), hash, 'PENDING_VERIFICATION', '']);

  Logger.log('Backup created: ' + copyName + ' (id=' + copy.getId() + ')');
  return { backupId: backupId, fileId: copy.getId(), url: copy.getUrl() };
}

/** Fires nightly via trigger. Rotation logic (deleting old dailies/weeklies) intentionally deferred ? see note below. */
function runDailyBackup() {
  backupNow('DAILY');
}

/** Monthly check: re-open the most recent backup to confirm it's actually readable, not just that the copy succeeded. */
function verifyLatestBackup() {
  var logSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.BACKUP_LOG);
  var data = logSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length < 2) throw new Error('No backups logged yet.');

  var lastRow = data.length - 1; // most recently appended
  var fileId = data[lastRow][4]; // Drive_File_ID column
  try {
    var testOpen = SpreadsheetApp.openById(fileId);
    var sheetCount = testOpen.getSheets().length;
    logSheet.getRange(lastRow + 1, 8).setValue('VERIFIED');
    logSheet.getRange(lastRow + 1, 9).setValue(new Date());
    Logger.log('Backup verified OK ? ' + sheetCount + ' sheets readable.');
  } catch (e) {
    logSheet.getRange(lastRow + 1, 8).setValue('VERIFICATION_FAILED');
    throw new Error('Backup verification FAILED for ' + fileId + ': ' + e.message);
  }
}
Honest gap I'm flagging rather than quietly writing: the grandfather-father-son rotation (deleting old dailies past 7, weeklies past 8, etc.) isn't in backupNow() above ? it's a genuinely separate piece of logic (needs to distinguish YEAR_END copies as permanent, walk the Backup_Log by type and age, delete from Drive, remove the log row) that deserves its own function and its own test rather than being buried inside the backup-creation call. Want that as the next file, or should we test what's here first?

That leaves exactly one thing before Part 0 hits FROZEN: the backup rotation logic (L-41) ? deleting old dailies/weeklies/monthlies past their retention count, while YEAR_END backups are never touched.
File 23 ? BackupRotationService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * BackupRotationService.gs ? L-41 retention
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Deletes Drive files + Backup_Log rows for backups past their
 * retention count, grouped by Backup_Type. YEAR_END and
 * PRE_SCHEMA_CHANGE are NEVER auto-deleted ? permanent by design.
 * Run this AFTER backupNow(), not before ? never rotate before
 * confirming today's backup actually landed.
 */

var RETENTION_COUNTS_ = {
  DAILY: 7,
  WEEKLY: 8,
  MONTHLY: 12
  // YEAR_END and PRE_SCHEMA_CHANGE deliberately absent ? no limit, never rotated.
};

function rotateBackups() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.BACKUP_LOG);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var deletedCount = 0;

  Object.keys(RETENTION_COUNTS_).forEach(function (type) {
    var keep = RETENTION_COUNTS_[type];

    // Collect all rows of this type, oldest first (Backup_Timestamp column index 1).
    var rowsOfType = [];
    for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
      if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][2]) === type) { // Backup_Type column
        rowsOfType.push({ sheetRow: r + 1, timestamp: new Date(data[r][1]), fileId: data[r][4] });
      }
    }
    rowsOfType.sort(function (a, b) { return a.timestamp - b.timestamp; });

    var excess = rowsOfType.length - keep;
    if (excess <= 0) return; // nothing to rotate for this type

    var toDelete = rowsOfType.slice(0, excess); // the oldest `excess` entries

    // Delete from Drive first, then remove log rows bottom-to-top so indices don't shift mid-loop.
    toDelete.forEach(function (entry) {
      try {
        DriveApp.getFileById(entry.fileId).setTrashed(true);
      } catch (e) {
        Logger.log('Could not trash file ' + entry.fileId + ' (may already be gone): ' + e.message);
      }
    });

    toDelete
      .map(function (e) { return e.sheetRow; })
      .sort(function (a, b) { return b - a; }) // descending, so row deletion doesn't shift earlier indices
      .forEach(function (rowNum) {
        sheet.deleteRow(rowNum);
        deletedCount++;
      });

    Logger.log('Rotated ' + type + ': removed ' + excess + ' old backup(s), kept ' + keep + ' most recent.');
  });

  Logger.log('Rotation complete. Total rows removed: ' + deletedCount);
}
File 24 ? Test_Stage_A_10_Rotation.gs ? since you only have one real backup so far (if any), this test creates a few synthetic DAILY log rows (without real Drive files, to avoid cluttering your Drive) purely to prove the rotation logic works, then cleans up after itself:
function test_Stage_A_10_rotation() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.BACKUP_LOG);
  var now = new Date();

  // Insert 9 fake DAILY rows (retention keeps only 7) with fake file IDs that don't exist in Drive ?
  // rotateBackups() should log a "could not trash" warning for these and still remove the log rows.
  for (var i = 9; i >= 1; i--) {
    var ts = new Date(now.getTime() - i * 86400000); // i days ago
    sheet.appendRow(['BAK-TEST-' + i, ts, 'DAILY', 'PART0', 'FAKE-FILE-ID-' + i, '', '', 'PENDING_VERIFICATION', '']);
  }
  Logger.log('Inserted 9 synthetic DAILY rows.');

  rotateBackups();

  // Count remaining DAILY rows ? expect exactly 7.
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var dailyCount = 0;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][2] === 'DAILY') dailyCount++;
  }
  Logger.log('DAILY rows remaining after rotation: ' + dailyCount + ' (expect 7 or fewer, including any real backups you already had).');
}

File 1 ? Bootstrap.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * Bootstrap.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * The ONE place this part's Spreadsheet ID is ever written as a
 * literal (L-34). Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once.
 */

function setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() {
  var SPREADSHEET_ID = '13Wm16-suELxyKPuferN7JcqP4OwYLFgErkwUys1K1-M';
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID', SPREADSHEET_ID);
  Logger.log('Spreadsheet ID stored in Script Properties.');
}

function getSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) throw new Error('SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once first.');
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);
}

var SHEETS_ = {
  FLATS: 'Flats',
  OCCUPANCY: 'Occupancy',
  OWNERSHIP_TRANSFER_LOG: 'Ownership_Transfer_Log'
};

function getSheet_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getSS_().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName + '. Schema drift? Check L-20.');
  return sheet;
}
File 2 ? GatewayLink.gs ? this is new territory: Part 1 is the first part to actually exercise L-33 (calling Gateway from outside Gateway). We need Part 1's own copy of the canonicalization utility (each part is self-contained ? no shared library dependency, per the five-workbook independence in Section 4) plus the mechanism to reach Gateway.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * GatewayLink.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-33/L-34: Part 1 never keeps its own session store, and never
 * hardcodes Gateway's identity as a literal outside this file.
 * Two ways to call Gateway from Apps Script: same-account library
 * import, or url-fetch to a deployed web app. We use library import
 * here ? simpler, no auth-token juggling between two web apps.
 *
 * ONE-TIME MANUAL STEP (do this in the Apps Script editor UI,
 * not in code): Resources/Libraries > Add a library > paste
 * Part 0's Script ID > select the latest version > identifier "Gateway".
 * That Script ID is stored by Google as part of THIS project's
 * manifest, not as a string literal we write ? consistent with L-34.
 */

/** The one function every non-Gateway part calls to verify a session (L-33). */
function verifySession_(token) {
  return Gateway.verifySessionToken(token);
}

/** Wraps Gateway's RBAC check so Part 1's code never imports RbacService logic itself. */
function requirePermission_(actorRole, permissionKey) {
  if (!Gateway.hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, 'PART1')) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: role "' + actorRole + '" lacks permission "' + permissionKey + '" in PART1.');
  }
}
Before this will run, you need that one manual step noted in the comment ? Part 1's Apps Script project needs Part 0 added as a library. Here's exactly how, since it's UI, not code:
1. In Part 1's Apps Script editor: Libraries (left sidebar) ? + Add a library
2. You need Part 0's Script ID (not the Sheet ID) ? get it from Part 0's editor: Project Settings (gear icon) ? copy the "Script ID" shown there
3. Paste it in, click Look up, select the latest version, set identifier to Gateway, click Add
Also ? one thing I should flag honestly rather than skip: Part 0's verifySessionToken and hasPermission functions need to actually be callable as a library, which in Apps Script just means they need to not be private (no trailing underscore) ? checking back, both already qualify. Good.

Now the real Part 1 logic. First up: FlatService.gs ? canonicalization (Part 1's own copy, per the workbook-independence rule) plus flat CRUD, enforcing L-04 (RBAC), L-05 (rate override fields), and L-35 (canonicalized lookups).
File 3 ? Canonicalize.gs (identical to Part 0's ? deliberately duplicated, not shared, per Section 4's independence principle):
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * Canonicalize.gs ? identical logic to Part 0's copy, duplicated
 * on purpose. Each of the five workbooks is independent (Section 4);
 * sharing this via the Gateway library would create an unnecessary
 * cross-workbook dependency for something this small and stable.
 */
function _normalizeWhitespace(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}
function canonicalizeKey(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
}
function canonicalizeName(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.replace(/\w\S*/g, function (word) { return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.substr(1).toLowerCase(); });
}
File 4 ? FlatService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * FlatService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, GatewayLink.gs
 */

var FLAT_COLS_ = {
  FLAT_ID: 1, WING: 2, FLAT_NO: 3, AREA_SQFT: 4, FLOOR: 5, FLAT_TYPE: 6,
  RATE_MODE_OVERRIDE: 7, RATE_VALUE_OVERRIDE: 8, ACTIVE_FLAG: 9,
  CREATED_TS: 10, CREATED_BY: 11, UPDATED_TS: 12
};

/** Every governed write starts the same way: verify session, then check permission (L-14's spirit). */
function _authorize_(sessionToken, permissionKey) {
  var session = verifySession_(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(session.role, permissionKey);
  return session;
}

function createFlat(sessionToken, flatData) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_FLATS');
  // flatData: {wing, flatNo, areaSqFt, floor, flatType, rateModeOverride, rateValueOverride}

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.FLATS);
  var flatId = canonicalizeKey(flatData.wing) + '-' + canonicalizeKey(flatData.flatNo);

  // L-06's idempotency spirit applies here too: no duplicate Flat_ID.
  var existing = _findFlatRow_(flatId);
  if (existing) throw new Error('Flat ' + flatId + ' already exists.');

  var now = new Date();
  sheet.appendRow([
    flatId, canonicalizeName(flatData.wing), canonicalizeKey(flatData.flatNo),
    Number(flatData.areaSqFt) || 0, flatData.floor || '', canonicalizeKey(flatData.flatType || ''),
    flatData.rateModeOverride ? canonicalizeKey(flatData.rateModeOverride) : '',
    flatData.rateValueOverride || '', 'TRUE', now, session.userId, now
  ]);

  return { flatId: flatId };
}

function _findFlatRow_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.FLATS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][FLAT_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target) return { rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Resolves L-05's rate for a given flat: flat override -> (Global_Settings default, read via Gateway) */
function resolveRateForFlat(flatId) {
  var found = _findFlatRow_(flatId);
  if (!found) throw new Error('Flat ' + flatId + ' not found.');

  var override = found.values[FLAT_COLS_.RATE_MODE_OVERRIDE - 1];
  if (override) {
    return { mode: override, value: found.values[FLAT_COLS_.RATE_VALUE_OVERRIDE - 1], source: 'FLAT_OVERRIDE' };
  }
  var defaultMode = Gateway.getSetting('RATE_MODE_DEFAULT');
  return { mode: defaultMode, value: null, source: 'GLOBAL_DEFAULT' }; // PER_SQFT value comes from Area_SqFt itself, not a stored number
}

Now, OccupancyService.gs ? implements L-38's "closed, not deleted" ownership history.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * OccupancyService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, GatewayLink.gs, FlatService.gs (_authorize_, _findFlatRow_)
 */

var OCC_COLS_ = {
  OCC_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, OWNER_NAME: 3, OWNER_MOBILE: 4, OWNER_EMAIL: 5,
  TENANT_NAME: 6, TENANT_MOBILE: 7, TENANT_EMAIL: 8, STATUS: 9, IS_CURRENT: 10,
  EFFECTIVE_FROM: 11, EFFECTIVE_TO: 12, CLOSED_REASON: 13, CREATED_TS: 14, CREATED_BY: 15
};

/** First-ever occupancy for a flat ? e.g. initial handover from developer to first owner. No prior record to close. */
function setInitialOccupancy(sessionToken, occupancyData) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_OCCUPANCY');
  // occupancyData: {flatId, ownerName, ownerMobile, ownerEmail, occupancyStatus}

  var flat = _findFlatRow_(occupancyData.flatId);
  if (!flat) throw new Error('Flat ' + occupancyData.flatId + ' does not exist. Create it first.');

  var existingCurrent = _findCurrentOccupancy_(occupancyData.flatId);
  if (existingCurrent) throw new Error('Flat ' + occupancyData.flatId + ' already has a current occupant. Use transferOccupancy() instead.');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OCCUPANCY);
  var occId = 'OCC-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    occId, canonicalizeKey(occupancyData.flatId), canonicalizeName(occupancyData.ownerName),
    occupancyData.ownerMobile || '', occupancyData.ownerEmail || '', '', '', '',
    canonicalizeKey(occupancyData.occupancyStatus || 'OWNER_OCCUPIED'), 'TRUE', now, '', '', now, session.userId
  ]);

  return { occupancyId: occId };
}

function _findCurrentOccupancy_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OCCUPANCY);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][OCC_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target && String(data[r][OCC_COLS_.IS_CURRENT - 1]).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE') {
      return { rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] };
    }
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * L-38: full ownership transfer. Deliberately does NOT check dues-cleared itself ?
 * that's Ownership_Transfer_Log's job (next file), which this function requires
 * a reference to. This function only performs the mechanical close-old/open-new
 * once that gate has already passed.
 */
function transferOccupancy(sessionToken, flatId, newOwnerData, closedReason, transferLogId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_OCCUPANCY');
  if (!transferLogId) throw new Error('L-38: a transferLogId is required ? dues must be cleared/written off and logged BEFORE the occupancy record changes.');

  var current = _findCurrentOccupancy_(flatId);
  if (!current) throw new Error('No current occupancy found for ' + flatId + ' ? use setInitialOccupancy() instead.');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OCCUPANCY);
  var now = new Date();

  // Close the old record ? never delete (L-38).
  sheet.getRange(current.rowIndex, OCC_COLS_.IS_CURRENT).setValue('FALSE');
  sheet.getRange(current.rowIndex, OCC_COLS_.EFFECTIVE_TO).setValue(now);
  sheet.getRange(current.rowIndex, OCC_COLS_.CLOSED_REASON).setValue(canonicalizeKey(closedReason || 'OWNERSHIP_TRANSFER'));

  // Open the new one.
  var occId = 'OCC-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  sheet.appendRow([
    occId, canonicalizeKey(flatId), canonicalizeName(newOwnerData.ownerName),
    newOwnerData.ownerMobile || '', newOwnerData.ownerEmail || '', '', '', '',
    canonicalizeKey(newOwnerData.occupancyStatus || 'OWNER_OCCUPIED'), 'TRUE', now, '', '', now, session.userId
  ]);

  return { closedOccupancyId: current.values[OCC_COLS_.OCC_ID - 1], newOccupancyId: occId };
}
File 7 ? Test_Stage_B_03.gs
function test_Stage_B_03_initial_occupancy() {
  var session = Gateway.login('9123602715', 'YourRealAdmin1Password');
  var result = setInitialOccupancy(session.token, {
    flatId: 'GA-3D', ownerName: 'first owner name', ownerMobile: '9000000001',
    ownerEmail: 'owner1@example.com', occupancyStatus: 'OWNER_OCCUPIED'
  });
  Logger.log('Initial occupancy set: ' + result.occupancyId);

  // Prove the guard: setting initial occupancy AGAIN for the same flat should fail.
  try {
    setInitialOccupancy(session.token, { flatId: 'GA-3D', ownerName: 'someone else', ownerMobile: '9000000002' });
    Logger.log('ERROR: a second initial occupancy was allowed ? this should not happen.');
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Correctly refused: ' + e.message);
  }
}

File 8 ? OwnershipTransferService.gs (implements L-38's dues-cleared gate)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 1: MEMBERS/FLAT DIRECTORY
 * OwnershipTransferService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, GatewayLink.gs, OccupancyService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-38: this is where the "dues cleared or written off" GATE lives.
 * transferOccupancy() refuses to run without a valid, matching
 * Transfer_ID from here ? the mechanical occupancy swap can never
 * happen before this record proves the gate was satisfied.
 */

var TRANSFER_COLS_ = {
  TRANSFER_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, PREV_OWNER: 3, NEW_OWNER: 4, DUES_CLEARED_PAISE: 5,
  WRITTEN_OFF_PAISE: 6, WRITE_OFF_APPROVED_BY: 7, TRANSFER_DATE: 8,
  LINKED_OCC_CLOSED: 9, LINKED_OCC_NEW: 10, CREATED_TS: 11
};

/**
 * Call this FIRST, before transferOccupancy(). Records the dues
 * settlement decision as its own auditable fact.
 * writtenOffAmountPaise > 0 requires writeOffApprovedBy (committee
 * member name/ID) ? the code enforces that pairing, not just convention.
 */
function recordOwnershipTransfer(sessionToken, flatId, previousOwnerName, newOwnerName, duesClearedPaise, writtenOffPaise, writeOffApprovedBy) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'APPROVE_WRITE_OFF'); // matches the RBAC permission already seeded in Part 0

  var written = Number(writtenOffPaise) || 0;
  if (written > 0 && !writeOffApprovedBy) {
    throw new Error('L-38: a write-off amount requires a named committee approver.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OWNERSHIP_TRANSFER_LOG);
  var transferId = 'XFER-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    transferId, canonicalizeKey(flatId), canonicalizeName(previousOwnerName), canonicalizeName(newOwnerName),
    Number(duesClearedPaise) || 0, written, writeOffApprovedBy || '', now, '', '', now
  ]);

  return { transferId: transferId };
}

/** Called AFTER transferOccupancy() succeeds, to back-fill the linked occupancy IDs onto this record ? closing the loop for audit. */
function _linkOccupancyIdsToTransfer_(transferId, closedOccId, newOccId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OWNERSHIP_TRANSFER_LOG);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][TRANSFER_COLS_.TRANSFER_ID - 1] === transferId) {
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, TRANSFER_COLS_.LINKED_OCC_CLOSED).setValue(closedOccId);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, TRANSFER_COLS_.LINKED_OCC_NEW).setValue(newOccId);
      return;
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Transfer record ' + transferId + ' not found during linking ? this should not happen.');
}

function _transferExists_(transferId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.OWNERSHIP_TRANSFER_LOG);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][TRANSFER_COLS_.TRANSFER_ID - 1] === transferId) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

_linkOccupancyIdsToTransfer_(transferLogId, current.values[OCC_COLS_.OCC_ID - 1], occId);

One thing worth flagging honestly: Gateway.logAudit_() ? does that underscore-suffixed name actually work as a cross-library call? Library-exposed functions in Apps Script need to be non-private, and a trailing underscore is Apps Script's own convention for "treat as private." I need to double check this before you spend time wiring it up.Good thing I checked ? that confirms the concern was real. Per Google's own documentation: "To make one or more methods of your script not be visible (nor usable) to your library users, end the name of the method with an underscore." Since Part 0's logAudit_() already ends in an underscore, Gateway.logAudit_(...) would silently be inaccessible from Part 1 ? this would have failed with a confusing "not a function" error the moment you tried it.
Fix: Part 0 needs a thin public wrapper that Part 1 can actually call. This is a small addition to Part 0's AuditService.gs ? worth noting, this is exactly the kind of change L-20 (schema/code change discipline) wants tracked, even though it's minor.
Add to Part 0's AuditService.gs:
/**
 * Public wrapper ? the ONLY audit-logging entry point other parts may call.
 * logAudit_() itself stays private; this exists purely because Apps Script
 * library rules make underscore-suffixed functions invisible to importers.
 */
function logAuditFromOtherPart(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetWorkbook, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, justificationNote) {
  logAudit_(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetWorkbook, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, justificationNote);
}

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * Bootstrap.gs

function setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() {
  var SPREADSHEET_ID = '14Ihv8LLC8V5nZ4FSi-omz8osEgr_rGXfTMIDcf0foRk';
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID', SPREADSHEET_ID);
  Logger.log('Spreadsheet ID stored in Script Properties.');
}

function getSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) throw new Error('SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once first.');
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);
}

var SHEETS_ = {
  MAINTENANCE_BILLS: 'Maintenance_Bills',
  MISC_BILLS: 'Misc_Bills',
  PAYMENT_RECEIPTS: 'Payment_Receipts',
  MISC_RECEIPTS: 'Misc_Receipts',
  MEMBER_CREDITS: 'Member_Credits',
  VENDOR_MASTER: 'Vendor_Master',
  EXPENSES_VOUCHERS: 'Expenses_Vouchers',
  PENDING_TRANSACTIONS: 'Pending_Transactions',
  SEQUENCE_COUNTERS: 'Sequence_Counters'
};

function getSheet_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getSS_().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName + '. Schema drift? Check L-20.');
  return sheet;
}
File 2 ? Canonicalize.gs (same as Parts 0/1, duplicated on purpose)
function _normalizeWhitespace(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}
function canonicalizeKey(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
}
function canonicalizeName(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.replace(/\w\S*/g, function (word) { return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.substr(1).toLowerCase(); });
}
File 3 ? GatewayLink.gs ? add Part 0 as a library here too, identifier Gateway, exactly as done for Part 1.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * GatewayLink.gs
 */
function verifySession_(token) {
  return Gateway.verifySessionToken(token);
}
function requirePermission_(actorRole, permissionKey) {
  if (!Gateway.hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, 'PART2')) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: role "' + actorRole + '" lacks permission "' + permissionKey + '" in PART2.');
  }
}
function _authorize_(sessionToken, permissionKey) {
  var session = verifySession_(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(session.role, permissionKey);
  return session;
}
function logAudit_(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId) {
  Gateway.logAuditFromOtherPart(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, 'PART2', targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, '');
}
