File 5 ? SequenceCounterService.gs (L-13 ? the real fix for gap-free, concurrency-safe numbering)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * SequenceCounterService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * L-13: atomic, gap-free, per-financial-year numbering. Every
 * future ID (Bill_ID, Receipt_No, Voucher_No, etc.) goes through
 * getNextSequence_() ? never "read last row, add 1."
 */
function getNextSequence_(counterKey, financialYear) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(15000);
  try {
    var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.SEQUENCE_COUNTERS);
    var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    var key = canonicalizeKey(counterKey);

    for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
      if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][0]) === key && data[r][1] === financialYear) {
        var next = Number(data[r][2]) + 1;
        sheet.getRange(r + 1, 3).setValue(next);
        sheet.getRange(r + 1, 4).setValue(new Date());
        return next;
      }
    }
    // No row yet for this counter+year ? start at 1.
    sheet.appendRow([counterKey, financialYear, 1, new Date()]);
    return 1;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/** Current Financial Year string, e.g. "2026-27", per L-39 (1 July?30 June). */
function getCurrentFinancialYear_() {
  var now = new Date();
  var year = now.getFullYear();
  var month = now.getMonth() + 1; // 1-12
  if (month >= 7) return year + '-' + String(year + 1).slice(-2);
  return (year - 1) + '-' + String(year).slice(-2);
}
File 6 ? Test_Stage_C_01.gs
function test_Stage_C_01_sequence() {
  setup_StoreSpreadsheetId();
  var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();
  Logger.log('Current FY: ' + fy);

  var a = getNextSequence_('RECEIPT', fy);
  var b = getNextSequence_('RECEIPT', fy);
  var c = getNextSequence_('BILL', fy); // different counter ? should start at 1 independently
  Logger.log('RECEIPT seq: ' + a + ', ' + b + ' (expect 1, 2)');
  Logger.log('BILL seq: ' + c + ' (expect 1, independent of RECEIPT)');
}

var VENDOR_COLS_ = {
  VENDOR_ID: 1, VENDOR_NAME: 2, GSTIN: 3, PAN: 4, CONTACT_PERSON: 5,
  MOBILE: 6, EMAIL: 7, ACTIVE_FLAG: 8, CREATED_TS: 9, CREATED_BY: 10
};

function createVendor(sessionToken, vendorData) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_VENDORS');
  // vendorData: {vendorName, gstin, pan, contactPerson, mobile, email}

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.VENDOR_MASTER);
  var vendorId = 'VEN-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    vendorId, canonicalizeName(vendorData.vendorName), canonicalizeKey(vendorData.gstin || ''),
    canonicalizeKey(vendorData.pan || ''), canonicalizeName(vendorData.contactPerson || ''),
    vendorData.mobile || '', vendorData.email || '', 'TRUE', now, session.userId
  ]);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'VENDOR_CREATED', SHEETS_.VENDOR_MASTER, vendorId, 'New vendor: ' + vendorData.vendorName, '');
  return { vendorId: vendorId };
}

function _findVendorRow_(vendorId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.VENDOR_MASTER);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(vendorId);
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][VENDOR_COLS_.VENDOR_ID - 1]) === target) return { rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] };
  }
  return null;
}

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * ExpenseVoucherService.gs ? L-31 maker-checker
 */
var VOUCHER_COLS_ = {
  VOUCHER_NO: 1, EXPENSE_DATE: 2, CATEGORY: 3, VENDOR_ID: 4, BASE_AMOUNT: 5,
  GST_AMOUNT: 6, TOTAL_AMOUNT: 7, PAYMENT_MODE: 8, DESCRIPTION: 9,
  MAKER: 10, CHECKER: 11, APPROVAL_STATUS: 12, PDF_URL: 13, HASH: 14, CREATED_TS: 15, RECORD_VERSION: 16
};

/** Step 1 ? maker creates the voucher. Always starts PENDING_CHECKER, regardless of amount, for a consistent trail ? but only vouchers ABOVE the threshold actually require a second approval before posting (see approveExpenseVoucher below). */
function createExpenseVoucher(sessionToken, voucherData) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'POST_EXPENSE_VOUCHER');
  // voucherData: {expenseDate, category, vendorId, baseAmountPaise, gstAmountPaise, paymentMode, description}

  var vendor = _findVendorRow_(voucherData.vendorId);
  if (!vendor) throw new Error('Vendor ' + voucherData.vendorId + ' not found.');

  var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();
  var seq = getNextSequence_('VOUCHER', fy);
  var voucherNo = 'V-' + fy + '-' + String(seq).padStart(5, '0');

  var base = Math.ceil(Number(voucherData.baseAmountPaise) || 0); // L-30: round up
  var gst = Math.ceil(Number(voucherData.gstAmountPaise) || 0);
  var total = base + gst;

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.EXPENSES_VOUCHERS);
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    voucherNo, voucherData.expenseDate || now, canonicalizeKey(voucherData.category || ''),
    canonicalizeKey(voucherData.vendorId), base, gst, total,
    canonicalizeKey(voucherData.paymentMode || ''), voucherData.description || '',
    session.userId, '', 'PENDING_CHECKER', '', '', now, 1
  ]);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'VOUCHER_CREATED', SHEETS_.EXPENSES_VOUCHERS, voucherNo, 'Total: ' + total + ' paise', '');
  return { voucherNo: voucherNo, totalAmountPaise: total };
}

/** Step 2 ? checker approves. Enforces maker != checker (L-31) and the threshold check. */
function approveExpenseVoucher(sessionToken, voucherNo) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'APPROVE_EXPENSE_VOUCHER');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.EXPENSES_VOUCHERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][VOUCHER_COLS_.VOUCHER_NO - 1] === voucherNo) {
      var maker = data[r][VOUCHER_COLS_.MAKER - 1];
      var status = data[r][VOUCHER_COLS_.APPROVAL_STATUS - 1];
      var total = Number(data[r][VOUCHER_COLS_.TOTAL_AMOUNT - 1]);

      if (status !== 'PENDING_CHECKER') throw new Error('Voucher ' + voucherNo + ' is not pending approval (status: ' + status + ').');
      if (maker === session.userId) throw new Error('L-31: maker and checker must be different people. You created this voucher.');

      var threshold = Gateway.getSetting('MAKER_CHECKER_THRESHOLD_PAISE');
      if (total <= threshold) {
        // Below threshold: this approval step is optional per policy, but if called, it still just confirms.
      }

      sheet.getRange(r + 1, VOUCHER_COLS_.CHECKER).setValue(session.userId);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, VOUCHER_COLS_.APPROVAL_STATUS).setValue('APPROVED');

      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'VOUCHER_APPROVED', SHEETS_.EXPENSES_VOUCHERS, voucherNo, 'Approved by different user than maker', '');
      return { voucherNo: voucherNo, status: 'APPROVED' };
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Voucher ' + voucherNo + ' not found.');
}

/** AMENDED (Stage C need): now also returns areaSqFt, required for PER_SQFT billing calculations in Part 2. */
function resolveRateForFlat(flatId) {
  var found = _findFlatRow_(flatId);
  if (!found) throw new Error('Flat ' + flatId + ' not found.');

  var override = found.values[FLAT_COLS_.RATE_MODE_OVERRIDE - 1];
  var areaSqFt = Number(found.values[FLAT_COLS_.AREA_SQFT - 1]) || 0;

  if (override) {
    return { mode: override, value: found.values[FLAT_COLS_.RATE_VALUE_OVERRIDE - 1], source: 'FLAT_OVERRIDE', areaSqFt: areaSqFt };
  }
  var defaultMode = Gateway.getSetting('RATE_MODE_DEFAULT');
  return { mode: defaultMode, value: null, source: 'GLOBAL_DEFAULT', areaSqFt: areaSqFt };
}
This is purely additive ? nothing that calls resolveRateForFlat() today breaks, since existing fields stay exactly as they were. File 11 ? BillingService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * BillingService.gs
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, GatewayLink.gs, FlatsLink.gs, SequenceCounterService.gs
 */
var BILL_COLS_ = {
  BILL_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, BILL_MONTH_YEAR: 3, FINANCIAL_YEAR: 4, BASE_CHARGE: 5,
  PREV_UNPAID: 6, LATE_FINE: 7, TOTAL_PAYABLE: 8, DUE_DATE: 9, PAYMENT_STATUS: 10,
  PDF_URL: 11, HASH: 12, CREATED_TS: 13, CREATED_BY: 14, RECORD_VERSION: 15
};

function _resolveBaseCharge_(flatId) {
  var rateInfo = resolveFlatRate_(flatId); // from FlatsLink.gs -> Members.resolveRateForFlat
  var mode = canonicalizeKey(rateInfo.mode);

  if (mode === 'PER_SQFT') {
    var perSqft = rateInfo.value ? Number(rateInfo.value) : Number(Gateway.getSetting('RATE_PER_SQFT_PAISE'));
    return Math.ceil(rateInfo.areaSqFt * perSqft); // L-30
  }
  // FLAT_SPECIFIC or GLOBAL_FLAT
  var flatAmount = rateInfo.value ? Number(rateInfo.value) : Number(Gateway.getSetting('GLOBAL_FLAT_RATE_PAISE'));
  return Math.ceil(flatAmount);
}

function _findBillsForFlat_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  var rows = [];
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][BILL_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target) rows.push({ rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] });
  }
  return rows;
}

/** L-07: fine is based on the PREVIOUS bill being overdue and unpaid ? never the current one. */
function _computeLateFine_(previousBillRow) {
  if (!previousBillRow) return 0;
  var status = previousBillRow.values[BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS - 1];
  var dueDate = new Date(previousBillRow.values[BILL_COLS_.DUE_DATE - 1]);
  var now = new Date();

  if (status === 'PAID' || now <= dueDate) return 0; // not overdue, or already settled ? no fine

  var mode = canonicalizeKey(Gateway.getSetting('PENALTY_MODE'));
  if (mode === 'FLAT') {
    return Math.ceil(Number(Gateway.getSetting('PENALTY_FLAT_AMOUNT_PAISE')));
  }
  if (mode === 'PERCENTAGE') {
    var pct = Number(Gateway.getSetting('PENALTY_PERCENTAGE'));
    var base = Number(previousBillRow.values[BILL_COLS_.BASE_CHARGE - 1]);
    return Math.ceil(base * pct / 100);
  }
  if (mode === 'PER_DAY') {
    var perDay = Number(Gateway.getSetting('PENALTY_PER_DAY_PAISE'));
    var daysLate = Math.floor((now - dueDate) / 86400000);
    return Math.ceil(perDay * daysLate);
  }
  throw new Error('Unknown PENALTY_MODE: ' + mode);
}

/** L-06: exactly one bill per (Flat_ID, Bill_Month_Year). */
function generateMonthlyBill(sessionToken, flatId, billMonthYear, dueDate) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'GENERATE_BILL');

  var existingBills = _findBillsForFlat_(flatId);
  var targetPeriod = canonicalizeKey(billMonthYear);
  for (var i = 0; i < existingBills.length; i++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(existingBills[i].values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_MONTH_YEAR - 1]) === targetPeriod) {
      throw new Error('Bill already exists for ' + flatId + ' / ' + billMonthYear + ' (L-06).');
    }
  }

  // Previous unpaid = sum of all not-fully-paid prior bills for this flat.
  var previousUnpaid = 0;
  var mostRecentBill = null;
  existingBills.forEach(function (b) {
    var status = b.values[BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS - 1];
    if (status !== 'PAID') previousUnpaid += Number(b.values[BILL_COLS_.TOTAL_PAYABLE - 1]);
    if (!mostRecentBill || new Date(b.values[BILL_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1]) > new Date(mostRecentBill.values[BILL_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1])) mostRecentBill = b;
  });

  var baseCharge = _resolveBaseCharge_(flatId);
  var lateFine = _computeLateFine_(mostRecentBill);
  var totalPayable = baseCharge + previousUnpaid + lateFine;

  var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();
  var seq = getNextSequence_('BILL', fy);
  var billId = 'BILL-' + fy + '-' + String(seq).padStart(5, '0');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var now = new Date();
  sheet.appendRow([
    billId, canonicalizeKey(flatId), canonicalizeKey(billMonthYear), fy,
    baseCharge, previousUnpaid, lateFine, totalPayable, dueDate, 'UNPAID',
    '', '', now, session.userId, 1
  ]);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'BILL_GENERATED', SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS, billId, 'Total: ' + totalPayable + ' paise', '');
  return { billId: billId, baseCharge: baseCharge, previousUnpaid: previousUnpaid, lateFine: lateFine, totalPayable: totalPayable };
}

Before running: add both settings rows above to Part 0's Global_Settings, and add ADMIN | GENERATE_BILL | TRUE | PART2 | bill generation to Roles_Permission_Matrix if it's not already there from the original seed (it wasn't ? this is a new permission). Then run and share the log.
Good catch on the test's own guard ? this is a real bug, not a fluke, and it's worth understanding exactly why, because it's a well-known Google Sheets trap that could bite other date-like fields too.
Likely cause: Bill_Month_Year is stored as a plain string like 'AUG-2026' ? but Google Sheets auto-detects text that looks like a date and silently converts it to an actual Date value in the cell, especially next to a real date column (Due_Date). So the first write stores 'AUG-2026', but Sheets may have quietly turned it into a real date object. When _findBillsForFlat_() reads it back and canonicalizeKey() runs String(dateObject), it produces something like "Wed Aug 01 2026 00:00:00 GMT+0530" ? which never matches canonicalizeKey('AUG-2026') again. The duplicate check silently fails to find its own prior row.
Please check one thing first ? open Maintenance_Bills and look at the Bill_Month_Year cell for BILL-2026-27-00002. If it's right-aligned (Sheets' date/number default) rather than left-aligned like plain text, that confirms it.
Fix ? force that column to stay plain text, in generateMonthlyBill(), right after appendRow:
var newRow = sheet.getLastRow();
sheet.getRange(newRow, BILL_COLS_.BILL_MONTH_YEAR).setNumberFormat('@'); // force plain text, stop Sheets auto-date-conversion
sheet.getRange(newRow, BILL_COLS_.BILL_MONTH_YEAR).setValue(canonicalizeKey(billMonthYear)); // re-set the value now that format is locked
Add these two lines immediately after the existing sheet.appendRow([...]) call.
Cleanup needed: there are now likely two rows for GA-3D / AUG-2026 in Maintenance_Bills (the real one from the first call, and the unintended duplicate from the second). Please delete the duplicate row manually before re-testing, so we start from a clean, single-bill state.

ReceiptService.gs ? this is the most complex piece so far, since it's where L-08 (waterfall), L-09 (credit), L-10 (concession), L-11 (immutability), and L-30 (rounding) all meet at once. One design decision I made and want to flag explicitly before you build on it, rather than bury it silently: a concession is applied entirely against the oldest outstanding bill, not spread across several ? this matches how concessions are actually used in practice (committee waives part of one specific bill) and keeps the audit trail clean (one concession, one bill, one advice note). Flag it now if that's not what you intended.
File 13 ? ReceiptService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * ReceiptService.gs ? L-08 waterfall, L-09 credit, L-10 concession, L-11 immutability
 */
var RECEIPT_COLS_ = {
  RECEIPT_NO: 1, BILL_ID: 2, FLAT_ID: 3, PAYMENT_DATE: 4, PAYMENT_MODE: 5,
  PAID_AMOUNT: 6, FINE_APPLIED: 7, CONCESSION: 8, ADVICE_NOTE: 9, BALANCE_REMAINING: 10,
  RECEIPT_STATUS: 11, VOID_REASON: 12, PDF_URL: 13, HASH: 14, LOGGED_BY: 15,
  CREATED_TS: 16, RECORD_VERSION: 17, REVERSAL_RECEIPT_NO: 18
};
var CREDIT_COLS_ = {
  CREDIT_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, SOURCE_RECEIPT: 3, CREDIT_DATE: 4, CREDIT_PAISE: 5,
  APPLIED_PAISE: 6, REMAINING_PAISE: 7, STATUS: 8, REFUND_REF: 9, CREATED_BY: 10, CREATED_TS: 11
};

/** Sum of all ACTIVE receipts' (Paid_Amount + Concession) applied against a given Bill_ID. */
function _amountAppliedToBill_(billId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var total = 0;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1] === billId && data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_STATUS - 1] === 'ACTIVE') {
      total += Number(data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.PAID_AMOUNT - 1]) + Number(data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.CONCESSION - 1]);
    }
  }
  return total;
}

function _getBillOutstanding_(billRow) {
  var total = Number(billRow.values[BILL_COLS_.TOTAL_PAYABLE - 1]);
  var applied = _amountAppliedToBill_(billRow.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1]);
  return total - applied;
}

/** Oldest-first, per L-08. Bill_Month_Year sorting relies on Created_Timestamp as the tiebreaker/proxy for period order. */
function _getOldestUnpaidBills_(flatId) {
  var bills = _findBillsForFlat_(flatId);
  var withOutstanding = bills.map(function (b) { return { row: b, outstanding: _getBillOutstanding_(b) }; })
    .filter(function (x) { return x.outstanding > 0; });
  withOutstanding.sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(a.row.values[BILL_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1]) - new Date(b.row.values[BILL_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1]); });
  return withOutstanding.map(function (x) { return x.row; });
}

/**
 * L-08 waterfall: applies payment oldest-bill-first. Concession (if any)
 * applies entirely to the oldest bill touched. Surplus after all bills
 * are cleared becomes a Member_Credit (L-09) ? never income.
 */
function recordPayment(sessionToken, flatId, paidAmountPaise, paymentMode, concessionPaise, committeeAdviceNote) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'RECORD_RECEIPT');
  var paid = Math.ceil(Number(paidAmountPaise) || 0); // L-30
  var concession = Math.ceil(Number(concessionPaise) || 0);

  if (paid <= 0) throw new Error('Paid amount must be positive.');
  if (concession > 0 && (!committeeAdviceNote || committeeAdviceNote.trim().length < 5)) {
    throw new Error('L-10: a concession requires a real Committee Advice Note.');
  }

  var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();
  var receiptNos = [];
  var remainingCash = paid;
  var remainingConcession = concession;
  var bills = _getOldestUnpaidBills_(flatId);
  var now = new Date();
  var billSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var receiptSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS);

  for (var i = 0; i < bills.length && (remainingCash > 0 || remainingConcession > 0); i++) {
    var bill = bills[i];
    var outstanding = _getBillOutstanding_(bill);
    if (outstanding <= 0) continue;

    var concessionHere = Math.min(remainingConcession, outstanding);
    var cashHere = Math.min(remainingCash, outstanding - concessionHere);
    if (concessionHere === 0 && cashHere === 0) continue;

    var seq = getNextSequence_('RECEIPT', fy);
    var receiptNo = 'RCT-' + fy + '-' + String(seq).padStart(5, '0');

    receiptSheet.appendRow([
      receiptNo, bill.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1], canonicalizeKey(flatId), now,
      canonicalizeKey(paymentMode), cashHere, 0, concessionHere,
      concessionHere > 0 ? committeeAdviceNote : '', 0, 'ACTIVE', '', '', '', session.userId, now, 1, ''
    ]);
    receiptNos.push(receiptNo);

    var newOutstanding = outstanding - concessionHere - cashHere;
    billSheet.getRange(bill.rowIndex, BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS).setValue(newOutstanding <= 0 ? 'PAID' : 'PARTIALLY_PAID');

    logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'RECEIPT_RECORDED', SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS, receiptNo,
      'Bill ' + bill.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1] + ': cash ' + cashHere + ', concession ' + concessionHere, '');

    remainingCash -= cashHere;
    remainingConcession -= concessionHere;
  }

  var creditCreated = null;
  if (remainingCash > 0) {
    var lastReceiptNo = receiptNos.length ? receiptNos[receiptNos.length - 1] : '';
    var creditId = 'CR-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_CREDITS).appendRow([
      creditId, canonicalizeKey(flatId), lastReceiptNo, now, remainingCash, 0, remainingCash, 'ACTIVE', '', session.userId, now
    ]);
    logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'MEMBER_CREDIT_CREATED', SHEETS_.MEMBER_CREDITS, creditId, 'Surplus: ' + remainingCash + ' paise', '');
    creditCreated = { creditId: creditId, amountPaise: remainingCash };
  }

  return { receiptNos: receiptNos, creditCreated: creditCreated };
}

File 16 ? TransactionQueueService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * TransactionQueueService.gs ? L-15/L-16
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * The ONLY way Part 2 communicates a financial event to Part 3.
 * Never writes to Part 3's Ledger_Entries directly ? enqueues
 * here, and Part 3's own script (built in Stage D) reads and posts.
 */
var QUEUE_COLS_ = {
  JOURNAL_ID: 1, CREATED_TS: 2, STATE: 3, SOURCE_TYPE: 4, SOURCE_RECORD_ID: 5,
  FLAT_ID: 6, AMOUNT_PAISE: 7, PAYLOAD_JSON: 8, ACTOR_USER_ID: 9,
  COMPLETED_TS: 10, ERROR_MESSAGE: 11, COMPENSATION_ACTION: 12
};

/**
 * sourceType: 'RECEIPT' | 'MISC_RECEIPT' | 'EXPENSE_VOUCHER' | 'CREDIT_REFUND'
 * payload: whatever Part 3 needs to build the journal entry ? kept as a
 * plain object here, stored as JSON, so Part 2 never needs to know Part 3's
 * internal ledger structure. That boundary is deliberate (Section 4).
 */
function enqueueTransaction_(sourceType, sourceRecordId, flatId, amountPaise, payload, actorUserId, compensationAction) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.PENDING_TRANSACTIONS);
  var journalId = 'JRN-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    journalId, now, 'PENDING', canonicalizeKey(sourceType), sourceRecordId,
    canonicalizeKey(flatId || ''), amountPaise, JSON.stringify(payload || {}),
    actorUserId, '', '', compensationAction || ''
  ]);

  return { journalId: journalId };
}

}, session.userId, 'REVERSE_RECEIPT_' + receiptNo);
And after the credit-creation block, right after its logAudit_('MEMBER_CREDIT_CREATED', ...) call:

And in approveExpenseVoucher() (ExpenseVoucherService.gs), right after its logAudit_('VOUCHER_APPROVED', ...) call:

Here's the Part 3 ? Accounting Book schema, designed fresh from the locked clauses. Build this as RANIKUTHI_V5_P3_ACCOUNTING_BOOK, with these 8 tabs:
1. Chart_of_Accounts ? the corrected CoA, seeded from Section 4 of the reference doc Account_Code, Account_Name, Account_Class, Normal_Balance, Parent_Account_Code, Active_Flag, Description, Created_Timestamp (Account_Class: ASSET/LIABILITY/EQUITY/INCOME/EXPENSE/OTHER. This is where 23100 Member Advance/Credit Payable, 60300 Written-Off Member Dues, and the 401xx/402xx mutuality-split income accounts get seeded.)
2. Ledger_Entries ? the actual double-entry book Ledger_ID, Journal_ID, Entry_Date, Financial_Year, Flat_ID, Debit_Account_Code, Credit_Account_Code, Amount_Paise, Source_Type, Source_Record_ID, Description, Reversal_Of_Ledger_ID, Created_Timestamp, Created_By (Journal_ID is the link back to Part 2's Pending_Transactions ? this is also the idempotency key: before posting, check whether a Journal_ID already has a ledger row, so a queue item can never be posted twice even if the consuming trigger runs more than once.)
3. Financial_Periods ? implements L-39's year-end discipline Period_ID, Financial_Year, Start_Date, End_Date, Status, Closed_By, Closed_Timestamp, CA_Signoff_Note
4. Fixed_Assets_Register ? the original schema-gap fix, finally getting built Asset_ID, Asset_Name, Category, Purchase_Date, Purchase_Cost_Paise, Depreciation_Method, Depreciation_Rate_Percent, Accumulated_Depreciation_Paise, Book_Value_Paise, Active_Flag, Created_Timestamp
5. Bank_Reconciliation ? built now per L-*, even with no bank account active yet Reconciliation_ID, Bank_Account_Code, Statement_Date, Statement_Balance_Paise, Ledger_Balance_Paise, Difference_Paise, Status, Reconciled_By, Reconciled_Timestamp, Notes
6. GST_Ledger ? built now despite no GST registration yet, same reasoning GST_Entry_ID, Transaction_Date, Source_Type, Source_Record_ID, Taxable_Amount_Paise, GST_Amount_Paise, GST_Rate_Percent, Direction, Created_Timestamp
7. Accounting_Reports Report_ID, Report_Type, Financial_Year, Generated_Timestamp, Generated_By, PDF_Drive_URL, SHA256_Hash
8. Member_Balance_Summary ? the CQRS read-model from L-17, finally getting a real home Flat_ID, Total_Billed_Paise, Total_Paid_Paise, Outstanding_Paise, Credit_Balance_Paise, Last_Payment_Date, Last_Reconciled_Timestamp (Always rebuildable from Ledger_Entries ? this cache is never the source of truth, only a fast mirror of it.)
That's 8 sheets ? the accounting core the whole rest of the system has been building toward. Once you've created it and run the schema-verification script, share the Sheet ID and log, same as every part before this.

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * Bootstrap.gs
 */
function setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() {
  var SPREADSHEET_ID = '1pHYzkl4S8ymPgZMl7YFceiBVocti6Z33Jy0UReWM2uM';
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID', SPREADSHEET_ID);
  Logger.log('Spreadsheet ID stored in Script Properties.');
}
function getSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) throw new Error('SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once first.');
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);
}
var SHEETS_ = {
  CHART_OF_ACCOUNTS: 'Chart_of_Accounts',
  LEDGER_ENTRIES: 'Ledger_Entries',
  FINANCIAL_PERIODS: 'Financial_Periods',
  FIXED_ASSETS_REGISTER: 'Fixed_Assets_Register',
  BANK_RECONCILIATION: 'Bank_Reconciliation',
  GST_LEDGER: 'GST_Ledger',
  ACCOUNTING_REPORTS: 'Accounting_Reports',
  MEMBER_BALANCE_SUMMARY: 'Member_Balance_Summary'
};
function getSheet_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getSS_().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName + '. Schema drift? Check L-20.');
  return sheet;
}
File 2 ? Canonicalize.gs (same as always)
function _normalizeWhitespace(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}
function canonicalizeKey(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
}
function canonicalizeName(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.replace(/\w\S*/g, function (word) { return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.substr(1).toLowerCase(); });
}
File 3 ? GatewayLink.gs ? add Part 0 as a library here too, identifier Gateway. Part 3 doesn't need Part 1 or Part 2 as libraries ? it only reads Part 2's queue by opening that spreadsheet directly by ID (a queue is data to be consumed, not an API to call), which is a different, simpler relationship than the RBAC/rate-lookup calls Parts 1/2 needed.
function verifySession_(token) { return Gateway.verifySessionToken(token); }
function requirePermission_(actorRole, permissionKey) {
  if (!Gateway.hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, 'PART3')) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: role "' + actorRole + '" lacks permission "' + permissionKey + '" in PART3.');
  }
}
function _authorize_(sessionToken, permissionKey) {
  var session = verifySession_(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(session.role, permissionKey);
  return session;
}
function logAudit_(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId) {
  Gateway.logAuditFromOtherPart(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, 'PART3', targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, '');
}
File 4 ? SeedChartOfAccounts.gs ? the full corrected CoA from the reference doc, seeded once, safe to re-run.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * SeedChartOfAccounts.gs
 * The corrected CoA: 23100 as Member Credit (Liability, not Asset),
 * 60300 for write-offs (L-38), 401xx/402xx mutuality split (L-18).
 */
function setup_SeedChartOfAccounts() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.CHART_OF_ACCOUNTS);
  var existing = {};
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) { if (data[r][0]) existing[String(data[r][0])] = true; }

  var accounts = [
    // Code, Name, Class, Normal_Balance, Parent, Description
    ['10000', 'ASSETS', 'ASSET', 'DEBIT', '', 'Top-level Assets header'],
    ['11000', 'Cash', 'ASSET', 'DEBIT', '10000', 'Cash on hand'],
    ['12000', 'Bank Accounts', 'ASSET', 'DEBIT', '10000', 'Bank Reconciliation ties here (L-*)'],
    ['13000', 'Maintenance Receivable', 'ASSET', 'DEBIT', '10000', 'Amounts owed by members'],
    ['14000', 'Fixed Assets', 'ASSET', 'DEBIT', '10000', 'Ties to Fixed_Assets_Register'],
    ['20000', 'LIABILITIES', 'LIABILITY', 'CREDIT', '', 'Top-level Liabilities header'],
    ['21000', 'Vendor Payable', 'LIABILITY', 'CREDIT', '20000', 'Amounts owed to vendors'],
    ['22000', 'GST Payable', 'LIABILITY', 'CREDIT', '20000', 'If/when GST registered'],
    ['23100', 'Member Advance / Credit Payable', 'LIABILITY', 'CREDIT', '20000', 'L-09: overpayment owed BACK to member, never income'],
    ['30000', 'EQUITY / RESERVES', 'EQUITY', 'CREDIT', '', 'Top-level Equity header'],
    ['31000', 'Accumulated Surplus', 'EQUITY', 'CREDIT', '30000', 'L-39: rolled up at year-end close'],
    ['40100', 'Member Maintenance Income', 'INCOME', 'CREDIT', '', 'L-18: mutuality-principle member income'],
    ['40200', 'Member Misc Income (Puja/Donations)', 'INCOME', 'CREDIT', '', 'L-19: Misc_Receipts source'],
    ['40300', 'Late Fine Income', 'INCOME', 'CREDIT', '', 'L-07 penalties collected'],
    ['40400', 'Interest Income', 'INCOME', 'CREDIT', '', ''],
    ['40201', 'Non-Member/Taxable Income', 'INCOME', 'CREDIT', '', 'L-18: hall rental, tower rent, ad income ? NOT mutuality-exempt'],
    ['50100', 'Repairs & Maintenance Expense', 'EXPENSE', 'DEBIT', '', ''],
    ['50200', 'Utilities Expense', 'EXPENSE', 'DEBIT', '', ''],
    ['50300', 'Salaries & Staff Expense', 'EXPENSE', 'DEBIT', '', ''],
    ['50400', 'Depreciation Expense', 'EXPENSE', 'DEBIT', '', 'Fixed_Assets_Register ties here'],
    ['50900', 'Other/General Expense', 'EXPENSE', 'DEBIT', '', 'Default bucket for Expenses_Vouchers without a specific mapping'],
    ['60300', 'Written-Off Member Dues (Committee Approved)', 'OTHER', 'DEBIT', '', 'L-38: ownership-transfer write-offs land here']
  ];

  var written = 0, skipped = 0, now = new Date();
  accounts.forEach(function (a) {
    if (existing[a[0]]) { skipped++; return; }
    sheet.appendRow([a[0], a[1], a[2], a[3], a[4], 'TRUE', a[5], now]);
    written++;
  });
  Logger.log('CoA seed complete. Written: ' + written + ', Skipped: ' + skipped);
}
File 5 ? Test_Stage_D_01.gs
function test_Stage_D_01() {
  setup_StoreSpreadsheetId();
  setup_SeedChartOfAccounts();

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.CHART_OF_ACCOUNTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  Logger.log('Total accounts: ' + (data.length - 1));

  // Spot-check the critical corrected accounts.
  var found23100 = false, found60300 = false;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (String(data[r][0]) === '23100') found23100 = true;
    if (String(data[r][0]) === '60300') found60300 = true;
  }
  Logger.log('23100 (Member Credit, Liability) present: ' + found23100);
  Logger.log('60300 (Write-Off) present: ' + found60300);
}

File 6 ? QueueReaderService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * QueueReaderService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Reads Part 2's Pending_Transactions directly by Sheet ID ?
 * a queue is data, not an API. This is the one place Part 3
 * reaches into another part's spreadsheet directly, and it only
 * ever reads/updates State ? never touches Part 2's other sheets.
 */
function setup_StorePart2SpreadsheetId() {
  var PART2_ID = '14Ihv8LLC8V5nZ4FSi-omz8osEgr_rGXfTMIDcf0foRk'; // Part 2's real Sheet ID
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('PART2_SPREADSHEET_ID', PART2_ID);
  Logger.log('Part 2 Spreadsheet ID stored.');
}
function _getPart2Queue_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('PART2_SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) throw new Error('PART2_SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StorePart2SpreadsheetId() first.');
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id).getSheetByName('Pending_Transactions');
}

var Q_COLS_ = {
  JOURNAL_ID: 1, CREATED_TS: 2, STATE: 3, SOURCE_TYPE: 4, SOURCE_RECORD_ID: 5,
  FLAT_ID: 6, AMOUNT_PAISE: 7, PAYLOAD_JSON: 8, ACTOR_USER_ID: 9,
  COMPLETED_TS: 10, ERROR_MESSAGE: 11, COMPENSATION_ACTION: 12
};

function _getPendingQueueRows_() {
  var sheet = _getPart2Queue_();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rows = [];
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][Q_COLS_.STATE - 1] === 'PENDING') rows.push({ rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] });
  }
  return rows;
}

function _markQueueRow_(rowIndex, newState, errorMessage) {
  var sheet = _getPart2Queue_();
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, Q_COLS_.STATE).setValue(newState);
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, Q_COLS_.COMPLETED_TS).setValue(new Date());
  if (errorMessage) sheet.getRange(rowIndex, Q_COLS_.ERROR_MESSAGE).setValue(errorMessage);
}
File 7 ? LedgerPostingService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * LedgerPostingService.gs ? implements L-06-style idempotency via
 * Journal_ID, L-14's debit=credit invariant, and posts one queue
 * item at a time so a failure never leaves a partial batch.
 */
var LEDGER_COLS_ = {
  LEDGER_ID: 1, JOURNAL_ID: 2, ENTRY_DATE: 3, FINANCIAL_YEAR: 4, FLAT_ID: 5,
  DEBIT_ACCOUNT: 6, CREDIT_ACCOUNT: 7, AMOUNT_PAISE: 8, SOURCE_TYPE: 9,
  SOURCE_RECORD_ID: 10, DESCRIPTION: 11, REVERSAL_OF: 12, CREATED_TS: 13, CREATED_BY: 14
};

function _alreadyPosted_(journalId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1] === journalId) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/** Writes ONE balanced pair (debit + credit) as two Ledger_Entries rows sharing one Journal_ID. Enforces L-14: amounts must match exactly. */
function _postJournalPair_(journalId, entryDate, fy, flatId, debitAccount, creditAccount, amountPaise, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, actorUserId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var now = new Date();
  var ledgerId1 = 'LED-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var ledgerId2 = 'LED-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();

  sheet.appendRow([ledgerId1, journalId, entryDate, fy, canonicalizeKey(flatId || ''), debitAccount, '', amountPaise, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, '', now, actorUserId]);
  sheet.appendRow([ledgerId2, journalId, entryDate, fy, canonicalizeKey(flatId || ''), '', creditAccount, amountPaise, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, '', now, actorUserId]);
}

/** Maps a queue item's Source_Type to the correct debit/credit account pair. This is the accounting judgment layer. */
function _resolveAccountsForSourceType_(sourceType, payload) {
  switch (sourceType) {
    case 'RECEIPT':
      // Member pays: debit Cash/Bank (11000), credit Maintenance Receivable (13000).
      return { debit: '11000', credit: '13000' };
    case 'CREDIT_CREATED':
      // Surplus becomes a liability: debit Cash (already recorded via RECEIPT), credit Member Credit (23100).
      // NOTE: this is a SEPARATE entry from the RECEIPT entry above ? see the honest gap flagged below.
      return { debit: '13000', credit: '23100' };
    case 'EXPENSE_VOUCHER':
      // Expense incurred: debit the expense account, credit Vendor Payable.
      return { debit: '50900', credit: '21000' }; // 50900 = general bucket; category-specific mapping is a later refinement
    default:
      throw new Error('No account mapping defined for Source_Type: ' + sourceType);
  }
}

/** Processes ALL pending queue items, one at a time, each independently ? one failure doesn't block the rest. */
function processQueue(sessionToken) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'POST_LEDGER_ENTRIES');
  var pending = _getPendingQueueRows_();
  var results = { posted: [], skipped: [], failed: [] };

  pending.forEach(function (item) {
    var journalId = item.values[Q_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1];
    try {
      if (_alreadyPosted_(journalId)) {
        _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'POSTED', 'Already posted ? idempotency guard caught a re-run.');
        results.skipped.push(journalId);
        return;
      }

      var sourceType = item.values[Q_COLS_.SOURCE_TYPE - 1];
      var payload = JSON.parse(item.values[Q_COLS_.PAYLOAD_JSON - 1] || '{}');
      var accounts = _resolveAccountsForSourceType_(sourceType, payload);
      var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_() || 'UNKNOWN'; // Part 3 needs its own copy of this helper too ? see note below

      _postJournalPair_(
        journalId, item.values[Q_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1], fy, item.values[Q_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
        accounts.debit, accounts.credit, item.values[Q_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1],
        sourceType, item.values[Q_COLS_.SOURCE_RECORD_ID - 1],
        'Auto-posted from Part 2 queue', session.userId
      );

      _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'POSTED', '');
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'LEDGER_POSTED', SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES, journalId, sourceType + ' posted', journalId);
      results.posted.push(journalId);
    } catch (e) {
      _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'FAILED', e.message);
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'LEDGER_POST_FAILED', SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES, journalId, e.message, journalId);
      results.failed.push({ journalId: journalId, error: e.message });
    }
  });

  return results;
}
Two honest gaps I'm flagging rather than quietly coding around, since they matter for correctness:
1. getCurrentFinancialYear_() doesn't exist in Part 3 yet ? it's currently only in Part 2. Same duplication pattern as Canonicalize.gs: add this small function to Part 3 directly, don't reach into Part 2 for it.
2. The CREDIT_CREATED mapping above is wrong as written ? it debits Maintenance Receivable again, which double-counts against what the RECEIPT entry already did. The real fix needs the actual amounts from each queue item's payload reconciled against each other, which is genuinely more involved than a single switch-statement mapping. I'd rather stop here and get your read on it than ship an entry that would silently produce an unbalanced book.
Add this to Part 3's Canonicalize.gs file (or a new small file):
function getCurrentFinancialYear_() {
  var now = new Date();
  var year = now.getFullYear();
  var month = now.getMonth() + 1;
  if (month >= 7) return year + '-' + String(year + 1).slice(-2);
  return (year - 1) + '-' + String(year).slice(-2);
}
Before we test processQueue() for real, I want to work through the CREDIT_CREATED accounting properly with you rather than guess. Should the credit's own queue entry debit Cash directly (treating it as if the surplus portion of the payment was a separate cash receipt), rather than debiting Maintenance Receivable again? That would make it: debit 11000 (Cash), credit 23100 (Member Credit) ? cleanly independent of the RECEIPT entry, no double-counting. Does that match your intent?

Good ? let me put on that hat properly and work through the actual accounting logic, not just guess at account codes.
The core principle: every rupee (or paisa) that moves has to land somewhere real. A "receipt with a concession" is really two different things happening at once ? real cash coming in, and a waiver being granted ? and they need to be recorded separately, or your Cash account would show money that never actually arrived.
Receipt without concession (member pays ?950 against a ?950 bill):
* Debit Cash ?950 (money physically came in)
* Credit Maintenance Receivable ?950 (they no longer owe it)
Receipt with a concession (member pays ?500 cash, committee waives ?100, bill was ?600):
* Debit Cash ?500 (only the real cash)
* Debit Approved Concession ?100 (this is a reduction the society absorbed ? not cash, so it needs its own line)
* Credit Maintenance Receivable ?600 (the full bill is now cleared either way)
I caught a real mistake in my first draft here: I'd written a single debit to Cash for cashPaise + concessionPaise combined ? that would overstate your actual cash balance by every concession ever granted. Good thing we stopped to check rather than ship it.
Surplus becoming a credit (member overpays by ?150):
* Debit Cash ?150 (real money came in)
* Credit Member Advance/Credit Payable (23100) ?150 (you now owe it back to them)
This is fully independent of the receipt entries above ? no double-counting, because it only ever touches Cash and the liability account, never Receivable.
Expense voucher approved (?3,000 plumbing expense):
* Debit Expense ?3,000 (cost incurred)
* Credit Vendor Payable ?3,000 (you owe the vendor, haven't paid yet)
One gap I found while designing this: the original Chart of Accounts I gave you was missing the Concession accounts entirely ? they existed in the very first CoA design from months ago but never made it into Part 3's seed. Fixing that now, safe to re-run:
Add to SeedChartOfAccounts.gs, inside the accounts array:
['60100', 'Approved Maintenance Concession', 'OTHER', 'DEBIT', '', 'L-10: concessions granted, tracked separately from cash'],
Run setup_SeedChartOfAccounts() again ? it'll skip the 22 existing rows and add just this one.
Now the corrected posting logic ? this needs to support multi-line journals (not just a simple pair), since a receipt-with-concession is genuinely a 3-line entry:
Replace _postJournalPair_ with this in LedgerPostingService.gs:
/** General multi-line journal poster. Enforces L-14: total debits MUST equal total credits, or it throws before writing anything. */
function _postJournalLines_(journalId, entryDate, fy, flatId, lines, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, actorUserId) {
  var totalDebits = 0, totalCredits = 0;
  lines.forEach(function (l) {
    if (l.side === 'DEBIT') totalDebits += l.amount; else totalCredits += l.amount;
  });
  if (totalDebits !== totalCredits) {
    throw new Error('L-14 VIOLATION: debits (' + totalDebits + ') != credits (' + totalCredits + ') for journal ' + journalId + '. Refusing to post.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var now = new Date();
  lines.forEach(function (l) {
    if (l.amount <= 0) return; // skip zero-amount lines (e.g. no concession given)
    var ledgerId = 'LED-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    var debitAcct = l.side === 'DEBIT' ? l.account : '';
    var creditAcct = l.side === 'CREDIT' ? l.account : '';
    sheet.appendRow([ledgerId, journalId, entryDate, fy, canonicalizeKey(flatId || ''), debitAcct, creditAcct, l.amount, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, '', now, actorUserId]);
  });
}
Replace _resolveAccountsForSourceType_ and the posting call inside processQueue()'s try block with:
function _buildJournalLines_(sourceType, amountPaise, payload) {
  if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT') {
    var cash = Number(payload.cashPaise) || 0;
    var concession = Number(payload.concessionPaise) || 0;
    var lines = [];
    if (cash > 0) lines.push({ account: '11000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: cash });
    if (concession > 0) lines.push({ account: '60100', side: 'DEBIT', amount: concession });
    lines.push({ account: '13000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: cash + concession });
    return lines;
  }
  if (sourceType === 'CREDIT_CREATED') {
    return [
      { account: '11000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: amountPaise },
      { account: '23100', side: 'CREDIT', amount: amountPaise }
    ];
  }
  if (sourceType === 'EXPENSE_VOUCHER') {
    // KNOWN LIMITATION: no GST split yet (payload lacks it), and every
    // expense posts to the same general bucket (50900) regardless of
    // category ? category-specific mapping is a real future refinement,
    // not something to fake now.
    return [
      { account: '50900', side: 'DEBIT', amount: amountPaise },
      { account: '21000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: amountPaise }
    ];
  }
  throw new Error('No journal-line mapping defined for Source_Type: ' + sourceType);
}
And inside processQueue(), replace the _postJournalPair_(...) call with:
var lines = _buildJournalLines_(sourceType, Number(item.values[Q_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]), payload);

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * Test_Stage_D_03.gs ? L-14 sanity check: total debits must equal total credits, always.
 */
function test_Stage_D_03_balance_check() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var totalDebits = 0, totalCredits = 0;
  var rowCount = 0;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    var debitAccount = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.DEBIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var creditAccount = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.CREDIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var amount = Number(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]) || 0;
    if (debitAccount) totalDebits += amount;
    if (creditAccount) totalCredits += amount;
    rowCount++;
  }

  Logger.log('Total ledger rows: ' + rowCount);
  Logger.log('Total Debits: ' + totalDebits + ' paise');
  Logger.log('Total Credits: ' + totalCredits + ' paise');
  Logger.log('Balanced: ' + (totalDebits === totalCredits));
}

Before building Member_Balance_Summary, I need to flag something real that surfaced while designing it ? not something to quietly work around.
The gap: Member_Balance_Summary is supposed to be rebuildable purely from Ledger_Entries (L-17). But checking what's actually in the ledger right now: only receipts and expense vouchers ever get enqueued to Part 2's queue. Bill generation never does. That means when a bill is created, nothing debits Maintenance Receivable (13000) or credits Income (40100/40300) ? the ledger only shows cash coming in against a receivable that, on the books, was never actually created. Right now the ledger is silently incomplete on the billing side. This isn't a summary-feature problem ? it's a real accounting completeness gap that needs fixing before I build anything that depends on the ledger being trustworthy.
The fix ? two small, deliberate amendments, not a redesign:

enqueueTransaction_('BILL_GENERATED', billId, flatId, baseCharge + lateFine, {
  billId: billId, baseCharge: baseCharge, lateFine: lateFine
}, session.userId, 'REVERSE_BILL_' + billId);

if (sourceType === 'BILL_GENERATED') {
  var base = Number(payload.baseCharge) || 0;
  var fine = Number(payload.lateFine) || 0;
  var lines = [];
  lines.push({ account: '13000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: base + fine }); // new receivable created
  if (base > 0) lines.push({ account: '40100', side: 'CREDIT', amount: base });   // maintenance income
  if (fine > 0) lines.push({ account: '40300', side: 'CREDIT', amount: fine });   // late fine income
  return lines;
}

File ? MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs ? L-17 CQRS read-model.
 * Always rebuildable from Ledger_Entries; never hand-edited.
 */
var SUMMARY_COLS_ = {
  FLAT_ID: 1, TOTAL_BILLED: 2, TOTAL_PAID: 3, OUTSTANDING: 4,
  CREDIT_BALANCE: 5, LAST_PAYMENT_DATE: 6, LAST_RECONCILED_TS: 7
};

/** Recomputes ONE flat's summary purely from Ledger_Entries ? the whole point of CQRS. */
function _computeFlatBalance_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);

  var totalBilled = 0, totalPaid = 0, creditIssued = 0, creditApplied = 0, lastPaymentDate = null;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) !== target) continue;

    var debitAcct = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.DEBIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var creditAcct = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.CREDIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var amount = Number(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]) || 0;
    var sourceType = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.SOURCE_TYPE - 1];

    if (debitAcct === '13000') totalBilled += amount;   // receivable created (billing)
    if (creditAcct === '13000') {                        // receivable cleared (payment/concession)
      totalPaid += amount;
      if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT') lastPaymentDate = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.ENTRY_DATE - 1];
    }
    if (creditAcct === '23100') creditIssued += amount;   // credit created
    if (debitAcct === '23100') creditApplied += amount;   // credit consumed (future: refund/application)
  }

  return {
    totalBilled: totalBilled,
    totalPaid: totalPaid,
    outstanding: totalBilled - totalPaid,
    creditBalance: creditIssued - creditApplied,
    lastPaymentDate: lastPaymentDate
  };
}

/** Rebuilds the summary row for one flat and writes it ? this is the "cache refresh" half of CQRS. */
function rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, flatId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_LEDGER'); // reuses an existing seeded permission
  var balance = _computeFlatBalance_(flatId);

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_BALANCE_SUMMARY);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  var now = new Date();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][SUMMARY_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target) {
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_BILLED).setValue(balance.totalBilled);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_PAID).setValue(balance.totalPaid);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.OUTSTANDING).setValue(balance.outstanding);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.CREDIT_BALANCE).setValue(balance.creditBalance);
      if (balance.lastPaymentDate) sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_PAYMENT_DATE).setValue(balance.lastPaymentDate);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_RECONCILED_TS).setValue(now);
      return balance;
    }
  }
  // No existing row ? create one.
  sheet.appendRow([canonicalizeKey(flatId), balance.totalBilled, balance.totalPaid, balance.outstanding, balance.creditBalance, balance.lastPaymentDate || '', now]);
  return balance;
}

Confirmed ? JRN-5E1706F6 is FAILED, exactly as suspected. This is a real, permanent gap, not a one-off ? any future posting failure (a typo, a temporary Sheets error, anything) would leave an item stuck in FAILED forever with no way back into the pipeline. Worth fixing properly now rather than patching around it once and hitting it again later.
Fix ? add a retry function to Part 3's LedgerPostingService.gs:
/**
 * Moves a specific FAILED queue item back to PENDING so processQueue()
 * will pick it up again. Deliberately manual/explicit ? a failure
 * should be looked at and understood, not silently auto-retried
 * forever (that risks masking a real, recurring problem).
 */
function retryFailedQueueItem(sessionToken, journalId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'POST_LEDGER_ENTRIES');
  var sheet = _getPart2Queue_();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][Q_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1] === journalId) {
      var currentState = data[r][Q_COLS_.STATE - 1];
      if (currentState !== 'FAILED') {
        throw new Error('Journal ' + journalId + ' is not in FAILED state (currently: ' + currentState + '). Only FAILED items can be retried.');
      }
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, Q_COLS_.STATE).setValue('PENDING');
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, Q_COLS_.ERROR_MESSAGE).setValue('');
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'QUEUE_ITEM_RETRIED', 'Pending_Transactions', journalId, 'Manually reset from FAILED to PENDING', journalId);
      return { journalId: journalId, newState: 'PENDING' };
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Journal ' + journalId + ' not found in queue.');
}

Found it ? and it's an easy mistake to make when patching a large file via chat instructions. The BILL_GENERATED block got inserted into processQueue(), not into _buildJournalLines_() where it belongs. Look closely: it references sourceType and payload before those variables are even declared (they're declared further down with var), and it says return lines; ? but that's inside a forEach callback, so return just skips to the next queue item silently. It never throws, never does anything ? it's dead code sitting in the wrong function, which is exactly why _buildJournalLines_() still has no BILL_GENERATED case and the same error kept coming back.
Here's the fully corrected file ? replace the entire contents of LedgerPostingService.gs with this:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * LedgerPostingService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Consumes Part 2's Pending_Transactions queue and posts balanced
 * double-entry journals to Ledger_Entries. Implements:
 *   - L-14: total debits MUST equal total credits, enforced before
 *     a single row is written ? never a partial/unbalanced post.
 *   - Idempotency via Journal_ID: a queue item already posted is
 *     never posted twice, even if this function runs more than once
 *     on the same pending row (e.g. a retried trigger).
 *   - One queue item at a time: a single failure never blocks or
 *     corrupts the processing of any other item in the batch.
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, GatewayLink.gs,
 *             QueueReaderService.gs, getCurrentFinancialYear_()
 */

var LEDGER_COLS_ = {
  LEDGER_ID: 1, JOURNAL_ID: 2, ENTRY_DATE: 3, FINANCIAL_YEAR: 4, FLAT_ID: 5,
  DEBIT_ACCOUNT: 6, CREDIT_ACCOUNT: 7, AMOUNT_PAISE: 8, SOURCE_TYPE: 9,
  SOURCE_RECORD_ID: 10, DESCRIPTION: 11, REVERSAL_OF: 12, CREATED_TS: 13, CREATED_BY: 14
};

/** L-06-style idempotency guard: has this Journal_ID already produced ledger rows? */
function _alreadyPosted_(journalId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1] === journalId) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * General multi-line journal poster. lines = [{account, side: 'DEBIT'|'CREDIT', amount}, ...]
 * Refuses to write ANYTHING if debits != credits (L-14) ? checked
 * fully before the first appendRow, so a rejected journal leaves
 * zero trace in Ledger_Entries, never a half-written one.
 */
function _postJournalLines_(journalId, entryDate, fy, flatId, lines, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, actorUserId) {
  var totalDebits = 0, totalCredits = 0;
  lines.forEach(function (l) {
    if (l.side === 'DEBIT') totalDebits += l.amount; else totalCredits += l.amount;
  });
  if (totalDebits !== totalCredits) {
    throw new Error('L-14 VIOLATION: debits (' + totalDebits + ') != credits (' + totalCredits + ') for journal ' + journalId + '. Refusing to post.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var now = new Date();
  lines.forEach(function (l) {
    if (!l.amount || l.amount <= 0) return; // skip zero-amount lines (e.g. no concession given)
    var ledgerId = 'LED-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
    var debitAcct = l.side === 'DEBIT' ? l.account : '';
    var creditAcct = l.side === 'CREDIT' ? l.account : '';
    sheet.appendRow([
      ledgerId, journalId, entryDate, fy, canonicalizeKey(flatId || ''),
      debitAcct, creditAcct, l.amount, sourceType, sourceRecordId, description, '', now, actorUserId
    ]);
  });
}

/**
 * Accounting judgment layer ? maps a queue item's Source_Type and
 * payload into a balanced set of journal lines. This is the ONE
 * place that decides what accounting treatment each event gets.
 */
function _buildJournalLines_(sourceType, amountPaise, payload) {
  if (sourceType === 'BILL_GENERATED') {
    var base = Number(payload.baseCharge) || 0;
    var fine = Number(payload.lateFine) || 0;
    var lines = [];
    lines.push({ account: '13000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: base + fine }); // new receivable created
    if (base > 0) lines.push({ account: '40100', side: 'CREDIT', amount: base });   // maintenance income
    if (fine > 0) lines.push({ account: '40300', side: 'CREDIT', amount: fine });   // late fine income
    return lines;
  }

  if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT') {
    var cash = Number(payload.cashPaise) || 0;
    var concession = Number(payload.concessionPaise) || 0;
    var lines = [];
    if (cash > 0) lines.push({ account: '11000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: cash });        // real cash received
    if (concession > 0) lines.push({ account: '60100', side: 'DEBIT', amount: concession }); // waived amount, tracked separately
    lines.push({ account: '13000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: cash + concession });        // full bill amount cleared
    return lines;
  }

  if (sourceType === 'CREDIT_CREATED') {
    // Surplus becomes a liability owed back to the member ? independent
    // of the RECEIPT entry above, so no double-counting of cash.
    return [
      { account: '11000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: amountPaise },
      { account: '23100', side: 'CREDIT', amount: amountPaise }
    ];
  }

  if (sourceType === 'EXPENSE_VOUCHER') {
    // KNOWN LIMITATION (flagged, not hidden): no GST split yet ? payload
    // doesn't carry it ? and every expense posts to the general bucket
    // (50900) regardless of Category. Category-specific account mapping
    // and GST handling are real future refinements, not faked here.
    return [
      { account: '50900', side: 'DEBIT', amount: amountPaise },
      { account: '21000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: amountPaise }
    ];
  }

  throw new Error('No journal-line mapping defined for Source_Type: ' + sourceType);
}

/**
 * Processes ALL pending queue items from Part 2, one at a time.
 * A failure on one item is logged and marked FAILED ? it does not
 * stop or corrupt processing of the remaining items (L-16's spirit:
 * failures are isolated and visible, never silent or contagious).
 */
function processQueue(sessionToken) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'POST_LEDGER_ENTRIES');
  var pending = _getPendingQueueRows_();
  var results = { posted: [], skipped: [], failed: [] };

  pending.forEach(function (item) {
    var journalId = item.values[Q_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1];
    try {
      if (_alreadyPosted_(journalId)) {
        _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'POSTED', 'Already posted ? idempotency guard caught a re-run.');
        results.skipped.push(journalId);
        return;
      }

      var sourceType = item.values[Q_COLS_.SOURCE_TYPE - 1];
      var amountPaise = Number(item.values[Q_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]);
      var payload = JSON.parse(item.values[Q_COLS_.PAYLOAD_JSON - 1] || '{}');
      var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();

      var lines = _buildJournalLines_(sourceType, amountPaise, payload);

      _postJournalLines_(
        journalId, item.values[Q_COLS_.CREATED_TS - 1], fy, item.values[Q_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
        lines, sourceType, item.values[Q_COLS_.SOURCE_RECORD_ID - 1],
        'Auto-posted from Part 2 queue', session.userId
      );

      _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'POSTED', '');
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'LEDGER_POSTED', SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES, journalId, sourceType + ' posted, ' + amountPaise + ' paise', journalId);
      results.posted.push(journalId);
    } catch (e) {
      _markQueueRow_(item.rowIndex, 'FAILED', e.message);
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'LEDGER_POST_FAILED', SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES, journalId, e.message, journalId);
      results.failed.push({ journalId: journalId, error: e.message });
    }
  });

  return results;
}

/**
 * Moves a specific FAILED queue item back to PENDING so processQueue()
 * will pick it up again. Deliberately manual/explicit ? a failure
 * should be looked at and understood, not silently auto-retried
 * forever (that risks masking a real, recurring problem).
 */
function retryFailedQueueItem(sessionToken, journalId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'POST_LEDGER_ENTRIES');
  var sheet = _getPart2Queue_();
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][Q_COLS_.JOURNAL_ID - 1] === journalId) {
      var currentState = data[r][Q_COLS_.STATE - 1];
      if (currentState !== 'FAILED') {
        throw new Error('Journal ' + journalId + ' is not in FAILED state (currently: ' + currentState + '). Only FAILED items can be retried.');
      }
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, Q_COLS_.STATE).setValue('PENDING');
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, Q_COLS_.ERROR_MESSAGE).setValue('');
      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'QUEUE_ITEM_RETRIED', 'Pending_Transactions', journalId, 'Manually reset from FAILED to PENDING', journalId);
      return { journalId: journalId, newState: 'PENDING' };
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Journal ' + journalId + ' not found in queue.');
}

function test_Stage_D_05c_tracedebug() {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey('GA-3D');

  Logger.log('LEDGER_COLS_ actually resolves to: ' + JSON.stringify(LEDGER_COLS_));

  var totalBilled = 0, totalPaid = 0;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    var rowFlatId = canonicalizeKey(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]);
    if (rowFlatId !== target) continue;

    var debitAcct = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.DEBIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var creditAcct = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.CREDIT_ACCOUNT - 1];
    var amount = Number(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]) || 0;

      totalBilled += amount;
      Logger.log('  -> matched DEBIT 13000, totalBilled now ' + totalBilled);
    }
    if (creditAcct === '13000') {
      totalPaid += amount;
      Logger.log('  -> matched CREDIT 13000, totalPaid now ' + totalPaid);
    }
  }

}

Understood ? here's the complete file, not just the one function. Replace the entire contents of MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs with this:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs ? L-17 CQRS read-model.
 * Always rebuildable from Ledger_Entries; never hand-edited.
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * BUGFIX (this version): account code comparisons now force
 * String() on both sides before comparing. Google Sheets can
 * return a cell like 13000 as a JS Number, not a String, so
 * '13000' === 13000 silently evaluates to false and every
 * comparison failed without throwing any error.
 */
var SUMMARY_COLS_ = {
  FLAT_ID: 1, TOTAL_BILLED: 2, TOTAL_PAID: 3, OUTSTANDING: 4,
  CREDIT_BALANCE: 5, LAST_PAYMENT_DATE: 6, LAST_RECONCILED_TS: 7
};

/** Recomputes ONE flat's summary purely from Ledger_Entries ? the whole point of CQRS. */
function _computeFlatBalance_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);

  var totalBilled = 0, totalPaid = 0, creditIssued = 0, creditApplied = 0, lastPaymentDate = null;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) !== target) continue;

    var debitAcct = String(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.DEBIT_ACCOUNT - 1]);
    var creditAcct = String(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.CREDIT_ACCOUNT - 1]);
    var amount = Number(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]) || 0;
    var sourceType = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.SOURCE_TYPE - 1];

    if (debitAcct === '13000') totalBilled += amount;   // receivable created (billing)
    if (creditAcct === '13000') {                        // receivable cleared (payment/concession)
      totalPaid += amount;
      if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT') lastPaymentDate = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.ENTRY_DATE - 1];
    }
    if (creditAcct === '23100') creditIssued += amount;   // credit created
    if (debitAcct === '23100') creditApplied += amount;   // credit consumed (future: refund/application)
  }

  return {
    totalBilled: totalBilled,
    totalPaid: totalPaid,
    outstanding: totalBilled - totalPaid,
    creditBalance: creditIssued - creditApplied,
    lastPaymentDate: lastPaymentDate
  };
}

/** Rebuilds the summary row for one flat and writes it ? this is the "cache refresh" half of CQRS. */
function rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, flatId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_LEDGER'); // reuses an existing seeded permission
  var balance = _computeFlatBalance_(flatId);

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_BALANCE_SUMMARY);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  var now = new Date();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][SUMMARY_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target) {
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_BILLED).setValue(balance.totalBilled);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_PAID).setValue(balance.totalPaid);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.OUTSTANDING).setValue(balance.outstanding);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.CREDIT_BALANCE).setValue(balance.creditBalance);
      if (balance.lastPaymentDate) sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_PAYMENT_DATE).setValue(balance.lastPaymentDate);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_RECONCILED_TS).setValue(now);
      return balance;
    }
  }
  // No existing row ? create one.
  sheet.appendRow([canonicalizeKey(flatId), balance.totalBilled, balance.totalPaid, balance.outstanding, balance.creditBalance, balance.lastPaymentDate || '', now]);
  return balance;
}
One more thing worth doing before you run this ? since we now know Sheets can silently return account codes as Numbers, LedgerPostingService.gs's _buildJournalLines_() should be double-checked too, even though it only writes codes rather than comparing them. It's not broken, but for consistency and to close this off properly: is Account_Code in Chart_of_Accounts itself stored as text or as a number? If it's a number there too, that's fine for this system since nothing currently does account-code arithmetic ? but worth confirming so we know definitively rather than assuming.

Confirmed ? Account_Code is right-aligned, so it's stored as a Number, same as the Debit_Account_Code/Credit_Account_Code columns in Ledger_Entries that just bit us. Good that we checked directly rather than assumed; this closes the loop on that investigation properly.
Practical implication for the future, worth stating clearly: any code that reads Chart_of_Accounts.Account_Code and compares it against a string literal (e.g., '13000') will have the exact same silent-false-match bug we just fixed in MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs. Nothing in Part 3 currently does this ? _buildJournalLines_() only writes codes, never compares them ? so there's no live bug here today. But it's now a known, documented trap for anything built later (e.g., a future category-to-account mapping feature, or a report that filters by account code) ? always String() it first.

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 4: SERVICE DIRECTORY & COMPLAINTS
 * Bootstrap.gs
 */
function setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() {
  var SPREADSHEET_ID = '1JdaBFqRRjqHxwj-d8wYXK43N_4f-T_BG_hYi6AJGDOc';
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID', SPREADSHEET_ID);
  Logger.log('Spreadsheet ID stored in Script Properties.');
}
function getSS_() {
  var id = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty('SPREADSHEET_ID');
  if (!id) throw new Error('SPREADSHEET_ID not set. Run setup_StoreSpreadsheetId() once first.');
  return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);
}
var SHEETS_ = {
  COMPLAINTS: 'Complaints',
  SERVICE_DIRECTORY: 'Service_Directory',
  NOTICES_CIRCULARS: 'Notices_Circulars'
};
function getSheet_(sheetName) {
  var sheet = getSS_().getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Sheet not found: ' + sheetName + '. Schema drift? Check L-20.');
  return sheet;
}
File 2 ? Canonicalize.gs (same as every prior part, duplicated on purpose)
function _normalizeWhitespace(value) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  return String(value).trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
}
function canonicalizeKey(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
}
function canonicalizeName(value) {
  var v = _normalizeWhitespace(value);
  if (v === '') return '';
  return v.replace(/\w\S*/g, function (word) { return word.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + word.substr(1).toLowerCase(); });
}
File 3 ? GatewayLink.gs ? add Part 0 as a library here too, identifier Gateway.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 4: SERVICE DIRECTORY & COMPLAINTS
 * GatewayLink.gs
 */
function verifySession_(token) { return Gateway.verifySessionToken(token); }
function requirePermission_(actorRole, permissionKey) {
  if (!Gateway.hasPermission(actorRole, permissionKey, 'PART4')) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: role "' + actorRole + '" lacks permission "' + permissionKey + '" in PART4.');
  }
}
function _authorize_(sessionToken, permissionKey) {
  var session = verifySession_(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(session.role, permissionKey);
  return session;
}
function logAudit_(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId) {
  Gateway.logAuditFromOtherPart(actorUserId, actorRole, actionType, 'PART4', targetSheet, recordKey, changeDetails, correlationId, '');
}

File 4 ? ComplaintService.gs
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 4: SERVICE DIRECTORY & COMPLAINTS
 * ComplaintService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Deliberately simple compared to Parts 2/3: no double-entry, no
 * sequence counters, no maker-checker ? this part just needs
 * reliable status tracking and RBAC-gated visibility.
 */
var COMPLAINT_COLS_ = {
  COMPLAINT_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, RAISED_BY: 3, CATEGORY: 4, TITLE: 5, DESCRIPTION: 6,
  PRIORITY: 7, PHOTO_URL: 8, STATUS: 9, ASSIGNED_TO: 10, ADMIN_NOTES: 11,
  CLOSED_BY: 12, CLOSURE_TYPE: 13, CLOSURE_REMARKS: 14,
  RAISED_TS: 15, LAST_UPDATED_TS: 16, CLOSED_TS: 17
};

/** Any authenticated user can raise a complaint for their own flat ? this is intentionally the most open permission in this part. */
function raiseComplaint(sessionToken, complaintData) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'RAISE_COMPLAINT');
  // complaintData: {flatId, category, title, description, priority, photoUrl}

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  var complaintId = 'CMP-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    complaintId, canonicalizeKey(complaintData.flatId), session.userId,
    canonicalizeKey(complaintData.category || 'GENERAL'), complaintData.title || '', complaintData.description || '',
    canonicalizeKey(complaintData.priority || 'NORMAL'), complaintData.photoUrl || '',
    'OPEN', '', '', '', '', '', now, now, ''
  ]);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'COMPLAINT_RAISED', SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS, complaintId, complaintData.title || '', '');
  return { complaintId: complaintId };
}

function _findComplaintRow_(complaintId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.COMPLAINT_ID - 1] === complaintId) return { rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] };
  }
  return null;
}

/** Admin/Committee assigns a complaint and can add internal notes. Status moves OPEN -> IN_PROGRESS. */
function assignComplaint(sessionToken, complaintId, assignedToUserId, adminNotes) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_COMPLAINTS');
  var found = _findComplaintRow_(complaintId);
  if (!found) throw new Error('Complaint ' + complaintId + ' not found.');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.ASSIGNED_TO).setValue(assignedToUserId);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.ADMIN_NOTES).setValue(adminNotes || '');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS).setValue('IN_PROGRESS');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.LAST_UPDATED_TS).setValue(new Date());

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'COMPLAINT_ASSIGNED', SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS, complaintId, 'Assigned to ' + assignedToUserId, '');
  return { complaintId: complaintId, status: 'IN_PROGRESS' };
}

/** Closes a complaint. closureType: RESOLVED | DUPLICATE | NOT_ACTIONABLE | WITHDRAWN */
function closeComplaint(sessionToken, complaintId, closureType, closureRemarks) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_COMPLAINTS');
  var found = _findComplaintRow_(complaintId);
  if (!found) throw new Error('Complaint ' + complaintId + ' not found.');

  var currentStatus = found.values[COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS - 1];
  if (currentStatus === 'CLOSED') throw new Error('Complaint ' + complaintId + ' is already closed.');

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  var now = new Date();
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS).setValue('CLOSED');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSED_BY).setValue(session.userId);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSURE_TYPE).setValue(canonicalizeKey(closureType));
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSURE_REMARKS).setValue(closureRemarks || '');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.LAST_UPDATED_TS).setValue(now);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSED_TS).setValue(now);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'COMPLAINT_CLOSED', SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS, complaintId, canonicalizeKey(closureType), '');
  return { complaintId: complaintId, status: 'CLOSED' };
}
File 5 ? Test_Stage_E_01.gs
function test_Stage_E_01_complaint() {
  var session = Gateway.login('9123602715', 'YourRealAdmin1Password');

  var complaint = raiseComplaint(session.token, {
    flatId: 'GA-3D', category: 'PLUMBING', title: 'Leaking tap in common bathroom',
    description: 'Tap on 3rd floor common washroom has been leaking for 2 days.', priority: 'NORMAL'
  });
  Logger.log('Complaint raised: ' + complaint.complaintId);

  var assigned = assignComplaint(session.token, complaint.complaintId, 'U-3CE8B916', 'Assigning to plumber vendor.');
  Logger.log('Assigned: ' + JSON.stringify(assigned));

  var closed = closeComplaint(session.token, complaint.complaintId, 'RESOLVED', 'Plumber fixed the tap on 30-Aug-2026.');
  Logger.log('Closed: ' + JSON.stringify(closed));

  // Prove the guard: closing an already-closed complaint must fail.
  try {
    closeComplaint(session.token, complaint.complaintId, 'RESOLVED', 'Trying again');
    Logger.log('ERROR: double-close was allowed ? this should not happen.');
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Correctly refused: ' + e.message);
  }
}

File ? UserManagementService.gs (new, in Part 0's project)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * UserManagementService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Resolves the standing gap: AdminBootstrap.gs only ever creates
 * the initial 2 Admins. This is the general-purpose, ongoing
 * user-creation path for Committee/Resident/CA accounts ? and
 * for additional Admins beyond the original two, if ever needed.
 * Reuses the same temp-password-shown-once pattern as
 * AdminBootstrap.gs and BreakGlassRecovery.gs, for consistency.
 * Depends on: Bootstrap.gs, Canonicalize.gs, RbacService.gs,
 *             PasswordService.gs, AuditService.gs
 */

/**
 * roleName: ADMIN | COMMITTEE | RESIDENT | CA
 * userData: {flatId, fullName, mobileNumber, emailAddress}
 * flatId is required for RESIDENT (ties them to a unit), optional
 * for COMMITTEE/CA/ADMIN (they may not personally own a flat).
 */
function createUser(sessionToken, roleName, userData) {
  var session = verifySessionToken(sessionToken);
  if (!session) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(session.role, 'MANAGE_USERS'); // already seeded to ADMIN, scope ALL, in Stage A

  var role = canonicalizeKey(roleName);
  var validRoles = ['ADMIN', 'COMMITTEE', 'RESIDENT', 'CA'];
  if (validRoles.indexOf(role) === -1) {
    throw new Error('Invalid role "' + roleName + '". Must be one of: ' + validRoles.join(', '));
  }
  if (role === 'RESIDENT' && !userData.flatId) {
    throw new Error('flatId is required when creating a RESIDENT user.');
  }
  if (!userData.mobileNumber || !userData.emailAddress) {
    throw new Error('mobileNumber and emailAddress are both required (L-01 login, L-36 OTP delivery).');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.USERS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var targetMobile = canonicalizeMobile(userData.mobileNumber);
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeMobile(data[r][USERS_COLS_.MOBILE - 1]) === targetMobile) {
      throw new Error('A user with mobile number ' + userData.mobileNumber + ' already exists.');
    }
  }

  var userId = 'U-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  var salt = generateSalt_();
  var tempPassword = Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8);
  var hash = hashPassword_(tempPassword, salt);
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.appendRow([
    userId, canonicalizeKey(userData.flatId || ''), canonicalizeName(userData.fullName || ''),
    canonicalizeMobile(userData.mobileNumber), userData.emailAddress, role, 'ACTIVE',
    hash, salt, 'TRUE',    // Force_Password_Reset ? must change on first login, same as bootstrap admins
    0, '',                 // Failed_Login_Count, Locked_Until
    '', '',                // Active_Session_Token, Session_Issued_At
    '', '',                // Last_Activity_Timestamp, Last_Login_Timestamp
    now, session.userId, now
  ]);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'USER_CREATED', SHEETS_.USERS, userId, 'Role: ' + role + ', Flat: ' + (userData.flatId || 'N/A'), '');

  Logger.log('Created user ' + userId + ' (' + role + ', ' + userData.mobileNumber + '). ONE-TIME temp password: ' + tempPassword + ' ? relay this securely, it will not be shown again.');
  return { userId: userId, role: role };
}
