Run this in Part 0's project, share the log, and once confirmed ? the entire backend is genuinely complete and multi-role-ready. That's the moment to update memory and move into Stage F: Frontend SPA.
Before writing any HTML, there's one architectural decision that needs to be made explicit ? it directly determines how every screen gets built, and getting it wrong now would mean redoing all of Stage F later.
The gap: nothing is actually reachable from a browser yet.
Everything we've built so far talks to itself via Apps Script's internal execution (the Run button) or cross-project library calls (Gateway.login(...)). Both of those only work inside the Apps Script environment. A real webpage running in someone's browser can't call a library function directly ? it needs each part deployed as its own Web App, with a doPost(e) entry point that the browser reaches over plain HTTP.
So Stage F actually has two layers, not one:
1. A thin API router in each of the 5 parts ? a doPost(e) function that takes a JSON request like {action: 'login', mobileNumber: '...', password: '...'}, calls the already-proven internal function, and returns the result as JSON. This is new code, but it's a thin wrapper ? it doesn't touch any of the tested business logic underneath, just exposes it safely to the outside world.
2. The actual SPA ? one HTML/CSS/JS page, hosted from its own minimal Web App deployment, calling each part's router via fetch().
Starting with Part 0's router first, since nothing else works without login existing.
File ? WebApp.gs (new, in Part 0's project)
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * WebApp.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * The ONLY new surface exposed to the outside world. Routes a
 * JSON action to the already-proven internal function and returns
 * JSON. Adds NO new business logic ? every function called here
 * is one that's already tested end-to-end via the Run button.
 */
function doPost(e) {
  var response = { success: false, error: 'Unknown error' };
  try {
    var request = JSON.parse(e.postData.contents);
    var action = request.action;

    if (action === 'login') {
      var result = login(request.mobileNumber, request.password);
      response = { success: true, data: result };
    } else if (action === 'verifySession') {
      var session = verifySessionToken(request.token);
      response = { success: true, data: session };
    } else if (action === 'changePassword') {
      var result2 = changePassword(request.token, request.oldPassword, request.newPassword);
      response = { success: true, data: result2 };
    } else {
      response = { success: false, error: 'Unknown action: ' + action };
    }
  } catch (err) {
    response = { success: false, error: err.message };
  }
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify(response)).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}

/** Simple reachability check ? visiting the Web App URL directly in a browser should show this, proving the deployment itself works before any real login is attempted. */
function doGet(e) {
  return ContentService.createTextOutput(JSON.stringify({ status: 'Ranikuthi V5 Gateway is reachable.' })).setMimeType(ContentService.MimeType.JSON);
}
Deployment steps, exact and in order:
1. In Part 0's Apps Script editor: Deploy ? New deployment
2. Type: Web app
3. Execute as: Me
4. Who has access: Anyone (this is what allows an unauthenticated browser to reach doPost at all ? the actual security still comes from login()'s own password check inside, nothing is exposed by this choice alone)
5. Click Deploy, then copy the Web App URL it gives you ? it'll look like https://script.google.com/macros/s/XXXXX/exec
First test ? paste that URL directly into a browser's address bar. You should see the doGet JSON response ({"status":"Ranikuthi V5 Gateway is reachable."}). Share that result before we go any further ? this confirms the deployment itself works before we test an actual login through it.

Confirmed ? Part 0's Gateway is now genuinely reachable from a browser, not just from inside Apps Script. That's the first real step outside the sandbox we've been testing in all along.
Next: prove doPost actually works too, with a real login. Browsers can't easily send a POST request just by visiting a URL, so we need a small test page ? this also happens to be the very first fragment of the actual frontend, not just a throwaway test.
File ? a new HTML file, e.g. test.html, in Part 0's Apps Script project (Files ? + ? HTML)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head><title>Ranikuthi V5 ? Gateway Reachability Test</title></head>
<body>
  <h3>Gateway POST Test</h3>

      document.getElementById('output').textContent = 'Sending request...';

        method: 'POST',

          action: 'login',

Good ? this reuses everything already proven and avoids fighting the platform. Here's the shift, concretely.
Conceptual change: Part 0 becomes the single relay Part 0's own Web App serves the SPA and answers all its calls, either directly (for its own functions like login) or by relaying to the other four parts as library calls (exactly the same mechanism Parts 1?4 already use to call Gateway ? just now in the opposite direction).
Step 1 ? Add Parts 1?4 as libraries inside Part 0's project, same "Add a library" process as before, just reversed:
* Part 1's Script ID ? identifier Members
* Part 2's Script ID ? identifier Billing
* Part 3's Script ID ? identifier Accounting
* Part 4's Script ID ? identifier Complaints
We don't need all four working today ? just add Members for now, since that's what today's test needs. The rest can be added when we actually build screens that need them.
Step 2 ? Replace WebApp.gs entirely. doPost is no longer needed at all ? google.script.run doesn't go through HTTP the way fetch does, so the whole POST-routing approach is being retired, not patched.
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 0: GATEWAY / IDENTITY
 * WebApp.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Serves the SPA and exposes server-side functions for the SPA's
 * google.script.run calls. This REPLACES the earlier doPost/doGet
 * fetch-based approach, which hits browser sandbox restrictions
 * when calling a DIFFERENT Apps Script project's Web App URL.
 * Part 0 is now the single relay: the browser only ever talks to
 * THIS project; Part 0 relays to Parts 1-4 via library calls
 * (server-to-server, no sandbox issue ? same mechanism already
 * proven throughout Stages B-E).
 */
function doGet(e) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('index')
    .setTitle('Ranikuthi Flat Owners\' Association')
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1');
}

/** Client-callable wrapper for login. Never call login() directly from the client ? always through a named wrapper like this, so the client-facing API surface is explicit and auditable. */
function clientLogin(mobileNumber, password) {
  return login(mobileNumber, password);
}

/** Client-callable wrapper for session verification. */
function clientVerifySession(token) {
  return verifySessionToken(token);
}

/** Client-callable wrapper for password change. */
function clientChangePassword(token, oldPassword, newPassword) {
  return changePassword(token, oldPassword, newPassword);
}

    function testLogin() {
      document.getElementById('output').textContent = 'Sending request...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('output').textContent = JSON.stringify(result, null, 2);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('output').textContent = 'SERVER ERROR: ' + error.message;

        .clientLogin('9123602715', 'YourRealAdmin1Password');

File ? index.html (full replacement)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
  <base target="_top">
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Ranikuthi FOA</title>
  <style>
    body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; max-width: 400px; margin: 60px auto; padding: 0 20px; }
    h2 { text-align: center; }
    input { width: 100%; padding: 10px; margin: 8px 0; box-sizing: border-box; font-size: 16px; }
    button { width: 100%; padding: 12px; margin-top: 12px; font-size: 16px; background: #1a73e8; color: white; border: none; cursor: pointer; }
    button:disabled { background: #999; cursor: not-allowed; }
    #errorMsg { color: #c0392b; margin-top: 10px; text-align: center; }
    #dashboard { display: none; text-align: center; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>

  <div id="loginScreen">
    <h2>Ranikuthi Flat Owners' Association</h2>
    <input type="tel" id="mobileInput" placeholder="Mobile Number" maxlength="10">
    <input type="password" id="passwordInput" placeholder="Password">
    <button id="loginBtn" onclick="doLogin()">Log In</button>
    <div id="errorMsg"></div>
  </div>

  <div id="dashboard">
    <h2>Welcome</h2>
    <p id="welcomeText"></p>
    <button onclick="doLogout()">Log Out</button>
  </div>

  <script>
    // Session is held only in memory (a JS variable), not localStorage/sessionStorage ?
    // Apps Script Web Apps don't reliably persist browser storage across script.google.com
    // redirects, and L-03's session model is server-side anyway (Users sheet), so the
    // client only needs to hold the token for the current page life.
    var currentSession = null;

    function setError(msg) {
      document.getElementById('errorMsg').textContent = msg;
    }

    function doLogin() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('mobileInput').value.trim();
      var password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
      setError('');

      if (!mobile || !password) {
        setError('Please enter both mobile number and password.');
        return;
      }

      var btn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Logging in...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (session) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          currentSession = session;

          if (session.forcePasswordReset) {
            // L-36: a forced reset must happen before anything else ? no dashboard access yet.
            setError('Your password must be changed before continuing. (Password-change screen: next to be built.)');
            return;
          }

          showDashboard(session);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          setError(error.message);
        })
        .clientLogin(mobile, password);
    }

    function showDashboard(session) {
      document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('welcomeText').textContent =
        'Logged in as ' + session.role + (session.flatId ? ' (Flat ' + session.flatId + ')' : '') + '.';
      // Role-specific dashboards (Admin/Committee/Resident/CA) are the next
      // real piece of work ? this is a single generic placeholder for now,
      // not a finished dashboard.
    }

    function doLogout() {
      currentSession = null;
      document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('loginScreen').style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('mobileInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('passwordInput').value = '';
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>

    #errorMsg { color: #c0392b; margin-top: 10px; text-align: center; }

    .active { display: block; }
  </style>

<body>

  <div id="loginScreen" class="screen active">
    <h2>Ranikuthi Flat Owners' Association</h2>
    <input type="tel" id="mobileInput" placeholder="Mobile Number" maxlength="10">
    <input type="password" id="passwordInput" placeholder="Password">
    <button id="loginBtn" onclick="doLogin()">Log In</button>
    <div id="errorMsg"></div>
  </div>

  <div id="passwordChangeScreen" class="screen">
    <h2>Set Your Password</h2>
    <p>Your account requires a new password before you can continue.</p>
    <input type="password" id="newPasswordInput" placeholder="New Password (min 8 characters)">
    <input type="password" id="confirmPasswordInput" placeholder="Confirm New Password">
    <button id="changePwBtn" onclick="doChangePassword()">Set Password</button>
    <div id="pwErrorMsg"></div>
  </div>

  <div id="dashboard" class="screen">
    <h2>Welcome</h2>
    <p id="welcomeText"></p>
    <div id="successMsg"></div>
    <button onclick="doLogout()">Log Out</button>
  </div>

  <script>
    var currentSession = null;

    function showScreen(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('active'); });
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
    }

    function setError(elementId, msg) {
      document.getElementById(elementId).textContent = msg;
    }

    function doLogin() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('mobileInput').value.trim();
      var password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
      setError('errorMsg', '');

      if (!mobile || !password) {
        setError('errorMsg', 'Please enter both mobile number and password.');
        return;
      }

      var btn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Logging in...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (session) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          currentSession = session;

          if (session.forcePasswordReset) {
            showScreen('passwordChangeScreen');
            return;
          }
          showDashboard(session);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          setError('errorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientLogin(mobile, password);
    }

    function doChangePassword() {
      var newPw = document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value;
      setError('pwErrorMsg', '');

      if (newPw.length < 8) {
        setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Password must be at least 8 characters.');
        return;
      }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) {
        setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Passwords do not match.');
        return;
      }

      var btn = document.getElementById('changePwBtn');
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.textContent = 'Saving...';

      // oldPassword is empty because changePassword() skips the old-password
      // check specifically when Force_Password_Reset is TRUE (proven in Stage A).
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          currentSession.forcePasswordReset = false;
          showDashboard(currentSession);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.textContent = 'Set Password';
          setError('pwErrorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, '', newPw);
    }

    function showDashboard(session) {
      showScreen('dashboard');
      document.getElementById('welcomeText').textContent =
        'Logged in as ' + session.role + (session.flatId ? ' (Flat ' + session.flatId + ')' : '') + '.';
    }

    function doLogout() {
      currentSession = null;
      document.getElementById('mobileInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('passwordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value = '';
      showScreen('loginScreen');
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Picking up exactly where we left off ? building real role-specific dashboards, starting with the two most-tested backend paths: a Resident's own balance view, and Admin's bill/receipt actions.
First, a security gap worth catching now rather than later: rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(token, flatId) in Part 3 takes any flatId as a parameter ? nothing stops a Resident from passing someone else's flat ID and viewing their balance. That's fine when only trusted internal tests call it directly, but the moment it's reachable from a browser, it needs a version that can't be tricked. So:
New function ? add to Part 3's MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs (append, don't replace the file):
/**
 * Resident-safe wrapper: ALWAYS uses the session's own flatId,
 * ignoring anything the caller might try to pass. This is the
 * only balance-viewing function safe to expose to a Resident ?
 * rebuildMemberBalanceSummary() itself trusts its flatId argument
 * and must never be called directly from client-facing code.
 */
function viewOwnBalance(sessionToken) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_OWN_BALANCE'); // already seeded to RESIDENT in Stage A
  if (!session.flatId) throw new Error('This account is not linked to a flat.');
  return rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, session.flatId); // forces own flat, no parameter trust
}
Two libraries needed in Part 0's project now ? Deploy ? Libraries ? Add:
* Part 2's Script ID (from Part 2's editor: Project Settings gear icon ? copy Script ID) ? identifier Billing
* Part 3's Script ID (same process) ? identifier Accounting
Add to Part 0's WebApp.gs (append these three functions, keep everything already there):
function clientViewOwnBalance(token) {
  return Accounting.viewOwnBalance(token);
}
function clientGenerateBill(token, flatId, billMonthYear, dueDate) {
  return Billing.generateMonthlyBill(token, flatId, billMonthYear, dueDate);
}
function clientRecordPayment(token, flatId, paidAmountPaise, paymentMode, concessionPaise, adviceNote) {
  return Billing.recordPayment(token, flatId, paidAmountPaise, paymentMode, concessionPaise, adviceNote);
}

  <script>
    var currentSession = null;

    function showScreen(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('active'); });
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
    }
    function setError(elId, msg) { document.getElementById(elId).textContent = msg; }

    function doLogin() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('mobileInput').value.trim();
      var password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
      setError('errorMsg', '');
      if (!mobile || !password) { setError('errorMsg', 'Please enter both mobile number and password.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Logging in...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (session) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          currentSession = session;
          if (session.forcePasswordReset) { showScreen('passwordChangeScreen'); return; }
          showDashboard(session);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          setError('errorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientLogin(mobile, password);
    }

    function doChangePassword() {
      var newPw = document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value;
      setError('pwErrorMsg', '');
      if (newPw.length < 8) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Password must be at least 8 characters.'); return; }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Passwords do not match.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('changePwBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          currentSession.forcePasswordReset = false;
          showDashboard(currentSession);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Set Password';
          setError('pwErrorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, '', newPw);
    }

    function showDashboard(session) {
      showScreen('dashboard');
      document.getElementById('welcomeText').textContent =
        'Logged in as ' + session.role + (session.flatId ? ' (Flat ' + session.flatId + ')' : '') + '.';

      document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'none';

      if (session.role === 'RESIDENT') {
        document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'block';
        loadMyBalance();
      } else if (session.role === 'ADMIN' || session.role === 'COMMITTEE') {
        document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'block';
      }
      // CA view intentionally not built yet ? next increment.
    }

    function loadMyBalance() {
      document.getElementById('balanceContent').textContent = 'Loading...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (balance) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML =
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Billed</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalBilled) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Paid</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalPaid) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span><b>Outstanding</b></span><span><b>' + rupees(balance.outstanding) + '</b></span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Credit Balance</span><span>' + rupees(balance.creditBalance) + '</span></div>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientViewOwnBalance(currentSession.token);
    }

    function rupees(paise) { return '?' + (Number(paise) / 100).toFixed(2); }

    function doGenerateBill() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('billFlatId').value.trim();
      var monthYear = document.getElementById('billMonthYear').value.trim();
      var dueDate = document.getElementById('billDueDate').value;
      document.getElementById('billResult').textContent = 'Generating...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Bill ' + result.billId + ' ? Total: ' + rupees(result.totalPayable) + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientGenerateBill(currentSession.token, flatId, monthYear, dueDate ? new Date(dueDate) : new Date());
    }

    function doRecordPayment() {
      // L-22: UI confirmation gate before committing a receipt.
      if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to record this payment? This cannot be edited afterward, only voided.')) return;

      var flatId = document.getElementById('rcptFlatId').value.trim();
      var amount = document.getElementById('rcptAmount').value;
      var mode = document.getElementById('rcptMode').value;
      var concession = document.getElementById('rcptConcession').value || 0;
      var note = document.getElementById('rcptNote').value;
      document.getElementById('rcptResult').textContent = 'Recording...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Receipt(s): ' + result.receiptNos.join(', ') +
            (result.creditCreated ? ' ? Credit created: ' + rupees(result.creditCreated.amountPaise) : '') + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordPayment(currentSession.token, flatId, Number(amount), mode, Number(concession), note);
    }

    function doLogout() {
      currentSession = null;
      document.getElementById('mobileInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('passwordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value = '';
      showScreen('loginScreen');
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

Good ? real progress. The redeploy fixed the "not a function" error, and now we're getting a genuine, informative error instead of a silent hang. This is actually a permission-scoping bug worth understanding properly rather than just patching blindly.
Look at viewOwnBalance() from a couple of messages ago:
function viewOwnBalance(sessionToken) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_OWN_BALANCE'); // already seeded to RESIDENT in Stage A
  if (!session.flatId) throw new Error('This account is not linked to a flat.');
  return rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, session.flatId); // forces own flat, no parameter trust
}
This correctly checks VIEW_OWN_BALANCE on the Resident first ? that part passed, since we're not seeing that error. But it then calls rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(), and that function does its own separate authorization check:
function rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, flatId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_LEDGER'); // reuses an existing seeded permission
  ...
So a Resident correctly passes the VIEW_OWN_BALANCE gate, then immediately fails the inner function's VIEW_LEDGER gate ? which was only ever seeded for ADMIN and COMMITTEE/CA, never RESIDENT, and rightly so: a Resident genuinely shouldn't have blanket ledger-viewing rights. The bug is architectural, not a missing permission row: viewOwnBalance() is checking the right thing, but the function it calls is checking something broader and wrong for this caller.
The fix ? rebuildMemberBalanceSummary() shouldn't authorize at all; it should trust that its caller already did. This is a good example of a layering mistake: a function got written as if it were always called directly by an authorized session, but now it's also called internally by another already-authorized function (viewOwnBalance), and double-checking with the wrong permission breaks that.
Corrected ? full replacement of MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 3: ACCOUNTING BOOK
 * MemberBalanceSummaryService.gs ? L-17 CQRS read-model.
 * Always rebuildable from Ledger_Entries; never hand-edited.
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * BUGFIX (this version): rebuildMemberBalanceSummary() no longer
 * authorizes internally. It's called both directly (by
 * Committee/Admin/CA, who need VIEW_LEDGER) and indirectly via
 * viewOwnBalance() (Residents, who only ever get their own flat
 * and should NOT need VIEW_LEDGER at all). Authorization now
 * happens once, at the correct outer layer, not duplicated with
 * a permission that doesn't fit every caller.
 */
var SUMMARY_COLS_ = {
  FLAT_ID: 1, TOTAL_BILLED: 2, TOTAL_PAID: 3, OUTSTANDING: 4,
  CREDIT_BALANCE: 5, LAST_PAYMENT_DATE: 6, LAST_RECONCILED_TS: 7
};

function _computeFlatBalance_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.LEDGER_ENTRIES);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);

  var totalBilled = 0, totalPaid = 0, creditIssued = 0, creditApplied = 0, lastPaymentDate = null;

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) !== target) continue;

    var debitAcct = String(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.DEBIT_ACCOUNT - 1]);
    var creditAcct = String(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.CREDIT_ACCOUNT - 1]);
    var amount = Number(data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.AMOUNT_PAISE - 1]) || 0;
    var sourceType = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.SOURCE_TYPE - 1];

    if (debitAcct === '13000') totalBilled += amount;
    if (creditAcct === '13000') {
      totalPaid += amount;
      if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT') lastPaymentDate = data[r][LEDGER_COLS_.ENTRY_DATE - 1];
    }
    if (creditAcct === '23100') creditIssued += amount;
    if (debitAcct === '23100') creditApplied += amount;
  }

  return {
    totalBilled: totalBilled,
    totalPaid: totalPaid,
    outstanding: totalBilled - totalPaid,
    creditBalance: creditIssued - creditApplied,
    lastPaymentDate: lastPaymentDate
  };
}

/**
 * NOTE: this function no longer checks permissions itself ? the
 * caller must authorize BEFORE calling this. Two valid callers:
 *   - rebuildMemberBalanceSummaryForCommittee() below (VIEW_LEDGER, any flat)
 *   - viewOwnBalance() in this same file (VIEW_OWN_BALANCE, own flat only)
 * Do NOT expose this raw function directly to client code.
 */
function rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, flatId) {
  var balance = _computeFlatBalance_(flatId);

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_BALANCE_SUMMARY);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  var now = new Date();

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][SUMMARY_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target) {
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_BILLED).setValue(balance.totalBilled);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.TOTAL_PAID).setValue(balance.totalPaid);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.OUTSTANDING).setValue(balance.outstanding);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.CREDIT_BALANCE).setValue(balance.creditBalance);
      if (balance.lastPaymentDate) sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_PAYMENT_DATE).setValue(balance.lastPaymentDate);
      sheet.getRange(r + 1, SUMMARY_COLS_.LAST_RECONCILED_TS).setValue(now);
      return balance;
    }
  }
  sheet.appendRow([canonicalizeKey(flatId), balance.totalBilled, balance.totalPaid, balance.outstanding, balance.creditBalance, balance.lastPaymentDate || '', now]);
  return balance;
}

/** Committee/Admin/CA entry point ? explicit VIEW_LEDGER check, any flat allowed. */
function rebuildMemberBalanceSummaryForCommittee(sessionToken, flatId) {
  _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_LEDGER');
  return rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, flatId);
}

/** Resident entry point ? VIEW_OWN_BALANCE check, own flat forced, ignores any flatId argument entirely. */
function viewOwnBalance(sessionToken) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VIEW_OWN_BALANCE');
  if (!session.flatId) throw new Error('This account is not linked to a flat.');
  return rebuildMemberBalanceSummary(sessionToken, session.flatId);
}
One caller elsewhere needs updating too ? Part 3's own Test_Stage_D_05_summary.gs (the internal test from Stage D) calls rebuildMemberBalanceSummary directly, which now has no authorization at all. That test should be updated to call rebuildMemberBalanceSummaryForCommittee instead, so it still exercises a real authorization path. Not urgent to fix right this moment, but worth doing before we consider Part 3 re-verified.

    function doProcessQueue() {
      document.getElementById('queueResult').textContent = 'Processing...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('queueResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Posted: ' + result.posted.length + ', Skipped: ' + result.skipped.length + ', Failed: ' + result.failed.length + '</span>' +
            (result.failed.length ? '<br><span class="err">' + JSON.stringify(result.failed) + '</span>' : '');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('queueResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientProcessQueue(currentSession.token);
    }
One honest design question this raises, worth deciding now rather than letting it linger: should posting to the ledger really be a manual button an Admin clicks, or should clientRecordPayment/clientGenerateBill automatically call processQueue() right after enqueueing, so residents see up-to-date balances without anyone remembering to click a separate button? I'd lean toward keeping it manual and visible ? it matches L-16's philosophy that posting failures should be seen and handled deliberately, not swallowed by an automatic background call the Admin never notices failed. But this does mean every real bill/payment needs this one extra click. Your call on whether that's an acceptable manual step or worth automating.

Replace doRecordPayment() in index.html:
    function doRecordPayment() {
      if (!confirm('Are you sure you want to record this payment? This cannot be edited afterward, only voided.')) return;

      var flatId = document.getElementById('rcptFlatId').value.trim();
      var amountRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptAmount').value);
      var mode = document.getElementById('rcptMode').value;
      var concessionRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptConcession').value) || 0;
      var note = document.getElementById('rcptNote').value;

      if (!amountRupees || amountRupees <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Please enter a valid amount in Rupees.</span>';
        return;
      }

      // Convert Rupees -> paise ONLY here, at the UI boundary. Math.round guards
      // against floating-point artifacts (e.g. 100.1 * 100 = 10009.999999...).
      var amountPaise = Math.round(amountRupees * 100);
      var concessionPaise = Math.round(concessionRupees * 100);

      document.getElementById('rcptResult').textContent = 'Recording...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Receipt(s): ' + result.receiptNos.join(', ') +
            (result.creditCreated ? ' ? Credit created: ' + rupees(result.creditCreated.amountPaise) : '') + '</span>';
        })

Good ? glad that resolved cleanly. Before moving on, one thing worth closing the loop on properly: the actual ?1 mistaken receipt is still sitting in your real ledger right now, uncorrected. Per L-11, it can't just be deleted ? it needs a formal void/reversal, which is exactly the safeguard the system was designed to provide for real mistakes like this.
Let's build that now, since it's genuinely needed, not hypothetical.
New function ? append to Part 2's ReceiptService.gs:
/**
 * L-11: receipts are immutable. This does NOT edit or delete the
 * original receipt ? it marks it VOID and creates a linked reversal.
 * The bill it was applied against is re-opened (status reverted)
 * since the payment against it is being undone.
 */
function voidReceipt(sessionToken, receiptNo, voidReason) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'VOID_RECEIPT');
  if (!voidReason || voidReason.trim().length < 5) {
    throw new Error('A real void reason is required.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rowIndex = -1, receiptRow = null;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_NO - 1] === receiptNo) { rowIndex = r + 1; receiptRow = data[r]; break; }
  }
  if (!receiptRow) throw new Error('Receipt ' + receiptNo + ' not found.');
  if (receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_STATUS - 1] !== 'ACTIVE') {
    throw new Error('Receipt ' + receiptNo + ' is not ACTIVE (currently: ' + receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_STATUS - 1] + ').');
  }

  var billId = receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1];
  var cashPaise = Number(receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.PAID_AMOUNT - 1]);
  var concessionPaise = Number(receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.CONCESSION - 1]);
  var now = new Date();

  // Mark the original VOID ? never deleted, never overwritten (L-11).
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_STATUS).setValue('VOID');
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, RECEIPT_COLS_.VOID_REASON).setValue(voidReason);

  // Re-open the bill this receipt had been applied against ? the amount is no longer paid.
  var billSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var billData = billSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (var b = 1; b < billData.length; b++) {
    if (billData[b][BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1] === billId) {
      billSheet.getRange(b + 1, BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS).setValue('UNPAID'); // simplified: full reversal reopens fully
      break;
    }
  }

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'RECEIPT_VOIDED', SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS, receiptNo, voidReason, '');

  // L-16: enqueue the compensating ledger entry ? reverses exactly what the original RECEIPT posted.
  enqueueTransaction_('RECEIPT_VOID', receiptNo, receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1], cashPaise + concessionPaise, {
    originalReceiptNo: receiptNo, cashPaise: cashPaise, concessionPaise: concessionPaise
  }, session.userId, '');

  return { receiptNo: receiptNo, status: 'VOID' };
}
Part 3's LedgerPostingService.gs needs the reversal mapping added ? insert this case into _buildJournalLines_(), alongside the existing ones:
  if (sourceType === 'RECEIPT_VOID') {
    // Exact mirror image of the original RECEIPT entry ? same accounts, sides flipped.
    var cash = Number(payload.cashPaise) || 0;
    var concession = Number(payload.concessionPaise) || 0;
    var lines = [];
    lines.push({ account: '13000', side: 'DEBIT', amount: cash + concession }); // receivable reopens
    if (cash > 0) lines.push({ account: '11000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: cash });        // cash reversed out
    if (concession > 0) lines.push({ account: '60100', side: 'CREDIT', amount: concession }); // concession reversed out
    return lines;
  }

Run this directly in Part 2's editor (not through the UI yet ? voiding is rare and deliberate, matching L-28's philosophy that sensitive corrections shouldn't be a casual one-click browser action):
function fix_void_mistaken_1rupee_receipt() {

  var result = voidReceipt(session.token, 'RCT-2026-27-00XXX', 'Data entry error: intended Rs 100, recorded as Rs 1 (paise/rupee unit confusion) ? corrected via V5 UI fix.');

You'll need to replace RCT-2026-27-00XXX with the actual receipt number of the mistaken ?1 entry ? could you check Payment_Receipts and tell me which one it is? Once voided and posted (via "Post to Ledger" as usual), you can then record the correct ?100 payment cleanly through the now-fixed UI.


Good catch ? and this one isn't a code bug in the same family as before (no wrong type, no serialization issue). It's a real, legitimate gap in the design: L-09 says credit should be "reduced by future FIFO application against dues," but no function was ever built that actually does that application. Credit just sits there as a separate liability, and nothing automatically nets it against new outstanding amounts. Worth fixing properly.
What actually happened, traced through the real numbers (so you know the ledger itself isn't lying, just incomplete): the mistaken Re1 receipt was applied, then a correct ?100 payment was recorded before the mistaken one got voided ? so that payment's surplus calculation (?51 credit) was based on the bill still showing the Re1 as applied. When the Re1 receipt was then voided, it correctly reopened exactly ?1 of receivable. The net economic position is actually correct (?51 credit ? ?1 outstanding = ?50 real surplus, which matches reality), but the system never nets these against each other ? it just shows both numbers side by side, which is exactly the confusing, wrong-looking result you saw.
The real fix: build the missing credit-application function.
New file ? CreditApplicationService.gs in Part 2's project:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * CreditApplicationService.gs
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * Implements the part of L-09 that was never built: applying an
 * EXISTING Member_Credit balance against a flat's outstanding
 * dues, FIFO by credit creation date, oldest bill first. This is
 * separate from a payment's own same-transaction surplus (which
 * recordPayment already handles) ? this is for credit sitting
 * from an EARLIER transaction being applied later, on demand.
 */
var MC_COLS_ = {
  CREDIT_ID: 1, FLAT_ID: 2, SOURCE_RECEIPT: 3, CREDIT_DATE: 4, CREDIT_PAISE: 5,
  APPLIED_PAISE: 6, REMAINING_PAISE: 7, STATUS: 8, REFUND_REF: 9, CREATED_BY: 10, CREATED_TS: 11
};

function _getActiveCreditsForFlat_(flatId) {
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_CREDITS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var target = canonicalizeKey(flatId);
  var credits = [];
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (canonicalizeKey(data[r][MC_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1]) === target &&
        Number(data[r][MC_COLS_.REMAINING_PAISE - 1]) > 0 &&
        data[r][MC_COLS_.STATUS - 1] !== 'REFUNDED') {
      credits.push({ rowIndex: r + 1, values: data[r] });
    }
  }
  credits.sort(function (a, b) { return new Date(a.values[MC_COLS_.CREDIT_DATE - 1]) - new Date(b.values[MC_COLS_.CREDIT_DATE - 1]); });
  return credits;
}

/**
 * Applies as much of the flat's existing credit as possible against
 * its oldest outstanding bills. Safe to call any time ? does nothing
 * (returns zero applied) if there's no credit or no outstanding.
 */
function applyMemberCreditToOutstanding(sessionToken, flatId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'APPLY_MEMBER_CREDIT');

  var credits = _getActiveCreditsForFlat_(flatId);
  var bills = _getOldestUnpaidBills_(flatId);
  var creditSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MEMBER_CREDITS);
  var billSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var totalApplied = 0;
  var now = new Date();

  var billIndex = 0;
  for (var c = 0; c < credits.length; c++) {
    var credit = credits[c];
    var remaining = Number(credit.values[MC_COLS_.REMAINING_PAISE - 1]);

    while (remaining > 0 && billIndex < bills.length) {
      var bill = bills[billIndex];
      var outstanding = _getBillOutstanding_(bill);
      if (outstanding <= 0) { billIndex++; continue; }

      var applyAmount = Math.min(remaining, outstanding);

      // Record the application as a synthetic receipt so the bill's
      // applied-total (and every other calc that reads Payment_Receipts)
      // sees this consistently with any other payment.
      var fy = getCurrentFinancialYear_();
      var seq = getNextSequence_('RECEIPT', fy);
      var receiptNo = 'RCT-' + fy + '-' + String(seq).padStart(5, '0');
      getSheet_(SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS).appendRow([
        receiptNo, bill.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1], canonicalizeKey(flatId), now,
        'CREDIT_APPLIED', 0, 0, 0, '', 0, 'ACTIVE', '', '', '', session.userId, now, 1, ''
      ]);

      var newAppliedRemaining = Number(credit.values[MC_COLS_.APPLIED_PAISE - 1]) + applyAmount;
      var newCreditRemaining = remaining - applyAmount;
      creditSheet.getRange(credit.rowIndex, MC_COLS_.APPLIED_PAISE).setValue(newAppliedRemaining);
      creditSheet.getRange(credit.rowIndex, MC_COLS_.REMAINING_PAISE).setValue(newCreditRemaining);
      creditSheet.getRange(credit.rowIndex, MC_COLS_.STATUS).setValue(newCreditRemaining <= 0 ? 'FULLY_APPLIED' : 'ACTIVE');

      var newOutstanding = outstanding - applyAmount;
      billSheet.getRange(bill.rowIndex, BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS).setValue(newOutstanding <= 0 ? 'PAID' : 'PARTIALLY_PAID');

      enqueueTransaction_('CREDIT_APPLIED', receiptNo, flatId, applyAmount, {
        creditId: credit.values[MC_COLS_.CREDIT_ID - 1], billId: bill.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1]
      }, session.userId, 'REVERSE_CREDIT_APPLICATION_' + receiptNo);

      logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'CREDIT_APPLIED', SHEETS_.MEMBER_CREDITS, credit.values[MC_COLS_.CREDIT_ID - 1],
        'Applied ' + applyAmount + ' paise to ' + bill.values[BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1], '');

      totalApplied += applyAmount;
      remaining -= applyAmount;
      if (newOutstanding <= 0) billIndex++;
    }
  }

  return { totalApplied: totalApplied };
}

    // Liability reduced, receivable reduced ? no cash moves at all.
    return [
      { account: '23100', side: 'DEBIT', amount: amountPaise },
      { account: '13000', side: 'CREDIT', amount: amountPaise }
    ];
  }

  <script>
    var currentSession = null;

    function showScreen(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('active'); });
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
    }
    function setError(elId, msg) { document.getElementById(elId).textContent = msg; }

    function doLogin() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('mobileInput').value.trim();
      var password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
      setError('errorMsg', '');
      if (!mobile || !password) { setError('errorMsg', 'Please enter both mobile number and password.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Logging in...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (session) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          currentSession = session;
          if (session.forcePasswordReset) { showScreen('passwordChangeScreen'); return; }
          showDashboard(session);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          setError('errorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientLogin(mobile, password);
    }

    function doChangePassword() {
      var newPw = document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value;
      setError('pwErrorMsg', '');
      if (newPw.length < 8) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Password must be at least 8 characters.'); return; }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Passwords do not match.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('changePwBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          currentSession.forcePasswordReset = false;
          showDashboard(currentSession);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Set Password';
          setError('pwErrorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, '', newPw);
    }

    function showDashboard(session) {
      showScreen('dashboard');
      document.getElementById('welcomeText').textContent =
        'Logged in as ' + session.role + (session.flatId ? ' (Flat ' + session.flatId + ')' : '') + '.';

      document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'none';

      if (session.role === 'RESIDENT') {
        document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'block';
        loadMyBalance();
      } else if (session.role === 'ADMIN' || session.role === 'COMMITTEE') {
        document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'block';
      }
      // CA view intentionally not built yet ? next increment.
    }

    function loadMyBalance() {
      document.getElementById('balanceContent').textContent = 'Loading...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (balance) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML =
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Billed</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalBilled) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Paid</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalPaid) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span><b>Outstanding</b></span><span><b>' + rupees(balance.outstanding) + '</b></span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Credit Balance</span><span>' + rupees(balance.creditBalance) + '</span></div>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientViewOwnBalance(currentSession.token);
    }

    function rupees(paise) { return '?' + (Number(paise) / 100).toFixed(2); }

    function doGenerateBill() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('billFlatId').value.trim();
      var monthYear = document.getElementById('billMonthYear').value.trim();
      var dueDateStr = document.getElementById('billDueDate').value; // plain string, e.g. "2026-12-15"
      document.getElementById('billResult').textContent = 'Generating...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Bill ' + result.billId + ' ? Total: ' + rupees(result.totalPayable) + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientGenerateBill(currentSession.token, flatId, monthYear, dueDateStr);
    }

    function doRecordPayment() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('rcptFlatId').value.trim();
      var amountRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptAmount').value);
      var mode = document.getElementById('rcptMode').value;
      var concessionRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptConcession').value) || 0;
      var note = document.getElementById('rcptNote').value;

      if (!amountRupees || amountRupees <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Please enter a valid amount in Rupees.</span>';
        return;
      }

      var confirmMsg = 'Record payment of ?' + amountRupees.toFixed(2) + ' for flat ' + flatId +
        (concessionRupees > 0 ? ' with a concession of ?' + concessionRupees.toFixed(2) : '') +
        '?\n\nThis cannot be edited afterward, only voided.';
      if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return;

      var amountPaise = Math.round(amountRupees * 100);
      var concessionPaise = Math.round(concessionRupees * 100);

      document.getElementById('rcptResult').textContent = 'Recording...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Receipt(s): ' + result.receiptNos.join(', ') +
            (result.creditCreated ? ' ? Credit created: ' + rupees(result.creditCreated.amountPaise) : '') + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordPayment(currentSession.token, flatId, amountPaise, mode, concessionPaise, note);
    }

    function doProcessQueue() {
      document.getElementById('queueResult').textContent = 'Processing...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('queueResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Posted: ' + result.posted.length + ', Skipped: ' + result.skipped.length + ', Failed: ' + result.failed.length + '</span>' +
            (result.failed.length ? '<br><span class="err">' + JSON.stringify(result.failed) + '</span>' : '');
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('queueResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientProcessQueue(currentSession.token);
    }

    function doApplyCredit() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('applyCreditFlatId').value.trim();
      document.getElementById('applyCreditResult').textContent = 'Applying...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('applyCreditResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Applied: ' + rupees(result.totalApplied) + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('applyCreditResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientApplyCredit(currentSession.token, flatId);
    }

    function doLogout() {
      currentSession = null;
      document.getElementById('mobileInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('passwordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value = '';
      showScreen('loginScreen');
    }

Add to Part 4's ComplaintService.gs (append this function):
/** Lists complaints, optionally filtered by status. Dates converted to strings ? same lesson as the balance-view Date bug. */
function listComplaints(sessionToken, statusFilter) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'MANAGE_COMPLAINTS');
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var results = [];

  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    var status = data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS - 1];
    if (statusFilter && status !== statusFilter) continue;
    results.push({
      complaintId: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.COMPLAINT_ID - 1],
      flatId: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
      category: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.CATEGORY - 1],
      title: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.TITLE - 1],
      priority: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.PRIORITY - 1],
      status: status,
      assignedTo: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.ASSIGNED_TO - 1],
      raisedTimestamp: data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.RAISED_TS - 1] ? new Date(data[r][COMPLAINT_COLS_.RAISED_TS - 1]).toISOString() : null
    });
  }
  return results;
}

    function doListComplaints() {
      document.getElementById('complaintsList').textContent = 'Loading...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (list) {
          if (list.length === 0) {
            document.getElementById('complaintsList').textContent = 'No complaints found.';
            return;
          }
          var html = '';
          list.forEach(function (c) {
            html += '<div style="border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:4px 0;">' +
              '<b>' + c.complaintId + '</b> ? ' + c.title + ' (' + c.flatId + ', ' + c.priority + ', <b>' + c.status + '</b>)' +
              (c.assignedTo ? ' ? assigned to ' + c.assignedTo : '') + '</div>';
          });
          document.getElementById('complaintsList').innerHTML = html;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('complaintsList').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientListComplaints(currentSession.token, ''); // empty = all statuses
    }

        })

          document.getElementById('assignResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientAssignComplaint(currentSession.token, complaintId, assignTo, notes);

MANAGE_COMPLAINTS was already seeded for ADMIN back when we tested this via Apps Script, so no new permission row should be needed. Redeploy, log in as Admin, click List Open Complaints to find CMP-618DC689, then try assigning and closing it through the browser.

Genuinely good catch ? right now only Admin/Committee can close a complaint (MANAGE_COMPLAINTS), so a Resident who raised something by mistake has no self-service way to retract it. This needs a real design decision, not just wiring: should a Resident be able to close any of their own complaints in any state, or only their own, and only before an Admin has started acting on it?
I'd argue for the narrower rule: a Resident may only withdraw their own complaint while it's still OPEN ? once it's IN_PROGRESS (an Admin has assigned it, possibly already spent time or money on it), letting the original Resident unilaterally close it would undercut the Admin's own visibility into work already underway. If a Resident wants to retract something already in progress, that should go through the Admin, not a self-service button.
Add to Part 4's ComplaintService.gs (append):
/**
 * Resident self-service withdrawal ? narrower than closeComplaint():
 * only the ORIGINAL RAISER can call this, and only while status is
 * still OPEN (not yet assigned/acted upon). Anything further along
 * must go through an Admin's closeComplaint() instead.
 */
function withdrawOwnComplaint(sessionToken, complaintId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'RAISE_COMPLAINT'); // same permission as raising one
  var found = _findComplaintRow_(complaintId);
  if (!found) throw new Error('Complaint ' + complaintId + ' not found.');

  if (found.values[COMPLAINT_COLS_.RAISED_BY - 1] !== session.userId) {
    throw new Error('You can only withdraw complaints you raised yourself.');
  }
  var currentStatus = found.values[COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS - 1];
  if (currentStatus !== 'OPEN') {
    throw new Error('This complaint is already ' + currentStatus + ' ? it can no longer be self-withdrawn. Please contact the committee.');
  }

  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS);
  var now = new Date();
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.STATUS).setValue('CLOSED');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSED_BY).setValue(session.userId);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSURE_TYPE).setValue('WITHDRAWN');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSURE_REMARKS).setValue('Withdrawn by resident who raised it.');
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.LAST_UPDATED_TS).setValue(now);
  sheet.getRange(found.rowIndex, COMPLAINT_COLS_.CLOSED_TS).setValue(now);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'COMPLAINT_SELF_WITHDRAWN', SHEETS_.COMPLAINTS, complaintId, 'Withdrawn by original raiser', '');
  return { complaintId: complaintId, status: 'CLOSED' };
}

function clientCreateVoucher(token, expenseDate, category, vendorId, baseAmountRupees, gstAmountRupees, paymentMode, description) {
  var baseAmountPaise = Math.round(Number(baseAmountRupees) * 100);
  var gstAmountPaise = Math.round(Number(gstAmountRupees) * 100);
  return Billing.createExpenseVoucher(token, {
    expenseDate: new Date(expenseDate), category: category, vendorId: vendorId,
    baseAmountPaise: baseAmountPaise, gstAmountPaise: gstAmountPaise, paymentMode: paymentMode, description: description
  });
}
function clientApproveVoucher(token, voucherNo) {
  return Billing.approveExpenseVoucher(token, voucherNo);
}
function clientCreateVendor(token, vendorName, gstin, pan, contactPerson, mobile, email) {
  return Billing.createVendor(token, { vendorName: vendorName, gstin: gstin, pan: pan, contactPerson: contactPerson, mobile: mobile, email: email });
}

    function doCreateVendor() {
      var name = document.getElementById('vendorName').value.trim();
      var gstin = document.getElementById('vendorGstin').value.trim();
      var mobile = document.getElementById('vendorMobile').value.trim();
      document.getElementById('vendorResult').textContent = 'Adding...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('vendorResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Vendor created: ' + result.vendorId + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('vendorResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientCreateVendor(currentSession.token, name, gstin, '', '', mobile, '');
    }

    function doCreateVoucher() {
      var date = document.getElementById('voucherDate').value;
      var category = document.getElementById('voucherCategory').value.trim();
      var vendorId = document.getElementById('voucherVendorId').value.trim();
      var baseAmount = document.getElementById('voucherBaseAmount').value;
      var gstAmount = document.getElementById('voucherGstAmount').value || 0;
      var mode = document.getElementById('voucherPaymentMode').value;
      var description = document.getElementById('voucherDescription').value.trim();

      if (!confirm('Create voucher for ?' + parseFloat(baseAmount).toFixed(2) + ' (vendor ' + vendorId + ')? A DIFFERENT admin must approve this before it posts.')) return;

      document.getElementById('voucherResult').textContent = 'Creating...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('voucherResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Voucher ' + result.voucherNo + ' created ? Total: ?' + (result.totalAmountPaise / 100).toFixed(2) + '. Awaiting a DIFFERENT admin\'s approval.</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('voucherResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientCreateVoucher(currentSession.token, date, category, vendorId, baseAmount, gstAmount, mode, description);
    }

    function doApproveVoucher() {
      var voucherNo = document.getElementById('approveVoucherNo').value.trim();
      if (!confirm('Approve voucher ' + voucherNo + '? You must NOT be the person who created it.')) return;

      document.getElementById('approveVoucherResult').textContent = 'Approving...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('approveVoucherResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">' + result.voucherNo + ' ? ' + result.status + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('approveVoucherResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientApproveVoucher(currentSession.token, voucherNo);
    }

Good catch, and this is a genuine, worthwhile gap ? nothing in adminInitiateReset ever checked that the actor and the target are different people. Since you were already logged in as admin2 with valid credentials, you could use the "reset someone else" tool on your own account, which defeats the entire point of L-27 (a peer verifying and resetting, not self-service through the back door). It didn't create a security hole ? you still needed your own email access either way ? but it's confusing, pointless, and worth blocking outright, since a legitimate self-reset should always go through changePassword() instead.
Fix ? add one check to AdminResetService.gs's adminInitiateReset:
function adminInitiateReset(actorSessionToken, targetUserId) {
  var actor = verifySessionToken(actorSessionToken);
  if (!actor) throw new Error('Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.');
  requirePermission_(actor.role, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET_PASSWORD');

  // NEW: block self-targeting ? L-27 is a PEER reset. If you're already
  // logged in with a valid session, use changePassword() to set your own
  // password instead; this tool exists for resetting SOMEONE ELSE.
  if (actor.userId === targetUserId) {
    throw new Error('You cannot reset your own password through this tool. If you are logged in and want to change your password, use the password-change option instead.');
  }

  var otp = requestOtp(targetUserId, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET', actor.userId);

  var resetSheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.ADMIN_RESET_UTILITY);
  var resetId = 'RST-' + Utilities.getUuid().substring(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  resetSheet.appendRow([resetId, actor.userId, actor.role, targetUserId, new Date(), '', otp.otpId, 'OTP_SENT']);

  return { resetId: resetId, otpId: otp.otpId, message: 'OTP sent to target user\'s registered email.' };
}
That's the only change needed ? everything else in the file (adminCompleteReset) stays exactly as it is, since the block at initiation is sufficient; a reset can never be started against yourself in the first place.
Save, and since this is a Part 0 library function (not the Web App file itself), no redeploy is needed ? Part 0's other library-consuming behavior updates immediately, but since this is Part 0's own code being called by Part 0's own WebApp.gs, it does need a redeploy (new version) to take effect for the live Web App, same as always. Test it by trying to initiate a reset against your own User ID again ? it should now be cleanly refused.

Add this function inside the <script> block:
    function doSelfChangePassword() {
      var oldPw = document.getElementById('selfOldPassword').value;
      var newPw = document.getElementById('selfNewPassword').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('selfConfirmPassword').value;

      if (newPw.length < 8) {
        document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">New password must be at least 8 characters.</span>';
        return;
      }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) {
        document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">New passwords do not match.</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').textContent = 'Changing...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Password changed successfully.</span>';
          document.getElementById('selfOldPassword').value = '';
          document.getElementById('selfNewPassword').value = '';
          document.getElementById('selfConfirmPassword').value = '';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, oldPw, newPw);
    }

function clientCreateFlat(token, wing, flatNo, areaSqFt, floor, flatType) {
  return Members.createFlat(token, { wing: wing, flatNo: flatNo, areaSqFt: Number(areaSqFt), floor: floor, flatType: flatType });
}
function clientSetInitialOccupancy(token, flatId, ownerName, ownerMobile, ownerEmail, occupancyStatus) {
  return Members.setInitialOccupancy(token, { flatId: flatId, ownerName: ownerName, ownerMobile: ownerMobile, ownerEmail: ownerEmail, occupancyStatus: occupancyStatus });
}
function clientCreateUser(token, roleName, flatId, fullName, mobileNumber, emailAddress) {
  return createUser(token, roleName, { flatId: flatId, fullName: fullName, mobileNumber: mobileNumber, emailAddress: emailAddress });
}
function clientRecordOwnershipTransfer(token, flatId, previousOwnerName, newOwnerName, duesClearedRupees, writtenOffRupees, writeOffApprovedBy) {
  var duesClearedPaise = Math.round(Number(duesClearedRupees) * 100);
  var writtenOffPaise = Math.round(Number(writtenOffRupees || 0) * 100);
  return Members.recordOwnershipTransfer(token, flatId, previousOwnerName, newOwnerName, duesClearedPaise, writtenOffPaise, writeOffApprovedBy);
}
function clientTransferOccupancy(token, flatId, newOwnerName, newOwnerMobile, newOwnerEmail, closedReason, transferLogId) {
  return Members.transferOccupancy(token, flatId,
    { ownerName: newOwnerName, ownerMobile: newOwnerMobile, ownerEmail: newOwnerEmail, occupancyStatus: 'OWNER_OCCUPIED' },
    closedReason, transferLogId);
}

    function doCreateFlat() {
      var wing = document.getElementById('newFlatWing').value.trim();
      var flatNo = document.getElementById('newFlatNo').value.trim();
      var area = document.getElementById('newFlatArea').value;
      var floor = document.getElementById('newFlatFloor').value.trim();
      var flatType = document.getElementById('newFlatType').value.trim();

      document.getElementById('createFlatResult').textContent = 'Creating...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('createFlatResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Flat created: ' + result.flatId + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('createFlatResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientCreateFlat(currentSession.token, wing, flatNo, area, floor, flatType);
    }

    function doSetInitialOccupancy() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('occFlatId').value.trim();
      var ownerName = document.getElementById('occOwnerName').value.trim();
      var ownerMobile = document.getElementById('occOwnerMobile').value.trim();
      var ownerEmail = document.getElementById('occOwnerEmail').value.trim();

      document.getElementById('occResult').textContent = 'Setting...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('occResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Occupancy set: ' + result.occupancyId + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('occResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientSetInitialOccupancy(currentSession.token, flatId, ownerName, ownerMobile, ownerEmail, 'OWNER_OCCUPIED');
    }

    function doCreateUser() {
      var role = document.getElementById('newUserRole').value;
      var flatId = document.getElementById('newUserFlatId').value.trim();
      var fullName = document.getElementById('newUserFullName').value.trim();
      var mobile = document.getElementById('newUserMobile').value.trim();
      var email = document.getElementById('newUserEmail').value.trim();

      document.getElementById('createUserResult').textContent = 'Creating...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('createUserResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">User created: ' + result.userId + ' (' + result.role + '). Check Apps Script execution log for the one-time temp password to relay securely.</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('createUserResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientCreateUser(currentSession.token, role, flatId, fullName, mobile, email);
    }

    function doRecordTransfer() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('xferFlatId').value.trim();
      var prevOwner = document.getElementById('xferPrevOwner').value.trim();
      var newOwner = document.getElementById('xferNewOwner').value.trim();
      var duesCleared = document.getElementById('xferDuesCleared').value || 0;
      var writtenOff = document.getElementById('xferWrittenOff').value || 0;
      var approvedBy = document.getElementById('xferApprovedBy').value.trim();

      if (Number(writtenOff) > 0 && !approvedBy) {
        document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">A write-off amount requires a named approver (L-38).</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').textContent = 'Recording...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Transfer recorded: ' + result.transferId + '</span>';
          document.getElementById('xferTransferId').value = result.transferId;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordOwnershipTransfer(currentSession.token, flatId, prevOwner, newOwner, duesCleared, writtenOff, approvedBy);
    }

    function doTransferOccupancy() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('xferFlatId').value.trim();
      var newOwner = document.getElementById('xferNewOwner').value.trim();
      var newOwnerMobile = document.getElementById('xferNewOwnerMobile').value.trim();
      var newOwnerEmail = document.getElementById('xferNewOwnerEmail').value.trim();
      var reason = document.getElementById('xferReason').value.trim();
      var transferId = document.getElementById('xferTransferId').value.trim();

      if (!confirm('Finalize ownership transfer for ' + flatId + ' to ' + newOwner + '? This closes the previous occupancy record permanently (still visible, but no longer current).')) return;

      document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').textContent = 'Transferring...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Closed: ' + result.closedOccupancyId + ', New: ' + result.newOccupancyId + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientTransferOccupancy(currentSession.token, flatId, newOwner, newOwnerMobile, newOwnerEmail, reason, transferId);
    }

Good ? G2.1 needs one foundational piece first: a shared PDF-generation utility that every document type (bill/receipt/voucher) will reuse, so the letterhead/hash logic lives in exactly one place, not copy-pasted three times.
One design decision worth stating explicitly before the code, not hiding it: L-23 asks for the SHA-256 hash to appear inside the PDF's own footer ? but computing a hash of a document that includes its own hash is circular (the hash would have to describe content it doesn't yet contain). The practical, honest resolution: the footer states that the document is hash-verified and where to find the hash (in the system record), rather than literally printing the hash value inside the PDF itself. The actual hash ? computed over the real, final PDF bytes ? is what gets stored in SHA256_Hash and is the one that matters for verification. This is a small, deliberate interpretation of L-23, not a corner cut silently.
Add to Part 0's Global_Settings (manual rows, same as before): SOCIETY_NAME | Ranikuthi Flat Owners' Association | STRING | ADMIN | ... and SOCIETY_ADDRESS | <your real address> | STRING | ADMIN | ...
New file ? PdfGenerationService.gs in Part 2's project:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? PART 2: BILLING & FINANCIAL LOG
 * PdfGenerationService.gs
 * Implements L-12 (SHA-256 integrity), L-23 (letterhead standard),
 * L-24 (PDF link columns already exist in schema).
 * -------------------------------------------------------------
 * DESIGN NOTE: the footer states the document is hash-verified
 * and references the system record ? it does NOT print the literal
 * hash value inside the PDF (computing a hash of content that
 * includes its own hash is circular). The real hash, computed over
 * the final PDF bytes, is stored in the sheet's SHA256_Hash column ?
 * that stored value is the one used for verification.
 */
var DOCS_ROOT_FOLDER_NAME_ = 'Ranikuthi_V5_Documents';

function _getOrCreateFolder_(parentFolder, name) {
  var existing = parentFolder.getFoldersByName(name);
  return existing.hasNext() ? existing.next() : parentFolder.createFolder(name);
}

function _getDocumentsFolder_(subFolderName) {
  var root = _getOrCreateFolder_(DriveApp.getRootFolder(), DOCS_ROOT_FOLDER_NAME_);
  return _getOrCreateFolder_(root, subFolderName);
}

function _computeSha256Hash_(blob) {
  var digest = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, blob.getBytes());
  return digest.map(function (b) { return (b < 0 ? b + 256 : b).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'); }).join('');
}

/**
 * Builds a letterheaded PDF: society name/address header, title,
 * body lines, and an integrity footer (9pt italic, per L-23).
 * The temporary Google Doc used to build this is trashed immediately
 * after export ? only the PDF is kept.
 */
function _buildLetterheadPdf_(title, bodyLines) {
  var societyName = Gateway.getSetting('SOCIETY_NAME') || 'Ranikuthi Flat Owners\' Association';
  var societyAddress = Gateway.getSetting('SOCIETY_ADDRESS') || '';

  var doc = DocumentApp.create('TEMP_' + title + '_' + Utilities.getUuid());
  var body = doc.getBody();

  body.appendParagraph(societyName).setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setBold(true);
  if (societyAddress) body.appendParagraph(societyAddress).setItalic(true);
  body.appendHorizontalRule();
  body.appendParagraph(title).setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING2);
  body.appendParagraph('');

  bodyLines.forEach(function (line) { body.appendParagraph(line); });

  body.appendParagraph('');
  body.appendHorizontalRule();
  var footer = body.appendParagraph('This is a system-generated document. Its integrity is verified by a SHA-256 hash held in the association\'s official records.');
  footer.setItalic(true);
  footer.setFontSize(9);

  doc.saveAndClose();
  var file = DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId());
  var pdfBlob = file.getAs('application/pdf');
  file.setTrashed(true); // only the PDF is kept, not the working Doc

  return pdfBlob;
}

/** Generates the PDF, saves it to the right folder, computes the real hash, and returns both. Does NOT write to any sheet ? callers do that themselves, since each sheet's columns differ. */
function _generateAndSavePdf_(title, bodyLines, subFolderName, fileName) {
  var pdfBlob = _buildLetterheadPdf_(title, bodyLines);
  var folder = _getDocumentsFolder_(subFolderName);
  var file = folder.createFile(pdfBlob).setName(fileName);
  var hash = _computeSha256Hash_(pdfBlob);
  return { url: file.getUrl(), hash: hash, fileId: file.getId() };
}
Add to BillingService.gs (append this function):
/** Generates and attaches a PDF for an existing bill. Call AFTER generateMonthlyBill(). */
function generateBillPdf(sessionToken, billId) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'GENERATE_BILL');
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rowIndex = -1, row = null;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][BILL_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1] === billId) { rowIndex = r + 1; row = data[r]; break; }
  }
  if (!row) throw new Error('Bill ' + billId + ' not found.');

  var bodyLines = [
    'Bill No: ' + billId,
    'Flat: ' + row[BILL_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
    'Billing Period: ' + row[BILL_COLS_.BILL_MONTH_YEAR - 1],
    'Base Charge: ?' + (Number(row[BILL_COLS_.BASE_CHARGE - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2),
    'Previous Unpaid: ?' + (Number(row[BILL_COLS_.PREV_UNPAID - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2),
    'Late Fine: ?' + (Number(row[BILL_COLS_.LATE_FINE - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2),
    'Total Payable: ?' + (Number(row[BILL_COLS_.TOTAL_PAYABLE - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2),
    'Due Date: ' + new Date(row[BILL_COLS_.DUE_DATE - 1]).toDateString(),
    'Status: ' + row[BILL_COLS_.PAYMENT_STATUS - 1]
  ];

  var result = _generateAndSavePdf_('Maintenance Bill', bodyLines, 'Bills', 'BILL_' + billId + '.pdf');

  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, BILL_COLS_.PDF_URL).setValue(result.url);
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, BILL_COLS_.HASH).setValue(result.hash);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'BILL_PDF_GENERATED', SHEETS_.MAINTENANCE_BILLS, billId, result.url, '');
  return { billId: billId, pdfUrl: result.url, hash: result.hash };
}
Add to Part 0's WebApp.gs:
function clientGenerateBillPdf(token, billId) {
  return Billing.generateBillPdf(token, billId);
}
