Good ? this is a real, known Apps Script authorization quirk, not a bug in the logic. Using DocumentApp for the first time requires a new OAuth scope (documents) that wasn't part of the original authorization when you first ran/deployed this project ? Apps Script needs to ask you for that additional permission, and the "400" you got is Google's authorization flow breaking when triggered indirectly through a google.script.run call from inside a Web App page rather than directly from the editor.
Fix: authorize the new scope directly from the Apps Script editor first, not through the browser UI.
Steps:
1. In Part 2's Apps Script editor, temporarily create and run this tiny function directly (select it, click Run):
function test_authorize_documentapp() {
  var doc = DocumentApp.create('AUTH_TEST_DELETE_ME');
  Logger.log('Created: ' + doc.getId());
  DriveApp.getFileById(doc.getId()).setTrashed(true);
  Logger.log('Trashed successfully. Authorization complete.');
}
2. This will trigger Apps Script's normal in-editor authorization dialog (a proper Google popup, not the broken redirect the Web App triggered) ? a fresh permission request naming DocumentApp/Drive. Review and click Allow.
3. Once authorized this way, the same permission carries over to the deployed Web App too ? Apps Script authorizations are per-project, not per-entry-point.

function _getDocumentsFolder_(subFolderName) {
  var rootFolderId = Gateway.getSetting('DOCUMENTS_ROOT_FOLDER_ID');
  if (!rootFolderId) throw new Error('DOCUMENTS_ROOT_FOLDER_ID is not set in Global_Settings.');
  var root = DriveApp.getFolderById(rootFolderId);
  return _getOrCreateFolder_(root, subFolderName);
}

Now extending the same proven pattern to receipts ? this is where L-25's void-filename convention actually matters, so it's built in from the start rather than bolted on later.
Add to ReceiptService.gs (append this function):
/** Generates and attaches a PDF for an existing receipt. Call AFTER recordPayment(). */
function generateReceiptPdf(sessionToken, receiptNo) {
  var session = _authorize_(sessionToken, 'RECORD_RECEIPT');
  var sheet = getSheet_(SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS);
  var data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  var rowIndex = -1, row = null;
  for (var r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (data[r][RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_NO - 1] === receiptNo) { rowIndex = r + 1; row = data[r]; break; }
  }
  if (!row) throw new Error('Receipt ' + receiptNo + ' not found.');

  var bodyLines = [
    'Receipt No: ' + receiptNo,
    'Flat: ' + row[RECEIPT_COLS_.FLAT_ID - 1],
    'Against Bill: ' + row[RECEIPT_COLS_.BILL_ID - 1],
    'Payment Date: ' + new Date(row[RECEIPT_COLS_.PAYMENT_DATE - 1]).toDateString(),
    'Payment Mode: ' + row[RECEIPT_COLS_.PAYMENT_MODE - 1],
    'Amount Paid: ?' + (Number(row[RECEIPT_COLS_.PAID_AMOUNT - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2),
    'Concession: ?' + (Number(row[RECEIPT_COLS_.CONCESSION - 1]) / 100).toFixed(2) +
      (Number(row[RECEIPT_COLS_.CONCESSION - 1]) > 0 ? ' (' + row[RECEIPT_COLS_.ADVICE_NOTE - 1] + ')' : ''),
    'Status: ' + row[RECEIPT_COLS_.RECEIPT_STATUS - 1]
  ];

  var result = _generateAndSavePdf_('Payment Receipt', bodyLines, 'Receipts', 'RECEIPT_' + receiptNo + '.pdf');

  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, RECEIPT_COLS_.PDF_URL).setValue(result.url);
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, RECEIPT_COLS_.HASH).setValue(result.hash);

  logAudit_(session.userId, session.role, 'RECEIPT_PDF_GENERATED', SHEETS_.PAYMENT_RECEIPTS, receiptNo, result.url, '');
  return { receiptNo: receiptNo, pdfUrl: result.url, hash: result.hash };
}
Now the L-25 piece ? amend voidReceipt() (already exists in ReceiptService.gs) to rename the PDF file when a receipt is voided. Find the line that sets RECEIPT_STATUS to 'VOID' and add this right after it:
  // L-25: if a PDF already exists for this receipt, rename it with _VOID appended.
  var existingPdfUrl = receiptRow[RECEIPT_COLS_.PDF_URL - 1];
  if (existingPdfUrl) {
    var fileIdMatch = existingPdfUrl.match(/[-\w]{25,}/); // extracts the Drive file ID from a standard Drive URL
    if (fileIdMatch) {
      var pdfFile = DriveApp.getFileById(fileIdMatch[0]);
      var currentName = pdfFile.getName();
      if (currentName.indexOf('_VOID') === -1) { // never double-append on repeated calls
        pdfFile.setName(currentName.replace('.pdf', '_VOID.pdf'));
      }
    }
  }

function clientGenerateReceiptPdf(token, receiptNo) {

}
Update doRecordPayment()'s success handler in index.html to offer PDF generation per receipt, same pattern as bills:
    function doRecordPayment() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('rcptFlatId').value.trim();
      var amountRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptAmount').value);
      var mode = document.getElementById('rcptMode').value;
      var concessionRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptConcession').value) || 0;
      var note = document.getElementById('rcptNote').value;

      if (!amountRupees || amountRupees <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Please enter a valid amount in Rupees.</span>';
        return;
      }

      var confirmMsg = 'Record payment of ?' + amountRupees.toFixed(2) + ' for flat ' + flatId +
        (concessionRupees > 0 ? ' with a concession of ?' + concessionRupees.toFixed(2) : '') +
        '?\n\nThis cannot be edited afterward, only voided.';
      if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return;

      var amountPaise = Math.round(amountRupees * 100);
      var concessionPaise = Math.round(concessionRupees * 100);

      document.getElementById('rcptResult').textContent = 'Recording...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          var pdfButtons = result.receiptNos.map(function (rNo) {
            return '<div>' + rNo + ' <button onclick="doGenerateReceiptPdf(\'' + rNo + '\', this)">Generate PDF</button> <span class="pdf-status-' + rNo + '"></span></div>';
          }).join('');
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Receipt(s) recorded' +
            (result.creditCreated ? ' ? Credit created: ' + rupees(result.creditCreated.amountPaise) : '') + '</span>' +
            pdfButtons;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordPayment(currentSession.token, flatId, amountPaise, mode, concessionPaise, note);
    }

    function doGenerateReceiptPdf(receiptNo, btnEl) {
      btnEl.disabled = true;
      btnEl.textContent = 'Generating...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          btnEl.outerHTML = '<a href="' + result.pdfUrl + '" target="_blank">View PDF</a>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btnEl.disabled = false;
          btnEl.textContent = 'Generate PDF';
          alert('PDF generation failed: ' + error.message);
        })
        .clientGenerateReceiptPdf(currentSession.token, receiptNo);
    }

  <script>
    var currentSession = null;

    function showScreen(id) {
      document.querySelectorAll('.screen').forEach(function (el) { el.classList.remove('active'); });
      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('active');
    }
    function setError(elId, msg) { document.getElementById(elId).textContent = msg; }

    function doLogin() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('mobileInput').value.trim();
      var password = document.getElementById('passwordInput').value;
      setError('errorMsg', '');
      if (!mobile || !password) { setError('errorMsg', 'Please enter both mobile number and password.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('loginBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Logging in...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (session) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          currentSession = session;
          if (session.forcePasswordReset) { showScreen('passwordChangeScreen'); return; }
          showDashboard(session);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Log In';
          setError('errorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientLogin(mobile, password);
    }

    function doChangePassword() {
      var newPw = document.getElementById('newPasswordInput').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('confirmPasswordInput').value;
      setError('pwErrorMsg', '');
      if (newPw.length < 8) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Password must be at least 8 characters.'); return; }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) { setError('pwErrorMsg', 'Passwords do not match.'); return; }

      var btn = document.getElementById('changePwBtn');
      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = 'Saving...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          currentSession.forcePasswordReset = false;
          showDashboard(currentSession);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btn.disabled = false; btn.textContent = 'Set Password';
          setError('pwErrorMsg', error.message);
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, '', newPw);
    }

    function showDashboard(session) {
      showScreen('dashboard');
      document.getElementById('welcomeText').textContent =
        'Logged in as ' + session.role + (session.flatId ? ' (Flat ' + session.flatId + ')' : '') + '.';

      document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('caView').style.display = 'none';

      if (session.role === 'RESIDENT') {
        document.getElementById('residentView').style.display = 'block';
        loadMyBalance();
      } else if (session.role === 'ADMIN' || session.role === 'COMMITTEE') {
        document.getElementById('adminView').style.display = 'block';
      } else if (session.role === 'CA') {
        document.getElementById('caView').style.display = 'block';
      }
    }

    function loadMyBalance() {
      document.getElementById('balanceContent').textContent = 'Loading...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (balance) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML =
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Billed</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalBilled) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Total Paid</span><span>' + rupees(balance.totalPaid) + '</span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span><b>Outstanding</b></span><span><b>' + rupees(balance.outstanding) + '</b></span></div>' +
            '<div class="balanceRow"><span>Credit Balance</span><span>' + rupees(balance.creditBalance) + '</span></div>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('balanceContent').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientViewOwnBalance(currentSession.token);
    }

    function rupees(paise) { return '?' + (Number(paise) / 100).toFixed(2); }

    function doGenerateBill() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('billFlatId').value.trim();
      var monthYear = document.getElementById('billMonthYear').value.trim();
      var dueDateStr = document.getElementById('billDueDate').value;
      document.getElementById('billResult').textContent = 'Generating...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Bill ' + result.billId + ' ? Total: ' + rupees(result.totalPayable) + '</span>' +
            '<br><button onclick="doGenerateBillPdf(\'' + result.billId + '\')">Generate PDF</button>' +
            '<div id="billPdfResult"></div>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('billResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientGenerateBill(currentSession.token, flatId, monthYear, dueDateStr);
    }

    function doGenerateBillPdf(billId) {
      document.getElementById('billPdfResult').textContent = 'Generating PDF...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('billPdfResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok"><a href="' + result.pdfUrl + '" target="_blank">View PDF</a></span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('billPdfResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientGenerateBillPdf(currentSession.token, billId);
    }

    function doRecordPayment() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('rcptFlatId').value.trim();
      var amountRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptAmount').value);
      var mode = document.getElementById('rcptMode').value;
      var concessionRupees = parseFloat(document.getElementById('rcptConcession').value) || 0;
      var note = document.getElementById('rcptNote').value;

      if (!amountRupees || amountRupees <= 0) {
        document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Please enter a valid amount in Rupees.</span>';
        return;
      }

      var confirmMsg = 'Record payment of ?' + amountRupees.toFixed(2) + ' for flat ' + flatId +
        (concessionRupees > 0 ? ' with a concession of ?' + concessionRupees.toFixed(2) : '') +
        '?\n\nThis cannot be edited afterward, only voided.';
      if (!confirm(confirmMsg)) return;

      var amountPaise = Math.round(amountRupees * 100);
      var concessionPaise = Math.round(concessionRupees * 100);

      document.getElementById('rcptResult').textContent = 'Recording...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          var pdfButtons = result.receiptNos.map(function (rNo) {
            return '<div>' + rNo + ' <button onclick="doGenerateReceiptPdf(\'' + rNo + '\', this)">Generate PDF</button></div>';
          }).join('');
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Receipt(s) recorded' +
            (result.creditCreated ? ' ? Credit created: ' + rupees(result.creditCreated.amountPaise) : '') + '</span>' +
            pdfButtons;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('rcptResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordPayment(currentSession.token, flatId, amountPaise, mode, concessionPaise, note);
    }

    function doGenerateReceiptPdf(receiptNo, btnEl) {
      btnEl.disabled = true;
      btnEl.textContent = 'Generating...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          btnEl.outerHTML = '<a href="' + result.pdfUrl + '" target="_blank">View PDF</a>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          btnEl.disabled = false;
          btnEl.textContent = 'Generate PDF';
          alert('PDF generation failed: ' + error.message);
        })
        .clientGenerateReceiptPdf(currentSession.token, receiptNo);
    }

    function doRaiseComplaint() {
      var category = document.getElementById('cmpCategory').value.trim();
      var title = document.getElementById('cmpTitle').value.trim();
      var description = document.getElementById('cmpDescription').value.trim();
      var priority = document.getElementById('cmpPriority').value;

      if (!title) {
        document.getElementById('cmpResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Please enter a title.</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('cmpResult').textContent = 'Submitting...';

      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('cmpResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Complaint ' + result.complaintId + ' submitted.</span>';
          document.getElementById('cmpCategory').value = '';
          document.getElementById('cmpTitle').value = '';
          document.getElementById('cmpDescription').value = '';

    function doListComplaints() {
      document.getElementById('complaintsList').textContent = 'Loading...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (list) {
          if (list.length === 0) {
            document.getElementById('complaintsList').textContent = 'No complaints found.';
            return;
          }
          var html = '';
          list.forEach(function (c) {
            html += '<div style="border-bottom:1px solid #eee;padding:4px 0;">' +
              '<b>' + c.complaintId + '</b> ? ' + c.title + ' (' + c.flatId + ', ' + c.priority + ', <b>' + c.status + '</b>)' +
              (c.assignedTo ? ' ? assigned to ' + c.assignedTo : '') + '</div>';
          });
          document.getElementById('complaintsList').innerHTML = html;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('complaintsList').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientListComplaints(currentSession.token, '');
    }

    function doSelfChangePassword() {
      var oldPw = document.getElementById('selfOldPassword').value;
      var newPw = document.getElementById('selfNewPassword').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('selfConfirmPassword').value;

      if (newPw.length < 8) {
        document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">New password must be at least 8 characters.</span>';
        return;
      }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) {
        document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">New passwords do not match.</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').textContent = 'Changing...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Password changed successfully.</span>';
          document.getElementById('selfOldPassword').value = '';
          document.getElementById('selfNewPassword').value = '';
          document.getElementById('selfConfirmPassword').value = '';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('selfChangePwResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientChangePassword(currentSession.token, oldPw, newPw);
    }

      google.script.run

    function doCompleteForgotPassword() {
      var mobile = document.getElementById('fpMobile').value.trim();
      var otpCode = document.getElementById('fpOtpCode').value.trim();
      var newPw = document.getElementById('fpNewPassword').value;
      var confirmPw = document.getElementById('fpConfirmPassword').value;

      if (newPw.length < 8) {
        document.getElementById('fpCompleteResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Password must be at least 8 characters.</span>';
        return;
      }
      if (newPw !== confirmPw) {
        document.getElementById('fpCompleteResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">Passwords do not match.</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('fpCompleteResult').textContent = 'Resetting...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function () {
          document.getElementById('fpCompleteResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Password reset. Please log in with your new password.</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('fpCompleteResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientCompleteForgotPassword(mobile, otpCode, newPw);
    }

    function doRecordTransfer() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('xferFlatId').value.trim();
      var prevOwner = document.getElementById('xferPrevOwner').value.trim();
      var newOwner = document.getElementById('xferNewOwner').value.trim();
      var duesCleared = document.getElementById('xferDuesCleared').value || 0;
      var writtenOff = document.getElementById('xferWrittenOff').value || 0;
      var approvedBy = document.getElementById('xferApprovedBy').value.trim();

      if (Number(writtenOff) > 0 && !approvedBy) {
        document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">A write-off amount requires a named approver (L-38).</span>';
        return;
      }

      document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').textContent = 'Recording...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="ok">Transfer recorded: ' + result.transferId + '</span>';
          document.getElementById('xferTransferId').value = result.transferId;
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('xferRecordResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientRecordOwnershipTransfer(currentSession.token, flatId, prevOwner, newOwner, duesCleared, writtenOff, approvedBy);
    }

    function doTransferOccupancy() {
      var flatId = document.getElementById('xferFlatId').value.trim();
      var newOwner = document.getElementById('xferNewOwner').value.trim();
      var newOwnerMobile = document.getElementById('xferNewOwnerMobile').value.trim();
      var newOwnerEmail = document.getElementById('xferNewOwnerEmail').value.trim();
      var reason = document.getElementById('xferReason').value.trim();
      var transferId = document.getElementById('xferTransferId').value.trim();

      if (!confirm('Finalize ownership transfer for ' + flatId + ' to ' + newOwner + '? This closes the previous occupancy record permanently (still visible, but no longer current).')) return;

      document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').textContent = 'Transferring...';
      google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
          document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').innerHTML =
            '<span class="ok">Closed: ' + result.closedOccupancyId + ', New: ' + result.newOccupancyId + '</span>';
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
          document.getElementById('xferOccupancyResult').innerHTML = '<span class="err">' + error.message + '</span>';
        })
        .clientTransferOccupancy(currentSession.token, flatId, newOwner, newOwnerMobile, newOwnerEmail, reason, transferId);
    }

    function updateFlatIdPreview() {
      var wing = document.getElementById('newFlatWing').value.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
      var flatNo = document.getElementById('newFlatNo').value.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/ /g, '_');
      document.getElementById('flatIdPreview').textContent = (wing && flatNo) ? (wing + '-' + flatNo) : '?';
    }

since long we have been building this. Now I realised that google sheet backend is extremely slow to operate, I have been trying my hands in github. supabase, render integration. I will continue this project here in this chat. can you branch the chat to work with same architecture and diligence to build through this options.

Here's my recommendation, with the reasoning, since you're trusting me to pick:
Backend: Node.js + Express. Supabase's client libraries are first-class in JavaScript/TypeScript (the Node ecosystem is where Supabase's own docs and community examples are richest), it deploys on Render with zero friction, and ? practically ? it's the stack I can write and debug most reliably for you, which matters given how much diagnosis we've had to do together already. Python + FastAPI is a fine alternative in the abstract, but there's no real advantage for this project's needs (no data science, no ML), so I'd rather not trade away Node's tighter Supabase integration for no real gain.
Frontend: React (via Vite). This is a real change from the vanilla HTML/JS we used for the Sheets version ? and worth it. A lot of our worst bugs this session (the stray brace, the orphaned code block, three duplicate password-change cards) came directly from hand-editing one giant HTML file with plain string concatenation. React's component model makes that entire failure class much harder to hit by construction, and it scales far better as the number of screens grows.
One structural simplification worth flagging now, not silently adopting: the five-workbook split (Gateway/Members/Billing/Accounting/Complaints) existed specifically because Apps Script's sandboxing forced hard boundaries between separate script projects. Postgres removes that constraint entirely ? a single database with proper foreign keys and real transactions doesn't need to be physically split into five workbooks to keep data separated; Row-Level Security and the API layer's own RBAC checks (still enforcing L-04 exactly as before) can achieve the same access boundaries within one unified backend codebase, organized internally into modules (auth/, members/, billing/, accounting/, complaints/) rather than five separate deployed services. This is simpler to build, simpler to deploy on Render (one web service, not five), and loses none of the access discipline we locked in Section 4 ? it's a genuine improvement enabled by the new stack, not a corner cut.
Proposed sequence, same single-track discipline as before:
1. GitHub repo scaffold (backend + frontend folders)
2. Supabase project + Postgres schema, translated from our existing Section 5 schema (the actual table structures carry over almost directly ? Sheets tabs become Postgres tables)
3. Backend API, built module by module, each one re-implementing an already-proven L-01?L-41 rule, tested before moving to the next
4. React frontend, built against the now-tested API
Confirm this direction, and I'll start with the GitHub repo structure and the Postgres schema next.


Important question to ask before we commit ? let me verify the specifics rather than guess, since this could make or break the whole plan.Good, this confirms exactly the concern I had, and it's worth being direct about it rather than glossing over it.
The honest situation: official Node.js dropped Windows 7 support after v13.14.0 ? v14.0.0 onward requires Windows 8 or newer. That's a real wall, and it matters a lot here: Vite (the standard modern React tooling) requires Node 18+, which simply won't run on Windows 7 through official channels. There are unofficial community-patched builds ? someone maintains Node 18.20.2 builds with Windows 7 support ? but that's an unofficial, third-party-patched runtime for a project this financially sensitive, which I'd be genuinely uneasy recommending as your development foundation.
There's a second, quieter problem too: even if Node itself ran, your actual browser on Windows 7 is the bigger risk ? Chrome and Firefox have both been dropping active support for Windows 7 for a while now, meaning the browser you'd test the new React frontend in is itself increasingly unmaintained and missing modern JS features the frontend might rely on.
My honest recommendation, given this: don't try to make React/Node run natively on your Windows 7 machine. Two realistic paths instead:
1. Cloud-based development ? since you're already comfortable working through a browser (which is basically what we did with Apps Script this whole time), a service like GitHub Codespaces or StackBlitz runs the actual Node environment in the cloud; your Windows 7 machine just needs a browser to view and edit through. This is close to zero setup friction and sidesteps the Windows 7 problem entirely.
2. Simplify the frontend choice ? if cloud development feels like an extra layer of complexity you don't want, we could skip React and keep the frontend as plain HTML/JS/CSS, same as before, served directly by the Node/Express backend. Plain HTML/JS has no Node-version requirement to run ? it only needs Node to build/serve, and even that could run entirely server-side on Render, with you only ever touching the frontend files as static text (edited anywhere, even a plain text editor) rather than running a dev toolchain locally at all.
Given everything we've been through together, I'd lean toward option 2 ? it removes an entire category of "does my machine support the tooling" risk, and the discipline we've built (careful, verified, complete-file edits) works just as well with plain HTML/JS as it did with Apps Script's HTML. React's benefits mostly matter at a scale of complexity we may not need, and Windows 7 compatibility is a real, not hypothetical, constraint on your side.
Which direction feels right to you?

Good instinct ? check the real, current state directly rather than guessing from names alone. Here's a query that gives you both the table names and their exact column structures in one shot.
Run this in Supabase's SQL Editor:
SELECT
  t.table_name,
  c.column_name,
  c.data_type,
  c.is_nullable,
  c.column_default
FROM information_schema.tables t
JOIN information_schema.columns c
  ON t.table_name = c.table_name
WHERE t.table_schema = 'public'
  AND t.table_type = 'BASE TABLE'
ORDER BY t.table_name, c.ordinal_position;
What this does: lists every table in your public schema (the one your own tables live in, not Supabase's internal system tables), alongside each column's name, data type, whether it allows NULL, and any default value ? ordered table by table, column by column in the order they were created.
Run it and paste the results here ? that gives me the real, current structure to work from, rather than relying on the old SQL files' names (which may or may not match what actually got created, given the project's history of things not always matching what was intended).


Good ? now I have the real, exact structure to work from, and it confirms this old schema should be discarded: no users table at all (so login was probably never actually implemented), several tables reference integer foreign keys with no visible constraints enforcing them, and none of this reflects any of the locked business rules (L-01 through L-41) we already have proven ? no RBAC, no session handling, no double-entry integrity checks, no audit trail structure matching what we built.
Run this in Supabase's SQL Editor to clear it all out in one shot:
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS
  audit_log,
  bills,
  chart_of_accounts,
  complaint_actions,
  complaints,
  flats,
  ledger_entries,
  notices,
  other_receivables,
  ownership_transfer,
  payments,
  receipts,
  reserve_fund,
  suggestions,
  trial_balance
CASCADE;
What CASCADE does here, so it's not a surprise: it also drops anything depending on these tables (foreign key constraints, and the auto-generated ID sequences like bills_bill_id_seq) ? exactly what we want for a full, clean reset, not a partial one that leaves orphaned sequences behind.
Run it, then run this to confirm the public schema is genuinely empty before we design the fresh schema:
SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema = 'public';
Share that result ? once confirmed empty, I'll design the real Postgres schema next, translated properly from our locked Section 5 design (the 22-sheet structure across five modules), with actual foreign keys, actual constraints enforcing L-01?L-41 where the database itself can help enforce them (e.g., CHECK constraints, NOT NULL, proper REFERENCES), not just application-code discipline alone.




-- =====================================================================
-- RANIKUTHI V5 ? Postgres schema (Supabase)
-- Translated from the locked Section 5 design (Google Sheets era) into
-- ONE unified database. Organized internally into 5 modules ? auth,
-- members, billing, accounting, complaints ? matching the old five
-- workbooks, but now a single Postgres DB with real FKs/transactions
-- instead of five sandboxed Apps Script projects (per the locked
-- architecture decision: Postgres removes the need for physical
-- workbook separation; RBAC + the API layer preserve the same access
-- boundaries).
--
-- Every table below is commented with the L-xx clause(s) it implements.
-- Money is stored as BIGINT paise everywhere (never NUMERIC/FLOAT) ?
-- L-30 (paise always rounds up) is enforced in application code at the
-- point of calculation, not in the DB, since ceiling-rounding is a
-- calculation-time rule, not a storage-time one.
--
-- This script is idempotent for a fresh rebuild: it drops the old,
-- unrelated tables you already found in Supabase (audit_log, bills,
-- chart_of_accounts, etc. from the discarded Flask/Python attempt) and
-- creates the real V5 schema in their place. Safe to re-run.
-- =====================================================================

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 0. Extensions
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
create extension if not exists pgcrypto;   -- gen_random_uuid()

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
-- 0.1 Clear the old, unrelated tables from the discarded Flask attempt
-- (idempotent ? safe even if already dropped)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------
drop table if exists
  audit_log, bills, chart_of_accounts, complaint_actions, complaints,
  flats, ledger_entries, notices, other_receivables, ownership_transfer,
  payments, receipts, reserve_fund, suggestions, trial_balance
cascade;

-- =====================================================================
-- MODULE: auth  (was Part 0 ? Gateway/Identity)
-- =====================================================================

-- L-04: RBAC is data, not code. roles is a lookup table (not a hardcoded
-- CHECK/enum) so new roles can be added without a schema migration.
create table roles (
  role_name   text primary key,
  description text
);

-- L-01 (mobile-only login), L-02 (lockout), L-03 (session timeouts),
-- L-36 (admin hard-reset + email OTP)
create table users (
  id                     uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  flat_id                uuid,                         -- fk added after `flats` exists
  full_name              text not null,
  mobile_number          text not null unique,         -- L-01: the ONLY login identifier
  email_address          text,                          -- OTP delivery only (L-36) ? never login (L-01)
  role_name              text not null references roles(role_name),
  account_status         text not null default 'ACTIVE' check (account_status in ('ACTIVE','LOCKED','DISABLED')),
  password_hash          text not null,
  password_salt          text not null,
  force_password_reset   boolean not null default false,
  failed_login_count     int not null default 0,
  locked_until           timestamptz,                   -- L-02: 15-min lockout
  active_session_token   text,
  session_issued_at      timestamptz,                   -- L-03: anchor for the 12h ABSOLUTE timeout
  last_activity_timestamp timestamptz,                  -- L-03: anchor for the 30-min INACTIVITY timeout
  last_login_timestamp   timestamptz,
  created_timestamp      timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by             uuid references users(id),
  updated_timestamp      timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- L-26: at least two active Admins at all times ? enforced in the API layer
-- (a DB constraint can't easily express "at least 2 rows of X remain after
-- this update", so this stays an application-level check before any
-- ADMIN deactivation/role-change commits).

-- L-05, L-07: structured mode choices (not just plain values)
create table global_settings (
  setting_key      text primary key,
  setting_value    text not null,
  value_type       text not null check (value_type in ('STRING','NUMBER','BOOLEAN','JSON')),
  editable_by_role text references roles(role_name),
  description      text,
  updated_timestamp timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_by       uuid references users(id)
);

-- L-04: direct implementation of role-permission mapping
create table roles_permission_matrix (
  id             uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  role_name      text not null references roles(role_name),
  permission_key text not null,
  allowed        boolean not null default true,
  workbook_scope text not null default 'ALL', -- e.g. 'AUTH' / 'MEMBERS' / 'BILLING' / 'ACCOUNTING' / 'COMPLAINTS' / 'ALL'
  notes          text,
  unique (role_name, permission_key, workbook_scope)
);

-- L-36: OTP purposes broadened from V4.1's single reset log
create table otp_log (
  id                 uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id            uuid not null references users(id),
  purpose            text not null check (purpose in ('FIRST_LOGIN','ADMIN_HARD_RESET','SELF_SERVICE_RESET')),
  delivery_channel   text not null default 'EMAIL' check (delivery_channel = 'EMAIL'), -- L-36: email only, never SMS/WhatsApp API
  otp_hash           text not null,
  requested_timestamp timestamptz not null default now(),
  expiry_timestamp   timestamptz not null,
  verified_timestamp timestamptz,
  status             text not null default 'PENDING' check (status in ('PENDING','VERIFIED','EXPIRED')),
  requested_by       uuid references users(id)
);

-- L-27 (peer reset) vs L-36 (admin hard-reset) ? same mechanism, distinct
-- authority level recorded via actor_role
create table admin_reset_utility (
  id                        uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  actor_user_id             uuid not null references users(id),
  actor_role                text not null references roles(role_name),
  target_user_id            uuid not null references users(id),
  reset_timestamp           timestamptz not null default now(),
  force_password_reset_set  boolean not null default true,
  otp_reference             uuid references otp_log(id),
  status                    text not null default 'COMPLETED' check (status in ('COMPLETED','FAILED'))
);

-- L-28: justification_note mandatory for BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY, nullable otherwise
create table system_audit_trail (
  id                uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  ts                timestamptz not null default now(),
  actor_user_id     uuid references users(id),
  actor_role        text references roles(role_name),
  action_type       text not null,
  target_module     text not null,          -- 'AUTH' / 'MEMBERS' / 'BILLING' / 'ACCOUNTING' / 'COMPLAINTS'
  target_table      text not null,
  record_key        text,
  change_details    jsonb,
  correlation_id    uuid,
  justification_note text,
  constraint break_glass_needs_justification check (
    action_type <> 'BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY' or justification_note is not null
  )
);

create table read_audit_log (
  id             uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  item_type      text not null,
  item_id        text not null,
  user_id        uuid not null references users(id),
  read_timestamp timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- L-41: backup rotation tiers ? DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY never mix with YEAR_END,
-- which is the one tier that never rotates out
create table backup_log (
  id                    uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  backup_timestamp      timestamptz not null default now(),
  backup_type           text not null check (backup_type in ('DAILY','WEEKLY','MONTHLY','YEAR_END','PRE_SCHEMA_CHANGE')),
  source                text not null default 'SUPABASE_POSTGRES', -- was Source_Workbook; one DB now, kept for continuity
  storage_location      text not null,       -- e.g. a Supabase Storage path or external bucket URL
  sha256_hash           text not null,
  verification_status   text not null default 'PENDING_VERIFICATION' check (verification_status in ('PENDING_VERIFICATION','VERIFIED','FAILED')),
  verification_timestamp timestamptz
);

-- =====================================================================
-- MODULE: members  (was Part 1 ? Members/Flat Directory)
-- =====================================================================

create table flats (
  id                       uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  flat_code                text not null unique,   -- e.g. 'A-101' ? the human-facing Flat_ID
  wing                     text,
  flat_no                  text,
  area_sqft                numeric(10,2),
  floor                    text,
  flat_type                text,
  rate_mode_override       text check (rate_mode_override in ('FLAT_SPECIFIC','GLOBAL_FLAT','PER_SQFT') or rate_mode_override is null),
  rate_value_override_paise bigint,
  active_flag              boolean not null default true,
  created_timestamp        timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by               uuid references users(id),
  updated_timestamp        timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Now that `flats` exists, wire up the deferred FK on users
alter table users
  add constraint users_flat_id_fkey foreign key (flat_id) references flats(id);

-- Occupancy ? Is_Current flags the live owner/tenant record per flat
create table occupancy (
  id                 uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  flat_id            uuid not null references flats(id),
  owner_name         text,
  owner_mobile       text,
  owner_email        text,
  tenant_name        text,
  tenant_mobile      text,
  tenant_email       text,
  occupancy_status   text not null check (occupancy_status in ('OWNER_OCCUPIED','TENANTED','VACANT')),
  is_current         boolean not null default true,
  effective_from     date not null default current_date,
  effective_to       date,
  closed_reason      text,
  created_timestamp  timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by         uuid references users(id)
);

-- Only one current occupancy row per flat at a time
create unique index occupancy_one_current_per_flat
  on occupancy (flat_id) where (is_current);

-- L-38: ownership transfer ? previous owner's Occupancy_Log record is
-- closed, never deleted; write-offs post to 60300 via accounting module
create table ownership_transfer_log (
  id                            uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  flat_id                       uuid not null references flats(id),
  previous_owner_name           text not null,
  new_owner_name                text not null,
  dues_cleared_amount_paise     bigint not null default 0,
  written_off_amount_paise      bigint not null default 0,
  write_off_approved_by         uuid references users(id),
  transfer_date                 date not null default current_date,
  linked_occupancy_id_closed    uuid references occupancy(id),
  linked_occupancy_id_new       uuid references occupancy(id),
  created_timestamp             timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- =====================================================================
-- SHARED: sequence_counters (was Part 2, sheet 9) ? but genuinely
-- cross-module, since bills/receipts/vouchers/ledger all need gap-free
-- numbering (L-13). Lives once, used by billing + accounting.
-- =====================================================================

create table sequence_counters (
  counter_key       text not null,      -- e.g. 'BILL', 'RECEIPT', 'VOUCHER', 'MISC_RECEIPT'
  financial_year    text not null,      -- e.g. '2026-27', matching L-39 (1 Jul ? 30 Jun)
  last_value        bigint not null default 0,
  updated_timestamp timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (counter_key, financial_year)
);

-- L-13: atomic, gap-free increment. Call via
--   select next_sequence_value('BILL', '2026-27');
-- inside the SAME transaction as the insert that consumes it ? this
-- takes the place of Apps Script's LockService.
create or replace function next_sequence_value(p_key text, p_fy text)
returns bigint as $$
declare v_next bigint;
begin
  insert into sequence_counters (counter_key, financial_year, last_value)
  values (p_key, p_fy, 1)
  on conflict (counter_key, financial_year)
  do update set last_value = sequence_counters.last_value + 1,
                updated_timestamp = now()
  returning last_value into v_next;
  return v_next;
end;
$$ language plpgsql;
-- NOTE: this function must be called from within the same DB transaction
-- as the row it numbers, and that transaction must actually commit the
-- insert ? a rolled-back transaction after calling this WILL burn a
-- number (a gap), same tradeoff the Apps Script LockService version had.
-- Application code must not call this speculatively / for preview.

-- =====================================================================
-- MODULE: billing  (was Part 2 ? Billing & Financial Log, committee-only)
-- =====================================================================

create table vendor_master (
  id                uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  vendor_name       text not null,
  gstin             text,
  pan               text,
  contact_person    text,
  mobile            text,
  email             text,
  active_flag       boolean not null default true,
  created_timestamp timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by        uuid references users(id)
);

-- L-06 (billing idempotency), L-37 (universal rate, no bill-level discount)
create table maintenance_bills (
  id                     uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  bill_no                text not null unique,           -- generated via next_sequence_value('BILL', fy)
  flat_id                uuid not null references flats(id),
  bill_month_year        text not null,                   -- e.g. '2026-09'
  financial_year         text not null,
  base_charge_paise      bigint not null,
  previous_unpaid_paise  bigint not null default 0,
  late_fine_paise        bigint not null default 0,
  total_payable_paise    bigint not null,
  due_date               date not null,
  payment_status         text not null default 'UNPAID' check (payment_status in ('UNPAID','PARTIAL','PAID','OVERDUE')),
  pdf_drive_url          text,
  sha256_hash            text,
  created_timestamp      timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by             uuid references users(id),
  record_version         int not null default 1,
  unique (flat_id, bill_month_year)          -- L-06: exactly one authoritative bill per flat per period
);

-- L-19: puja/donations ? first-class, separate from maintenance
create table misc_bills (
  id                uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  flat_id           uuid references flats(id),
  category          text not null,
  description       text,
  amount_paise      bigint not null,
  due_date          date,
  payment_status    text not null default 'UNPAID' check (payment_status in ('UNPAID','PARTIAL','PAID')),
  pdf_drive_url     text,
  sha256_hash       text,
  created_timestamp timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by        uuid references users(id)
);

-- L-08 (receipt waterfall + running part-payment history),
-- L-09/L-32 (Member Credit), L-10 (concession needs Advice Note),
-- L-11 (immutable ? void/reversal only)
create table payment_receipts (
  id                       uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  receipt_no               text not null unique,     -- next_sequence_value('RECEIPT', fy)
  bill_id                  uuid not null references maintenance_bills(id),
  flat_id                  uuid not null references flats(id),
  payment_date             date not null default current_date,
  payment_mode             text not null,
  paid_amount_paise        bigint not null check (paid_amount_paise >= 0),
  fine_applied_paise       bigint not null default 0,
  concession_paise         bigint not null default 0,
  committee_advice_note    text,
  balance_remaining_paise  bigint not null default 0,
  receipt_status           text not null default 'ACTIVE' check (receipt_status in ('ACTIVE','VOID')),
  void_reason              text,
  pdf_drive_url            text,
  sha256_hash              text,
  logged_by                uuid references users(id),
  created_timestamp        timestamptz not null default now(),
  record_version           int not null default 1,
  reversal_receipt_no      text,
  -- L-10: any non-zero concession requires a Committee Advice Note reference
  constraint concession_requires_advice_note check (
    concession_paise = 0 or committee_advice_note is not null
  )
);

create table misc_receipts (
  id                uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  misc_receipt_no   text not null unique,     -- next_sequence_value('MISC_RECEIPT', fy)
  misc_bill_id      uuid references misc_bills(id),
  payer_name        text not null,
  flat_id           uuid references flats(id),
  payment_date      date not null default current_date,
  payment_mode      text not null,
  amount_paise      bigint not null,
  category          text not null,

  pdf_drive_url     text,

-- L-09 (liability, never income), L-32 (refund procedure, FIFO application)
create table member_credits (

    checker_user_id is null or checker_user_id <> maker_user_id
  )

-- L-15/L-16: the ONLY write path from billing into the ledger.
-- Billing (this module) never writes ledger_entries directly; a backend
-- job/trigger consumes PENDING rows here and posts them in `accounting`.
create table pending_transactions (
  id                     uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  created_timestamp      timestamptz not null default now(),
  state                  text not null default 'PENDING' check (state in ('PENDING','PROCESSING','POSTED','FAILED','COMPENSATED')),
  source_type            text not null,        -- e.g. 'BILL_GENERATED','RECEIPT','MISC_RECEIPT','EXPENSE_VOUCHER','CREDIT_REFUND'
  source_record_id       uuid,
  flat_id                uuid references flats(id),
  amount_paise           bigint not null,
  payload                jsonb not null,        -- whatever accounting needs to build the journal entry
  actor_user_id          uuid references users(id),
  completed_timestamp    timestamptz,
  error_message          text,
  compensation_action    text                    -- L-16: defined compensating action if a later step fails
);

-- can never be posted twice, even if the consumer runs more than once)
create table ledger_entries (

  source_record_id      uuid,

  reversal_of_ledger_id uuid references ledger_entries(id),
  created_timestamp     timestamptz not null default now(),
  created_by            uuid references users(id),
  unique (journal_id)   -- L-14 idempotency: one journal, one posting, ever
);

/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 (Node/Supabase rebuild) ? backend/src/db.js
 * Single Supabase client, service_role key. RLS stays off; this backend
 * IS the access-control layer (L-04 enforced here, not in Postgres RLS).

require('dotenv').config();
const { createClient } = require('@supabase/supabase-js');

const supabase = createClient(
  process.env.SUPABASE_URL,
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY
);

module.exports = supabase;
Step 4 ? backend/src/modules/auth/auth.service.js (the actual L-01/L-02/L-03 logic):
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? auth module ? auth.service.js
 * L-01: mobile-only login. L-02: 5 fails -> 15 min lock. L-03: 30 min
 * inactivity / 12 hr absolute session timeout, new login kills old token.
 */
const crypto = require('crypto');
const supabase = require('../../db');

const LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD = 5;
const LOCKOUT_MINUTES = 15;
const INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MINUTES = 30;
const ABSOLUTE_TIMEOUT_HOURS = 12;

function hashPassword(password, salt) {
  return crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex');
}

function generateSalt() {
  return crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
}

function generateSessionToken() {
  return crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
}

async function logAudit(actorUserId, actionType, details) {
  await supabase.from('system_audit_trail').insert({
    actor_user_id: actorUserId,
    action_type: actionType,
    target_module: 'AUTH',
    target_table: 'users',
    record_key: actorUserId,
    change_details: details || null,
  });
}

async function login(mobileNumber, password) {
  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('*')
    .eq('mobile_number', mobileNumber)
    .single();

  // L-01: don't reveal whether the mobile number exists at all
  if (error || !user) {
    return { ok: false, status: 401, message: 'Invalid mobile number or password.' };
  }

  // L-02: lockout is self-clearing once locked_until has passed
  const now = new Date();
  if (user.account_status === 'LOCKED' && user.locked_until) {
    if (new Date(user.locked_until) > now) {
      return { ok: false, status: 423, message: 'Account locked. Try again later.' };
    }
    // lock window expired ? self-clear before proceeding
    await supabase
      .from('users')
      .update({ account_status: 'ACTIVE', failed_login_count: 0, locked_until: null })
      .eq('id', user.id);
    user.account_status = 'ACTIVE';
    user.failed_login_count = 0;
  }

  if (user.account_status === 'DISABLED') {
    return { ok: false, status: 403, message: 'Account disabled. Contact an Admin.' };
  }

  const computedHash = hashPassword(password, user.password_salt);
  const validPassword = crypto.timingSafeEqual(
    Buffer.from(computedHash, 'hex'),
    Buffer.from(user.password_hash, 'hex')
  );

  if (!validPassword) {
    const failedCount = user.failed_login_count + 1;
    const update = { failed_login_count: failedCount };
    if (failedCount >= LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD) {
      update.account_status = 'LOCKED';
      update.locked_until = new Date(now.getTime() + LOCKOUT_MINUTES * 60000).toISOString();
    }
    await supabase.from('users').update(update).eq('id', user.id);
    await logAudit(user.id, failedCount >= LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD ? 'ACCOUNT_LOCKED' : 'LOGIN_FAILURE');
    if (failedCount >= LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD) {
      return { ok: false, status: 423, message: `Account locked for ${LOCKOUT_MINUTES} minutes after too many failed attempts.` };
    }
    return { ok: false, status: 401, message: 'Invalid mobile number or password.' };
  }

  // L-03: a new login invalidates any prior session token for this user
  const sessionToken = generateSessionToken();
  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({
      failed_login_count: 0,
      active_session_token: sessionToken,
      session_issued_at: now.toISOString(),
      last_activity_timestamp: now.toISOString(),
      last_login_timestamp: now.toISOString(),
    })
    .eq('id', user.id);

  await logAudit(user.id, 'LOGIN_SUCCESS');

  return {
    ok: true,
    sessionToken,
    user: {
      id: user.id,
      full_name: user.full_name,
      role_name: user.role_name,
      flat_id: user.flat_id,
      force_password_reset: user.force_password_reset,
    },
  };
}

async function verifySessionToken(token) {
  if (!token) return null;

  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('*')
    .eq('active_session_token', token)
    .single();

  if (error || !user) return null;

  const now = new Date();
  const issuedAt = new Date(user.session_issued_at);
  const lastActivity = new Date(user.last_activity_timestamp);

  const hoursSinceIssued = (now - issuedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
  const minutesSinceActivity = (now - lastActivity) / (1000 * 60);

  if (hoursSinceIssued > ABSOLUTE_TIMEOUT_HOURS || minutesSinceActivity > INACTIVITY_TIMEOUT_MINUTES) {
    // L-03: expired session ? clear it so it can't be reused
    await supabase
      .from('users')
      .update({ active_session_token: null, session_issued_at: null })
      .eq('id', user.id);
    return null;
  }

  // sliding inactivity window: touch last_activity_timestamp
  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({ last_activity_timestamp: now.toISOString() })
    .eq('id', user.id);

  return {
    id: user.id,
    full_name: user.full_name,
    role_name: user.role_name,
    flat_id: user.flat_id,
    force_password_reset: user.force_password_reset,
  };
}

async function logout(userId) {
  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({ active_session_token: null, session_issued_at: null })
    .eq('id', userId);
  await logAudit(userId, 'LOGOUT');
}

module.exports = { login, verifySessionToken, logout, hashPassword, generateSalt };
Step 5 ? backend/src/modules/auth/auth.middleware.js:
const { verifySessionToken } = require('./auth.service');

async function requireAuth(req, res, next) {
  const authHeader = req.headers.authorization || '';
  const token = authHeader.startsWith('Bearer ') ? authHeader.slice(7) : null;

  const user = await verifySessionToken(token);
  if (!user) {
    return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Session expired or invalid. Please log in again.' });
  }
  req.user = user;
  next();
}

module.exports = { requireAuth };
Step 6 ? backend/src/modules/auth/auth.routes.js:
const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const { login, logout } = require('./auth.service');
const { requireAuth } = require('./auth.middleware');

router.post('/login', async (req, res) => {
  const { mobile_number, password } = req.body;
  if (!mobile_number || !password) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'mobile_number and password are required.' });
  }
  const result = await login(mobile_number, password);
  if (!result.ok) {
    return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  }
  res.json({ session_token: result.sessionToken, user: result.user });
});

router.post('/logout', requireAuth, async (req, res) => {
  await logout(req.user.id);
  res.json({ status: 'logged out' });
});

// proves session verification works end-to-end
router.get('/me', requireAuth, (req, res) => {
  res.json({ user: req.user });
});

module.exports = router;

Step 8 ? bootstrap the first Admin. Chicken-and-egg problem: you need a user to log in, but login needs a hashed password already in the DB. Create backend/src/scripts/seedAdmin.js:
require('dotenv').config();
const supabase = require('../db');
const { hashPassword, generateSalt } = require('../modules/auth/auth.service');

async function seed() {
  const mobile = process.argv[2];
  const password = process.argv[3];
  const fullName = process.argv[4] || 'Admin User';

  if (!mobile || !password) {
    console.log('Usage: node src/scripts/seedAdmin.js <mobile_number> <password> ["Full Name"]');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const salt = generateSalt();
  const hash = hashPassword(password, salt);

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .insert({
      full_name: fullName,
      mobile_number: mobile,
      role_name: 'ADMIN',
      password_hash: hash,
      password_salt: salt,
      account_status: 'ACTIVE',
    })
    .select();

  if (error) {
    console.error('Failed:', error.message);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  console.log('Admin created:', data[0].id, data[0].mobile_number);
}

That specific error ? permission denied at the Postgres level, not a JS/env problem ? almost always means one of two things. Let's check both in order, cheapest first.
Most likely cause: the wrong key is in .env. Supabase's API settings page shows two keys side by side ? anon public and service_role secret ? and it's an easy mix-up. The anon key doesn't have insert rights on tables like users unless RLS policies grant it; service_role bypasses that.
Check this first:
1. Go to Supabase dashboard ? Project Settings ? API.
2. Confirm the value in your .env under SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY matches the one labeled service_role secret ? not anon public. (The service_role key is longer/marked "secret" and usually has a warning next to it about never exposing it client-side ? that's the one we want here, since it never leaves your backend.)
If you had the anon key in there, swap it for the service_role key, save .env, and re-run:
node src/scripts/seedAdmin.js 9800000001 "TempPass123!" "Your Name"
If that's not it ? i.e. you confirm it already was the service_role key ? run this in Supabase's SQL Editor to check directly rather than guessing further:
select relname, relrowsecurity
from pg_class
where relname = 'users';

select grantee, privilege_type
from information_schema.role_table_grants
where table_name = 'users';
Paste both results back ? the first tells us if RLS somehow got enabled on users (it shouldn't be, our script never turned it on), and the second tells us exactly which Postgres roles currently have which privileges on that table, so I can give you the exact GRANT statement to fix it rather than a guess.


That output tells us exactly what's wrong: service_role was never granted SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE on any table in public ? it only has TRUNCATE/REFERENCES/TRIGGER, which are irrelevant here. Normally Supabase auto-grants this when you create tables through the dashboard, but running raw DDL directly in the SQL Editor can skip that step. This affects every table we created, not just users.
Run this in Supabase's SQL Editor ? grants for all existing tables, plus a default-privileges rule so any table you add later gets this automatically:
-- Fix existing tables
GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL FUNCTIONS IN SCHEMA public TO service_role;

-- Make sure any table/sequence/function created from now on gets the same grant automatically
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON TABLES TO service_role;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON SEQUENCES TO service_role;
ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON FUNCTIONS TO service_role;
Deliberately not granting anon/authenticated here. Since RLS is off across this schema and the access-control decision is "only the Express backend, via service_role, ever touches these tables directly" ? granting the anon key table access would mean anyone holding that key (which is safe to expose in a browser under Supabase's normal model, but not under ours, since we have no RLS policies) could read or write everything directly, bypassing every RBAC/lockout/audit rule we just built. Leaving anon/authenticated with no table grants is the correct, safer state for this architecture ? not an oversight.
Run that, then confirm with the same check as before:
select grantee, privilege_type
from information_schema.role_table_grants
where table_name = 'users' and grantee = 'service_role';
You should now see SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE, TRUNCATE, REFERENCES, TRIGGER for service_role. Once confirmed, re-run:
node src/scripts/seedAdmin.js 9800000001 "TempPass123!" "Your Name"
node src/scripts/seedAdmin.js 9800000002 "TempPass456!" "Second Admin Name"

That's actually a good sign, not a problem ? it means expired/logged-out tokens really do get rejected, which is exactly what L-03 requires. Good instinct to test the failure path deliberately rather than only the happy path.

Step 4 ? backend/src/services/email.service.js:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? email.service.js
 * L-36: OTP delivery is EMAIL only ? never SMS/WhatsApp API.
 */
require('dotenv').config();
const nodemailer = require('nodemailer');

const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
  service: 'gmail',
  auth: {
    user: process.env.EMAIL_USER,
    pass: process.env.EMAIL_APP_PASSWORD,
  },
});

async function sendOtpEmail(toEmail, otp, purpose) {
  await transporter.sendMail({
    from: `"${process.env.EMAIL_FROM_NAME}" <${process.env.EMAIL_USER}>`,
    to: toEmail,
    subject: 'Your Ranikuthi verification code',
    text: `Your one-time verification code is: ${otp}\n\nPurpose: ${purpose}\nThis code expires in 10 minutes. If you did not request this, contact an Admin immediately.`,
  });
}

module.exports = { sendOtpEmail };
Step 5 ? backend/src/modules/auth/otp.service.js:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 ? otp.service.js
 * L-36: OTP purposes ? FIRST_LOGIN / ADMIN_HARD_RESET / SELF_SERVICE_RESET.
 * Delivery is always EMAIL, matching the otp_log CHECK constraint.
 */
const crypto = require('crypto');
const supabase = require('../../db');
const { sendOtpEmail } = require('../../services/email.service');

const OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 10;

function generateOtp() {
  return String(crypto.randomInt(100000, 999999));
}

function hashOtp(otp) {
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(otp).digest('hex');
}

async function requestOtp(mobileNumber, purpose, requestedByUserId = null) {
  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('id, email_address')
    .eq('mobile_number', mobileNumber)
    .single();

  if (error || !user) {
    return { ok: false, status: 404, message: 'No user found for that mobile number.' };
  }
  if (!user.email_address) {
    return { ok: false, status: 400, message: 'No email on file for this user ? OTP cannot be delivered.' };
  }

  const otp = generateOtp();
  const otpHash = hashOtp(otp);
  const expiryTimestamp = new Date(Date.now() + OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60000).toISOString();

  const { data: otpRow, error: insertError } = await supabase
    .from('otp_log')
    .insert({
      user_id: user.id,
      purpose,
      delivery_channel: 'EMAIL',
      otp_hash: otpHash,
      expiry_timestamp: expiryTimestamp,
      status: 'PENDING',
      requested_by: requestedByUserId,
    })
    .select()
    .single();

  if (insertError) {
    return { ok: false, status: 500, message: insertError.message };
  }

  await sendOtpEmail(user.email_address, otp, purpose);

  return { ok: true, otpLogId: otpRow.id, userId: user.id };
}

async function verifyOtp(mobileNumber, otp, purpose) {
  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('id')
    .eq('mobile_number', mobileNumber)
    .single();

  if (error || !user) return { ok: false, status: 404, message: 'User not found.' };

  const { data: otpRow, error: otpError } = await supabase
    .from('otp_log')
    .select('*')
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .eq('purpose', purpose)
    .eq('status', 'PENDING')
    .order('requested_timestamp', { ascending: false })
    .limit(1)
    .single();

  if (otpError || !otpRow) return { ok: false, status: 400, message: 'No pending OTP found. Request a new one.' };

  if (new Date(otpRow.expiry_timestamp) < new Date()) {
    await supabase.from('otp_log').update({ status: 'EXPIRED' }).eq('id', otpRow.id);
    return { ok: false, status: 400, message: 'OTP expired. Request a new one.' };
  }

  if (hashOtp(otp) !== otpRow.otp_hash) {
    return { ok: false, status: 401, message: 'Incorrect OTP.' };
  }

  await supabase
    .from('otp_log')
    .update({ status: 'VERIFIED', verified_timestamp: new Date().toISOString() })
    .eq('id', otpRow.id);

  return { ok: true, otpLogId: otpRow.id, userId: user.id };
}

module.exports = { requestOtp, verifyOtp };
Step 6 ? add role-check middleware to backend/src/modules/auth/auth.middleware.js (append this to the existing file, and update its module.exports):
function requireRole(...allowedRoles) {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.user || !allowedRoles.includes(req.user.role_name)) {
      return res.status(403).json({ error: 'Insufficient role for this action.' });
    }
    next();
  };
}

module.exports = { requireAuth, requireRole };
Step 7 ? self-service reset routes. Append to backend/src/modules/auth/auth.routes.js (before module.exports):
const { requestOtp, verifyOtp } = require('./otp.service');
const { hashPassword, generateSalt } = require('./auth.service');
const supabase = require('../../db');

// Public ? for a user who forgot their password (not locked out, just forgot)
router.post('/otp/request', async (req, res) => {
  const { mobile_number } = req.body;
  if (!mobile_number) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'mobile_number is required.' });
  const result = await requestOtp(mobile_number, 'SELF_SERVICE_RESET');
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.json({ status: 'OTP sent to registered email.' });
});

router.post('/otp/reset', async (req, res) => {
  const { mobile_number, otp, new_password } = req.body;
  if (!mobile_number || !otp || !new_password) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'mobile_number, otp, and new_password are required.' });
  }
  const verify = await verifyOtp(mobile_number, otp, 'SELF_SERVICE_RESET');
  if (!verify.ok) return res.status(verify.status).json({ error: verify.message });

  const salt = generateSalt();
  const hash = hashPassword(new_password, salt);
  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({
      password_hash: hash,
      password_salt: salt,
      force_password_reset: false,
      account_status: 'ACTIVE',
      failed_login_count: 0,
      locked_until: null,
      active_session_token: null, // force a fresh login after reset
    })
    .eq('id', verify.userId);

  res.json({ status: 'Password reset. Please log in with your new password.' });
});
Step 8 ? admin-initiated reset routes (L-27 + L-36 ? same mechanism, distinct authority recorded in the audit record itself, not in separate code paths). Append to the same file:
router.post('/admin/reset/initiate', requireAuth, requireRole('ADMIN'), async (req, res) => {
  const { target_mobile_number } = req.body;
  if (!target_mobile_number) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'target_mobile_number is required.' });
  const result = await requestOtp(target_mobile_number, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET', req.user.id);
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.json({ status: 'OTP sent to target user\'s registered email.', otp_log_id: result.otpLogId });
});

router.post('/admin/reset/confirm', requireAuth, requireRole('ADMIN'), async (req, res) => {
  const { target_mobile_number, otp, new_password } = req.body;
  if (!target_mobile_number || !otp) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: 'target_mobile_number and otp are required.' });
  }
  const verify = await verifyOtp(target_mobile_number, otp, 'ADMIN_HARD_RESET');
  if (!verify.ok) return res.status(verify.status).json({ error: verify.message });

  // If the admin doesn't supply a password, generate a temporary one and return it once
  const tempPassword = new_password || crypto.randomBytes(6).toString('hex');
  const salt = generateSalt();
  const hash = hashPassword(tempPassword, salt);

  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({
      password_hash: hash,
      password_salt: salt,
      force_password_reset: true, // L-36: admin-issued reset always forces a change
      account_status: 'ACTIVE',
      failed_login_count: 0,
      locked_until: null,
      active_session_token: null,
    })
    .eq('id', verify.userId);

  await supabase.from('admin_reset_utility').insert({
    actor_user_id: req.user.id,
    actor_role: req.user.role_name,
    target_user_id: verify.userId,
    force_password_reset_set: true,
    otp_reference: verify.otpLogId,
    status: 'COMPLETED',
  });

  await supabase.from('system_audit_trail').insert({
    actor_user_id: req.user.id,
    actor_role: req.user.role_name,
    action_type: 'ADMIN_RESET_PASSWORD',
    target_module: 'AUTH',
    target_table: 'users',
    record_key: verify.userId,
  });

  res.json({
    status: 'Password reset.',
    temp_password: new_password ? undefined : tempPassword, // only shown when the admin didn't set one
  });
});

module.exports = router;
Add const crypto = require('crypto'); near the top of auth.routes.js (it's used by admin/reset/confirm above).
Step 9 ? break-glass recovery (L-28) ? deliberately CLI-only, never an HTTP route. This is the direct equivalent of "run only from the Apps Script editor by the Workspace owner": here, it's a script that can only be run by whoever has direct terminal access to the server ? never reachable from the app or the internet.
backend/src/scripts/breakGlassRecovery.js:
require('dotenv').config();
const supabase = require('../db');
const { hashPassword, generateSalt } = require('../modules/auth/auth.service');

async function run() {
  const mobile = process.argv[2];
  const newPassword = process.argv[3];
  const justification = process.argv.slice(4).join(' ');

  if (!mobile || !newPassword || !justification) {
    console.log('Usage: node src/scripts/breakGlassRecovery.js <admin_mobile_number> <new_password> <justification note...>');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const { data: user, error } = await supabase
    .from('users')
    .select('id, role_name')
    .eq('mobile_number', mobile)
    .single();

  if (error || !user) {
    console.error('No user found for that mobile number.');
    process.exit(1);
  }
  if (user.role_name !== 'ADMIN') {
    console.error('Break-glass recovery is for Admin accounts only (L-28).');
    process.exit(1);
  }

  const salt = generateSalt();
  const hash = hashPassword(newPassword, salt);

  await supabase
    .from('users')
    .update({
      password_hash: hash,
      password_salt: salt,
      account_status: 'ACTIVE',
      failed_login_count: 0,
      locked_until: null,
      force_password_reset: true,
      active_session_token: null,
    })
    .eq('id', user.id);

  await supabase.from('system_audit_trail').insert({
    actor_user_id: null, // no authenticated actor ? this ran outside the app entirely
    action_type: 'BREAK_GLASS_RECOVERY',
    target_module: 'AUTH',
    target_table: 'users',
    record_key: user.id,
    justification_note: justification,
  });

  console.log('Break-glass recovery complete for', mobile, '? force_password_reset set TRUE.');
}

run();
Note the justification_note isn't optional here ? the DB's break_glass_needs_justification CHECK constraint will reject the audit insert if you leave it blank, which is the right failure mode.

One small gap worth naming now rather than letting it surface as a surprise later: there's no endpoint yet for a user to actually act on force_password_reset: true (i.e., a forced "you must change your password before doing anything else" flow) ? right now that flag is set correctly but nothing in the API currently blocks other actions when it's true. That's fine to defer, since every other module we build will sit behind requireAuth anyway and we can add that one check once, centrally, in the middleware rather than per-route. Step 1 ? new file backend/src/modules/members/members.service.js:
/**
 * RANIKUTHI V5 (Node/Supabase) ? members module ? members.service.js
 * L-38 groundwork: flats CRUD. Admin/Committee manage; Residents read own flat only.
 */
const supabase = require('../../db');

async function createFlat(actorUserId, actorRole, flatData) {
  const { flat_code, wing, flat_no, area_sqft, floor, flat_type } = flatData;

  const { data: existing } = await supabase
    .from('flats')
    .select('id')
    .eq('flat_code', flat_code)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (existing) {
    return { ok: false, status: 409, message: `Flat ${flat_code} already exists.` };
  }

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('flats')
    .insert({
      flat_code, wing, flat_no,
      area_sqft: area_sqft || null,
      floor: floor || null,
      flat_type: flat_type || null,
      created_by: actorUserId,
    })
    .select()
    .single();

  if (error) return { ok: false, status: 500, message: error.message };

  await supabase.from('system_audit_trail').insert({
    actor_user_id: actorUserId,
    actor_role: actorRole,
    action_type: 'FLAT_CREATED',
    target_module: 'MEMBERS',
    target_table: 'flats',
    record_key: data.id,
    change_details: { flat_code },
  });

  return { ok: true, flat: data };
}

async function listFlats() {
  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('flats').select('*').order('flat_code');
  if (error) return { ok: false, status: 500, message: error.message };
  return { ok: true, flats: data };
}

/** Resident-safe: only ever returns the caller's OWN flat, ignoring any flat_id argument ? same pattern as the Sheets version's viewOwnBalance(). */
async function getOwnFlat(userFlatId) {
  if (!userFlatId) return { ok: false, status: 400, message: 'This account is not linked to a flat.' };

  const { data, error } = await supabase.from('flats').select('*').eq('id', userFlatId).single();
  if (error || !data) return { ok: false, status: 404, message: 'Flat not found.' };
  return { ok: true, flat: data };
}

async function updateFlat(actorUserId, actorRole, flatId, updates) {
  const allowedFields = ['wing', 'flat_no', 'area_sqft', 'floor', 'flat_type', 'rate_mode_override', 'rate_value_override_paise', 'active_flag'];
  const sanitized = {};
  for (const key of allowedFields) {
    if (updates[key] !== undefined) sanitized[key] = updates[key];
  }
  sanitized.updated_timestamp = new Date().toISOString();

  const { data, error } = await supabase
    .from('flats')
    .update(sanitized)
    .eq('id', flatId)
    .select()
    .single();

  if (error || !data) return { ok: false, status: 404, message: 'Flat not found or update failed.' };

  await supabase.from('system_audit_trail').insert({
    actor_user_id: actorUserId,
    actor_role: actorRole,
    action_type: 'FLAT_UPDATED',
    target_module: 'MEMBERS',
    target_table: 'flats',
    record_key: flatId,
    change_details: sanitized,
  });

  return { ok: true, flat: data };
}

module.exports = { createFlat, listFlats, getOwnFlat, updateFlat };
Step 2 ? new file backend/src/modules/members/members.routes.js:
const express = require('express');
const router = express.Router();
const { requireAuth, requireRole } = require('../auth/auth.middleware');
const { createFlat, listFlats, getOwnFlat, updateFlat } = require('./members.service');

router.post('/flats', requireAuth, requireRole('ADMIN', 'COMMITTEE'), async (req, res) => {
  const result = await createFlat(req.user.id, req.user.role_name, req.body);
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.status(201).json({ flat: result.flat });
});

router.get('/flats', requireAuth, requireRole('ADMIN', 'COMMITTEE', 'CA'), async (req, res) => {
  const result = await listFlats();
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.json({ flats: result.flats });
});

// Resident-safe: no flatId param taken from the client at all ? always the caller's own.
router.get('/flats/me', requireAuth, requireRole('RESIDENT'), async (req, res) => {
  const result = await getOwnFlat(req.user.flat_id);
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.json({ flat: result.flat });
});

router.patch('/flats/:id', requireAuth, requireRole('ADMIN', 'COMMITTEE'), async (req, res) => {
  const result = await updateFlat(req.user.id, req.user.role_name, req.params.id, req.body);
  if (!result.ok) return res.status(result.status).json({ error: result.message });
  res.json({ flat: result.flat });
});

module.exports = router;
